# Co-op level 3: examine, words and pickups by both players at once (design round 1, 2026-11-10)

Written from a reading pass (four readers on the game's machine code and the host) and one design, then two adversarial
reviews, all on 2026-11-10 evening. **Round 1 is refuted**: the design's mechanism (one per-player block swapped at the turn,
the game's holds run natively) breaks what works today, for reasons the reviewers read in the code. The refutations are
the specification for round 2. Nothing of this is built. The gate list for a close-up and a find beside a scene (the
afternoon's reading) is in `docs/coop-todo.md` and the scratchpad's closeup-gates.txt of that session.

## What the readers established (summary; the full reports are below)

- The close-up stepper (0x80024D78..0x800255A4) keeps no statics of its own: its state is the look record 0x80307108 (68
  bytes), the camera set (record 10, the stack), the holds' words and the window. It is not stepped per frame: it is
  reached only from the prompt press (0x80006EBC), the words' close (0x80024220) and the find's close (0x80024434).
- The words window (0x80024310) lives at r13-25960 with its script at -25944 and closed-with at -25940, its text in a
  shared pool of ten elements (0x805F87D0, drawn every frame by 0x801DA574, each placed by its own +8 x: the split already
  shows an element in one half by x >= 4096), its input channel in whichever input copy is in, the focus at r13-23556.
- The find (0x80024528) gives the thing to the player, sends events 71/46/47/190, registers its own channel (0x80024434)
  and places the thing through 0x801FA400 -> 0x80119B94 into a per-frame draw list counted at r13-23956.
- The two Tomes are a full RAM copy with a mode loop (tome_copy_step), a hook-off list and a send-back list; the lighter
  pattern is a block swapped around a call (CoopPlayerState, coop_states_swap, coop_zones_swap, coop_cameras_lend).
- The split shows HUD elements per half by x and 3D draws per half by a matrix mark (coop_found_mark); the HUD's uniform
  block is shared across all text, so per-element x is per block, not per element.

## The round-1 design (refuted; kept as the record)

THESIS (answers the user's point). Nothing here needs a new engine mechanism. The host already owns the three things required: (a) a per-player state block swapped in and out around game calls (CoopPlayerState, hle.c:11798-11805, coop_state 12077; the 2060-byte r13-24040 block COOP_WORDS/COOP_WORDS_SIZE 11846-11847 swapped at 14802-14814, 18408-18409, 21899-21900; PROMPT_SLOTS/PROMPT_LOOKS and the camera set in coop_cameras_lend 14247-14300; SCRIPT_STATES in coop_states_swap 14342; zones in coop_zones_swap 14382), (b) a per-player turn in which the game's own functions run as player 2 (coop_player_turn 18268-18520: COOP_PAD_UPDATE 0x8013E518 and COOP_CONTROLLER 0x80045BC4 guest-called with player 2's pad and identity), and (c) a renderer that already shows a 2D element in one half by its x (kHudAway 4096: interp.cpp build_view 682-690; used by coop_prompt_show COOP_PROMPT_AT and the bars COOP_BAR_AWAY) and a 3D object in one half by a matrix mark (coop_found_mark 0xD2/0xA0+view/0xE5, hle.c:3270-3277; interp.cpp marked_out_of 565-572). What is wrong today is not a missing mechanism but a missing rule: ownership of the words/close-up/find is decided by heuristics (g_coop_screen, g_coop_assist_at, coop_their_close_up 16944-16952 testing g_coop_use/r13-21492/coop_close_up_p2s, coop_play_hold's lr-in-stepper test 12850, hle_coop_found's finder guess), each of which assumes one close-up at a time and routes player 2's presses through the game's single pad ("its DETAIL and words come by the game's own pad, as player 1's", 16946). The design replaces all of that with one rule: THE OWNER IS WHOSE BLOCK IS IN (g_coop_turn, already the flag for "inside player 2's turn", used at 16999/17003). Everything the game does with the words, the stepper and the find then happens twice a frame, once per block, in real memory, with no copy of RAM and no "send back".

(1) COPY MECHANISM, PER OBJECT, WITH REASON
- All four objects (close-up stepper, words window + text, find, shown prompt): PATTERN B, the block swap, extended to one union block ("scene block") swapped at exactly one place, coop_player_turn's in/out, with the few out-of-turn "as player 2" paths (coop_use_start 15931 / continue_coop_use 15977, tome_copy_left 23585, coop_prompt_look 14796) changed to call the same block function instead of their partial lists. Reason, read from the dossier: every one of these objects exists to have WORLD side effects while open (0x80024528 gives the thing to the player via 0x801F95A0 and sends events 71/46/47/190 through 0x801F57C0/0x801F59B0; the close runs 0x801642D4(script,0,id); the stepper pushes the shared camera stack; the holds write channel masks other systems read), and both game frames already step the words (0x80024AF0 at 0x800084FC and 0x80111484). Pattern A (the Tome's RAM copy, tome_copy_step 13527-13700) fits an object whose way out never needs to run and whose outputs are few and replayable at close (A10); here the outputs ARE the object, so a copy would need the growing send-back list docs/coop-two-tomes.md warns against, plus 40 MB per open, a hand-kept hook-off list (23746-23770) and no sound. The per-player state here is small and already enumerated: under 8 KB (list in (2)).
- Not even a hybrid is needed for drawing: the Tome copy's skip_views/only_view renderer path is for a frame that belongs wholly to one player; a words window and a close-up are a few elements plus a camera, which the split already handles per element (x >= 4096 -> player 2's half) and per camera (view 1 built from hle_coop_player2_camera, mode 2, camera.c:1700-1803). The one renderer addition is in (4).
- The one object kept SHARED on purpose: the element pool 0x805F87D0 (10 x 0x660) and the channel table slots inside COOP_INPUT (0x805880A0+176, 20 x 32). The pool is shared because 0x801DA574 draws all ten every frame and the renderer places each by its own +8 x; a per-player pool would force per-draw marking of text. The channel table is already per player because CoopPlayerState.input[816] holds the whole 0x805880A0 block, so player 2's words channel exists only while their input copy is in (the 0x8013DB3C make at 0x80024310 lands in whichever copy is in; 0x8013E518, guest-called in the turn at 18373, frees closed channels of that copy).

(2) EXACT MEMORY PER PLAYER (the scene block; [read] from the dossier's writers/readers, [inf] where marked)
a. Already per player (CoopPlayerState 1916 B: input 816 @0x805880A0 incl. channels and the pad-held word +48; control 0x300 @0x805D7900 = the channel data blocks whose +16 masks 0x80199EA0 writes, so the hold's physical effect is ALREADY per player; aim 208; globals r13-25680..-25632; pad_globals r13-23604..-23548 incl. focus -23556 at +48 and its copy -23552 at +52; interact 5 words).
b. The 2060-byte r13 block COOP_WORDS r13-24040..r13-21981 [read 11846-11847]. NOTE [read, arithmetic]: this span covers busy -23344, running script -23228, channel pointers -23060/-23056/-23052, zones word -23040, -23564 input-taken, -23592 input lock, -23556/-23552 focus (again), prompt channel -22296 and prompt element -22236, PROMPT_SCREEN -24036, -24032/-23196/-23192 interact words. So swapping it (as coop_character_step 21899 and coop_player_turn 18408 already do) already carries the busy word and the zones word per player. Camera depth -21700 and -26864 are OUTSIDE it (carried by coop_camera_set_io).
c. New words set, 32 B contiguous: r13-25960 window, -25956, -25952 hide state, -25948 find countdown, -25944 words script, -25940 closed-with, -25936, -25932 find byte (r13-25960..-25929). Plus r13-25696 play-hold word (4 B), r13-25688 show flag (4 B), r13-24048/-24044 prompt flags (8 B, just below the 2060 block).
d. Bindings tables, whole: 0x8023E458 words 96 B (+12/+44/+76 element per open; +20/+24/+28 of each row written per frame by 0x8013E160 at 0x8013E288/2C8/2D8), 0x8023E4B8 find 64 B (+12 record, +44 element, same per-frame counters), 0x8023E510 "thing shown" 96 B [inf size: 3 rows] if ed52's window is ever player 2's.
e. Find record 0x8030A6E8 28 B (+0 element, +4 element2, +8 thing id, +16 data, +20 flags, +24 script) and the thing-shown record 0x8030A704 28 B.
f. Close-up stepper: look record 0x80307108 68 B, pending look 0x803090C4 32 B, prompt slots 0x80335A38 196 B (7 x 28; slot 6 = the shown record 0x80335AE0 that 0x80117360(1) clears) -- all three already swapped by coop_cameras_lend/coop_prompt_look.
g. Camera: the set 0x80602B18..0x8060394C (0xE34: all records incl. TB[10] 0x806036C8 / TA[10] 0x80603068, shown records 0x806037D8/0x80603860, stack 0x806038E8 5 x 20) + depth r13-21700 + index r13-26864 (coop_camera_set_io 14152) + live view COOP_CAMERA 0x803012E0 52 B (already swapped in coop_player_turn).
h. Scripts: SCRIPT_STATES 0x805CAA40 80 B (coop_states_swap) and each zone's inside word +104 (coop_zones_swap). Player identity words (coop_be_player2 12147: r13-26524, -21492, -21496, -21488, -21484 + casting words).
i. Host-side per player (not memory): g_coop_found_objects[2] and g_coop_unseen[12] become [2][...] indexed by view; the element owner table tw_element_laid's owner[10] gains a player axis (owner = {mode, player}).
Total new copying per turn: ~32+8+8+96+64+28 B on top of what coop_player_turn already moves (1916 + 2060 + 52 + 196 + 68 + 0xE34 + 80) -- under 8 KB each way.

(3) CALL SITES THAT REACH EACH OBJECT, AND WHERE THE SWAP GOES
Principle: no per-call swap anywhere. One swap pair in coop_player_turn (18268: block in after coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], load); block out before coop_state(&g_coop_state[0], load)). Every game function below runs either in the main frame (player 1's block is simply the game's memory) or inside the turn (player 2's block in). New guest calls inside the turn make player 2's half of the per-frame work happen:
- Stepper 0x80024D78: reached only by bl from 0x80006EBC (prompt press, line 2838), 0x80024220 (words close, 32710), 0x80024434 (find close, 32829) [read]. Player 1: all three run in the main frame via 0x801DA574's dispatch of the prompt channel r13-22296 (0x80008528) and 0x80024AF0 (0x800084FC). Player 2: (i) the press: in coop_player_turn after COOP_CONTROLLER, when player 2's pad has a new A/B/stick press and their shown record (0x80335AE0 in their swapped-in slots) has a prompt, guest-call COOP_PROMPT_USE 0x80006EBC(button, 0) -- replaces the host's current routing of their press to the game pad (g_coop_screen path) and the g_coop_assist_at guess in coop_prompt_pressed 16994; (ii) the ticks: guest-call 0x80024AF0() in the turn (it reads r13-25960 of the block in and runs 0x8013E160 on player 2's channel in their input copy, which runs 0x80024220 / 0x80024434 / 0x800243BC and from them the stepper). No hook on 0x80024D78 itself beyond the existing trace notify (24687).
- Stepper callees that are hooked today and change meaning: COOP_PLAY_HOLD 0x80199EA0 (coop_play_hold 12832-12900): keep only the scene rule ("let-go while the other's scene holds them"); drop the lr-in-stepper + g_coop_p2 test as owner test -- the channels it masks are in the block in. COOP_SHOW_MAIN 0x80043C88 (16055-16090): owner = g_coop_turn, not `theirs` (16104-16106). coop_words_hold 17017 (WORDS_HOLD 0x80042A8C by lr OPENS 0x8002436C / CLOSES 0x800242C4) and coop_find_hold 17082 (0x80042BB8): let the hold run natively (its busy bit, -24048 bit, -25696/-25932 word, zones word, channel masks and camera clear/0x801EEB58(2) all land in the block in); keep the hook as a trace. 0x80117404 ask / 0x80117360 clear: untouched, slots in the block. Camera 0x801ECD68/0x801ECCEC/0x801EEB58/0x801EB974/0x801EE8E0/0x801EEA9C: untouched, set in the block.
- Hide-others 0x800255A4 / 0x8002576C / 0x8002580C (main loop 0x8000821C) [read], and -25952: these mark room nodes (object flag 0x04000000) and SWAP CHARACTER STEPS (0x8002790C <-> 0x8002825C) or hide "the player" (0x80043D70) -- a world effect that would freeze the other player. New intercept at 0x800255A4 (both players): run the game's loop for its answer (or re-implement its test calls 0x801F0638/0x801F077C) but instead of 0x800338BC-marking nodes, put each hit object into g_coop_unseen[view of the owner] and return 0; -25952 then stays 0; intercept 0x8002576C to return 0 and clear that view's unseen list. 0x8002580C then never does anything. This is the per-draw mark path coop_found_object_draw already uses for "whoever a scene/look hides" (16104-16127) -- generalised to two lists.
- Words window 0x80024310 (MESSAGE_OPEN, hle_message_open 16763, hook 24191-24197): keep as trace; owner of the element = g_coop_turn at the time 0x801DB374/0x801DB088 run (hle_words_layout 16679, WORDS_LAYOUT hook 24313): for owner player 2 add 4096 to +8 after every layout (the hook runs on every relayout, so it sticks through scroll/page changes; the box and B icon are in the same element, hle.c:13001). Words close 0x80024220 and 0x80024384 (cancel's close): run inside 0x8013E160 -> the tick of the right block; nothing new. The close's 0x801642D4(script,0,0) and the stepper's 0x801642D4 at the close: run in the turn with SCRIPT_STATES/zones/identity in (today's coop_use_start list, now the block). 0x80093448 and 0x801047D0 readers of r13-25960 [read]: 0x80093448 is a behaviour pausing while a window is up -- it runs in the main loop with player 1's block, so player 2's window never pauses it [inf: desired].
- Find 0x80024528 (ACQUIRE, hle_coop_found 15857, hook 24204): finder = g_coop_turn ? 2 : 1 (replaces g_coop_use/r5 guesses). g_coop_found_objects becomes [2][2] by view; coop_found_object_draw marks out of the OTHER view. The find's handler 0x80024434 and B 0x800243BC: via the tick. Events 46/47/71/190 and 0x801F9DE8: real world, right identity (block), nothing to send back. New: the thing's placement step 0x801FA400 (drawable +24, runs from the main loop's 0x8014F8A0 with the game camera = player 1's; it calls 0x80119B94(object, 0, 30000) [read]) must for player 2's thing run with player 2's live view: intercept 0x801FA400 (by r3 drawable == player 2's found drawable) to skip in the main loop, and guest-call it in the turn with COOP_CAMERA 0x803012E0 swapped in (already is). See (4) for the renderer side.
- Shown prompt: 0x80117628 (main loop 0x800084F8) shows the first used slot of the slots in memory = player 1's. Player 2: guest-call 0x80117628 in the turn with their slots in; its new element goes through COOP_GAME_PROMPT_MAKE 0x8011708C (coop_game_prompt_made 22933, hook 24290) which already moves a game-made prompt to COOP_PROMPT_AT when the owner is player 2 -> owner = g_coop_turn. coop_prompt_show (18068) stays for the host-made prompts.
- Cancel 0x80024CDC/0x80024B24: the host's own calls of "the close-up cancel" (room change, cutscene, state load) must be made once per block, each with that block in.
- Script timers 0x80163E00 (main loop after 0x80008494 [read, coop_flags_ask comment]): run on the table in memory = player 1's; guest-call it in the turn for player 2's table [inf: needed so a script player 2's close-up started keeps ticking; verify with `ed fn 0x80163E00` first]. coop_flags_ask 0x8011727C / coop_busy_ask 0x80155738: the main loop asks with player 1's words in -> player 1 is gated only by their own find/words; keep the hooks as traces.
- Camera step 0x801EB9A0 at 0x80008424: player 1's set. coop_camera_step 14447: remove the `if (g_coop_cameras.lent) return;` (14448-14449) and the lend itself; player 2's set is stepped every turn.

(4) DRAWING EACH HALF'S OWN WINDOW AND CLOSE-UP CAMERA
- Words/prompt elements: per-element by x, no double drawing. Player 1's elements stay at the game's x and are shown in view 0 only (view 1 has hideHud=true and only brings back offRight x>1 NDC, interp.cpp 682-697); player 2's elements get +8 += 4096 (hle_words_layout for words, coop_game_prompt_made / COOP_PROMPT_AT for prompts) and show in view 1 only. camera.c change: delete the g_coop_screen || hle_coop_cameras_lent() branch (1619-1663); always the normal-play branch (1700-1803) with view 0 hide_hud = hle_coop_player1_tome_up(), view 1 hide_hud = true. Both halves thus always own their HUD; "who owns the game's one HUD" stops existing.
- Close-up camera: player 1's is the game's camera (record 10 in the game's set, pushed at 0x80024E90/0x80025080). Player 2's is record 10 in THEIR set, pushed by the same stepper code in their turn, stepped by coop_camera_step in their turn, read by hle_coop_player2_camera (14550, mode mod_p2_camera 2) and turned by camera_split_update into view 1's rot/pos relative to the game camera. Nothing new in the renderer for the camera. Known cost (not new): the game culls to player 1's camera, so a close-up of something far from player 1 may show fewer objects in player 2's half (mode 2 limitation).
- The find's thing: the one aurora addition. 0x801FA400 places the thing in CAMERA space (0x80119B94(object,0,30000)); build_view re-projects world draws through the player's PhotoView, so an object placed relative to player 1's camera lands wrong in view 1. Add a fourth matrix mark (coop_found_mark writes e.g. m+15=0xD2, m+31=0xB0+view, m+47=0xE5 [inf]) meaning "player N's camera-fixed draw": build_view keeps it in view N with the identity PhotoView (zoom/aspect only) and collapses it in the other view. interp.cpp marked_out_of (565-572) + build_view 700-715 are the only renderer lines; then rebuild .deps/GXRuntime with VS2022 and regenerate patches/gxruntime/eternal-darkness.patch.
- Toasts (frontend_toast_player 4141) and the hide list stay as they are.

(5) THE BUSY WORD AND THE HOLD PER PLAYER
- What becomes per player by the block: r13-23344 busy (inside the 2060 block; bit 2 by 0x80199EA0, 8 by 0x80042A8C, 16 by 0x80042BB8 via 0x801556CC), r13-24048 prompt flags (8 B added), r13-25696 play hold, r13-25932 find byte, r13-23040 zones word (in the 2060 block), the channel masks (COOP_CONTROL in CoopPlayerState), the player object's flag bit 4 (a different object per player anyway). 0x801556CC's side call 0x801F5620(102, player, ...) goes to the player of the block in.
- What stays host-answered: (i) coop_play_hold's SCENE rule (12832-12900): a let-go from one player's stepper while the other's scene holds them -- scenes are a different, still-shared system (fn22 one scene flag, coop-todo 27); (ii) coop_busy / coop_busy_ask reading the game's word in the main loop = player 1's, which is right; (iii) the kTwWords per-MODE swap (tw_words_io 13031) for the world/Tome split stays, layered under the per-player swap (mode axis x player axis in the element owner table); (iv) coop_breath, threat words, COOP_CAMERA swap as today.
- What is deleted when (6) is complete: g_coop_screen, g_coop_assist_at, coop_cameras_lend/lent, coop_window_to_game (19320), coop_player1_turn's game/own swap (17318-17400), coop_their_close_up, coop_no_pause's player-2 tests, hle_coop_found's finder guesses, coop_prompt_pressed's "what opens next is theirs" rule.

(7) RISKS: see the risks list (ranked).

BEFORE CODING (CLAUDE.md): `python -I tools/ed/ed.py keep`, then `fn 0x80024AF0`, `fn 0x80163E00`, `fn 0x801FA400`, `fn 0x800255A4`, `system words`, `system prompts`, `system examine`, `verb examine`, `verb pick up`, `needs 0x8061EEB8`; read docs/coop-next-session.md, docs/coop-failed-attempts.md (85, 182), docs/coop-no-pause.md; `ed test` after every change; failed attempts logged with code.

### Steps proposed

- Step 0 (no behaviour change, playable = today's runs still pass): add coop_scene_block_io(cpu, player, save|load) covering (2)c-e + the existing PROMPT_SLOTS/PROMPT_LOOKS/pending/camera set/SCRIPT_STATES/zones; call it in coop_player_turn (hle.c:18268) right after coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], load) and before coop_state(&g_coop_state[0], load); make coop_use_start/continue_coop_use (15931/15977), tome_copy_left (23585) and coop_prompt_look (14796) use it instead of their partial lists. Add `coop_owner(cpu) = g_coop_turn ? 1 : 0` and use it in hle_coop_found (15857), COOP_SHOW_MAIN (16104), coop_words_hold/coop_find_hold/coop_play_hold owner tests. Test: `sh tools/ed/run.sh saves/slot7.state step0` with each player examining in turn; `ed timeline` must match a pre-change run; `ed test`.
- Step A (a second words window): hle_words_layout (16679) owner = {mode, coop_owner}; for player 2 add 4096 to element +8 after layout (keep TW_ELEMENT_AWAY logic per mode). In the turn, after COOP_CONTROLLER, guest-call 0x80024AF0() and, on a new A/B press with a shown prompt in their slots, COOP_PROMPT_USE 0x80006EBC(button, 0). Let 0x80042A8C run natively in the turn (coop_words_hold becomes a trace for the block-in owner). camera.c: remove the g_coop_screen/lent branch (1619-1663). Playable: player 2 reads a sign/plaque (kind-1 words, 0x80024DF4 path) while player 1 reads another; each closes with their own B; neither stands still for the other.
- Step B (the close-up stepper per player): block already carries looks/pending/slots/camera set; remove coop_cameras_lend and the `lent` skip in coop_camera_step (14448); intercept 0x800255A4 (both players) to fill g_coop_unseen[owner view] and return 0, intercept 0x8002576C to clear that view's list and return 0; g_coop_unseen -> [2][12], coop_found_object_draw marks out of the other view; guest-call 0x80117628 in the turn so player 2's DETAIL/RETURN/PICK UP prompt shows (via coop_game_prompt_made at COOP_PROMPT_AT). Playable: both players in an ed219 close-up (DETAIL -> words -> RETURN) at once on different objects, each half its own record-10 camera, each pad its own steps.
- Step C (the find): block carries 0x8023E4B8, 0x8030A6E8, -25948/-25932; hle_coop_found finder = coop_owner; g_coop_found_objects -> [2][2]; intercept 0x801FA400 for player 2's found drawable (skip in the main loop, guest-call in the turn with COOP_CAMERA in); renderer: fourth matrix mark 'camera-fixed for view N' in coop_found_mark (3270) + interp.cpp marked_out_of/build_view (565-572, 700-715), rebuild GXRuntime, regenerate the patch; guest-call 0x80163E00 in the turn [verify the function first]. Playable: both players PICK UP different items at once; each thing floats in its own half; each carry list correct; events 47/190 and the row's +36 script run for the right player.
- Step D (Tome copy interplay): the clean snapshot (tw_mode_change 13807-13819) and tome_copy_step must run only with player 1's block in (they already run from the main loop, assert it); tome_copy_no_window (13365) frees only the block in; tome_copy_hook_off (23746) gains the new intercepts (0x800255A4, 0x8002576C, 0x801FA400); hle_words_layout owner in the copy = the Tome mode owner, not the turn; tome_copy_p1_left's 'wait for player 2's close-up' test reads player 2's block (r13-25960 and look kind in the block, not the game's). Playable: player 1 opens the Tome while player 2 is in a close-up and vice versa; player 2's Tome item use (tome_copy_left) opens their words in their half while player 1 examines.
- Step E (save states, room changes, cutscenes): tw_state_loaded (13184) clears player 2's block (window/element pointers are stale after a load) after guest-calling 0x80024CDC/0x80024384 with that block in; the frontend's state save is refused while g_coop_turn (as frontend_frame already is in the copy turn, 5250-5254); on a room change (coop_zones_new 14352 site) and on a cutscene start (cutscene_playing), cancel both close-ups each with its block in. Playable: save/load mid-words for each player; door while the other reads; cutscene while both are in close-ups.
- Step F (cleanup): delete g_coop_screen, g_coop_assist_at, coop_cameras_lend/hle_coop_cameras_lent, coop_window_to_game, coop_player1_turn's game/own swap, coop_their_close_up, the player-2 tests in coop_no_pause, coop_prompt_pressed's ownership rule; update docs/game/systems/words.md, prompts.md, camera.md, docs/coop-next-session.md, docs/coop-todo.md (27-30), docs/items-log.md with what the runs showed.

### Risks ranked

- 1. Save states (highest): a state saved with player 2's block in, or inside a guest call of the turn, is a corrupted game; a state loaded while player 2's block holds channel/element/thing pointers from before the load points at freed slots (channel table entries freed by 0x8013E518, pool elements freed by 0x801DA600). Mitigation in step E; until then test saves only with both players idle.
- 2. Tome copy interplay: the copy is a snapshot of real memory with player 1's block in; player 2's element lives in the pool at x+4096 and is drawn by the copy's 0x801DA574 into the menu frame (fitted whole into a half; off-screen, but it is a shared pool so a copy's 0x801DB374 can take the slot player 2 wanted); tome_copy_no_window frees whatever window is in; every new intercept must be added to tome_copy_hook_off or it runs against the copy; player 1's copy over player 2's close-up defers scripts (tome_copy_p1_left) by reading the game's words, which are now player 1's -- it must read player 2's block.
- 3. The 2060-byte block is coarse: it also swaps -23228 running script, -23060/-23056/-23052 channel pointers, -22296/-22236 the prompt channel and element, -23564/-23592 input flags. Anything a game function stores there during the turn that the main loop expects (or vice versa) is lost at swap-out -- the hidden-list hazard of docs/coop-two-tomes.md (kTwSwitches grew word by word). Log every differing word at swap-out in ED_COOP_LOG for the first runs.
- 4. Hide-others (0x800255A4/0x8002580C): the game's own path swaps character step functions room-wide; if the intercept misses a caller (the script native at 0x80017ECC calls 0x8002576C; 0x800255A4 is also reached with run_now != 0 from elsewhere?) one player freezes the other or a character stays frozen after a close. Verify callers with `ed fn` before step B.
- 5. Script states per player: a script player 2's find starts (events 190 -> row +36 script, shelf's event 6 0x801F7748) ticks only while their table is in; if 0x80163E00 is not the timer step or has other side effects, scripts stall or run twice. Also a script of player 2's that asks a room change or a cutscene while player 1's close-up is open must cancel player 1's close-up (step E) or the stepper's camera pop runs on a dead room.
- 6. Cutscenes and room changes: !g_play turns the split off and the game's own cutscene path takes the one camera/hold; both blocks must be cancelled with 0x80024CDC each with its block in, in a defined order, before the room's zones/cameras are rebuilt (coop_zones_new) -- a close-up left open across a room change leaves record 10 and a stack depth in player 2's set.
- 7. Prompt press routing: today player 2's press reaches the game through the game pad (g_coop_screen); switching to a guest call of 0x80006EBC in the turn changes the kind-3 (stick) gate (both channels live, 0x80199F50/F74) and the 0x8013E154 'taken' flag semantics for that frame; a press may double-fire (host call + game dispatch) until the old path is removed in the same step.
- 8. Element pool exhaustion: 10 elements shared by both players' words, both prompts, player 1's icon, the find's second element and fading-out elements (freed only at alpha 0 by 0x801DA600); 0x801DB374 with no free element prints and the window has no text. Count elements in the logs; free fading ones early if it happens.
- 9. Renderer change for the find's thing (fourth matrix mark) needs a GXRuntime rebuild and patch regeneration; a wrong mark collapses world draws (translation low bytes are a real position's bits for ordinary draws -- the three-byte pattern must stay improbable). Player 2's close-up camera also inherits the mode-2 culling limitation (world culled to player 1's view).
- 10. Input focus: 0x8013E160 passes over every channel but the focus (0x8013E1A0); the focus pair is in pad_globals per player, fine, but the prompt channel r13-22296 is dispatched by 0x801DA574 once per frame with player 1's block in -- player 2's prompt presses must come only from the turn's guest call, never from the game's dispatch, or a press is answered twice.
- 11. Hook ordering and performance: the extra guest calls per turn (0x80024AF0, 0x80117628, 0x80163E00, 0x801FA400) and ~8 KB of copying are cheap, but a guest call that waits (a DVD read inside a script) stalls the frame; keep hle_guest_call's block limit in mind and log calls over 2 ms.
- 12. Sound: two words windows may both play a voice line (0x8019DB90 family); the mixer is shared -- acceptable, but a voice stopping when the other's window closes is possible.

### Test plan proposed

Kit-driven (every step), from the repo root with PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 python -I tools/ed/ed.py: `keep` first; `sh tools/ed/run.sh saves/slot7.state NAME` with all logs, both players recorded and pictures; then `ed timeline` and logs/run-*-NAME/EVENTS.log; freezes in saves/reports/hang-*.txt. Scripts place both players (the kit can place players, run scripts and press for player 2); each test is a scripted sequence plus a picture check of each half.
Step 0: re-run two existing scenarios (player 1 examines; player 2 examines via their pad) and diff `ed timeline` against a pre-change run: identical events, no new [coop] lines about ownership guesses.
Step A: place player 1 at one readable object and player 2 at another in the same room; press A for player 1 (words up), then A for player 2; expect: two elements in the pool, one at game x and one at x+4096 (log element +8 at layout), r13-25960 nonzero in both blocks, each half showing its own words; move player 1's stick while player 2 reads (player 1 moves: busy bit 8 only in player 2's block); B for player 2 closes theirs only (0x80024220 in the turn, 0x8013DB94 on their channel), then B for player 1. Also: player 2 presses B first; and both scroll long text (stick rows write +28 counters in their own tables).
Step B: both players at different ed219 objects; press A for both; expect each half a record-10 camera (log 0x801EEB58(10) in each set, depth 1 each), both prompts DETAIL in their own half (slot 2/4 in each block's slots), press DETAIL for each -> words -> RETURN, in interleaved order (p1 open, p2 open, p2 words, p1 words, p1 RETURN, p2 RETURN) and reversed; neither player's character freezes (no 0x8002825C step swap: assert -25952 == 0 in both blocks); the stick cancel (0x80024B24) for one while the other is in words.
Step C: two pickable items; PICK UP for both; expect each thing on its own half only (matrix marks), each carry list (0x80151500/0x80150D84 via `ed`) gains its own item, events 71/46/47/190 per thing with the right player id in EVENTS.log, the row's +36 script run once per item; player 1 keeps walking during player 2's find fade (51 ticks); a find whose +20 bit0 self-closes for player 2.
Step D: player 1 opens the Tome while player 2 is in words/close-up/find, closes it, both continue; player 2 Start while player 1 reads; a Tome item use by player 2 (cannot be scripted: the USER plays this) opening their words beside player 1's examine.
Step E: save state with player 2 in words and player 1 in a close-up, load it: both blocks cleared, no hang (hang-*.txt empty), both can examine again; a door by player 1 while player 2 reads; a scripted cutscene start while both are in close-ups.
User must play (no script can): Tome item uses and spell casts beside the other's close-up, controller feel (controls.cpp held-over logic is not exercised by scripted presses, failed-attempts 147-152), long sessions across several rooms for pool exhaustion and camera stack depth drift (log depth -21700 of both sets at every room change), and anything with voice. After each run add seen items to docs/items-log.md and any failed attempt with its code to docs/coop-failed-attempts.md; `ed test` after every change.

## Why round 1 is refuted (the two reviews, verbatim)

### Review 1: regressions on confirmed paths

- REFUTED (regression, confirmed path 'player 1 words with player 2 free'). Design (3)/(5) lets 0x80042A8C / 0x80042BB8 / 0x80199EA0 'run natively' and keeps coop_busy_ask/coop_flags_ask as traces, saying 'coop_busy reading the game's word in the main loop = player 1's, which is right' (5.ii). [read] Today player 1's words work with player 2 free ONLY because the hold is passed over: coop_words_hold hle.c:17044-17056 (g_coop_words_free = coop_no_pause, hold not run, busy bit 8 never set) and coop_find_hold 17096-17113 (busy 16 never set), plus coop_busy_ask 17218-17231 answering 0 at the game's own gate. [read asm 3120-3122] 0x8000749C: bl 0x80155738; cmplwi r3,0; beq -> the main loop skips the player controller (0x80045BC4, hook hle_coop_controller 17759, where coop_player_turn lives) whenever the busy word is nonzero; and coop_player_turn itself refuses while coop_busy (18306-18317, coop_busy 3882 = r13-23344 != 0). With player 1's words hold run natively, busy = 2|8 in the game's memory (= player 1's block between turns) -> no controller call, no turn -> player 2 frozen for the whole of player 1's words/find. docs/coop-failed-attempts.md:460 records exactly this failure ('with busy 1 left... the game never calls main's controller, so hle_coop_controller never ran and player 2 stood through player 1's words'). Nothing in steps 0-F changes the 0x8000749C gate or the 18306 refusal.
- REFUTED (regression, confirmed path 'player 1's close-up with player 2 walking'). Design (3) drops coop_play_hold's lr-in-stepper test 'as owner test'. [read] That test (hle.c:12862-12870, lr in 0x80024D78..0x800255A4 and r3==0) is what sets g_coop_p1_look / g_coop_p1_look_kind=1; coop_p1_close_up 16871-16873 returns false unless g_coop_p1_look, and coop_busy_ask 17226 answers 'not busy' through coop_p1_close_up. Without it, player 1's DETAIL->words->RETURN holds (busy 2 at 0x80024E8C, 0x800250D8) stop the controller at 0x8000749C and player 2 stands from player 1's B till DETAIL and from the words' end till RETURN: the exact bug of logs/run-20261105-202032-jtg-p1door described at 12832-12837.
- REFUTED (Step 0 'no behaviour change' is false; regression on 'pickups named for the finder'). coop_owner = g_coop_turn ? 1 : 0 is wrong for every path that opens player 2's words/close-up/find today. [read] g_coop_turn is true only inside coop_player_turn (set 18319, cleared 18517). Player 2's B on a prompt is NOT run there: 18419-18420 'Their B with a prompt: its script runs at player 1's controller (coop_use_start)'; coop_use_start 15931-15975 / continue_coop_use 15977 run with g_coop_use.p2, g_coop_turn false. Player 2's PICK UP press today goes through the game's pad dispatch in the main frame (g_coop_screen, comment 16946-16948), also outside the turn. hle_coop_found's finder today (15863-15864) = (g_coop_use.active && g_coop_use.p2) || r5 == p2's id || coop_close_up_p2s(); with coop_owner it becomes 1 for all of these -> coop_found_notice names player 1, coop_found_for_finder marks the thing out of the wrong view, and the found-word carry (15867-15890: other = use.p2 ? states.p1 : states.p2) writes to the wrong table (the 'monk's key given again' case, 15891-15896). Same for the scene-end find (15905-15915, !g_coop_use.active && script == g_coop_scene_script) and tome_copy_left's words. The owner rule only becomes true after Steps A/B move the press and the tick into the turn, so Step 0 cannot use it.
- REFUTED (ordering: Step A breaks the confirmed 'close-up of player 2 alone with player 1 walking'). Step A deletes camera.c:1619-1663 while the lend stays until Step B. [read] camera.c:1621-1663: during hle_coop_screen()||hle_coop_cameras_lent() the GAME camera is player 2's close-up (record 10 pushed into the lent set, coop_cameras_lend 14247-14252) and view 1 gets identity (1658), view 0 player 1's kept camera (hle_coop_player1_camera 14537). Without that branch the normal-play path (1700-1803) gives view 0 the game camera = player 2's close-up picture in PLAYER 1's half, and builds view 1 from hle_coop_player2_camera (14550, g_coop_own_view) which is NOT stepped while lent (coop_camera_step 14448-14449 returns), so player 2's half shows a stale view. Both halves wrong for every player-2 examine until Step B lands. The camera.c deletion must move to Step B (with the lend removal).
- REFUTED (false premise in (2)b/(3): 'the 2060-byte block ... as coop_player_turn 18408 already do'). [read] hle.c:18405-18411 and 18505-18506: the block is saved/restored only `if (scan)`, i.e. ED_COOP_P2_SCAN set (default off). And even then it is restore-DISCARD, not per-player carry: coop_words_back 11851-11856 puts back everything except the sound words r13-22920..-22396 (MusyX voice allocation, comment 11840-11845: putting those back hung the next two-part sound). coop_character_step 21899-21906 and coop_prompt_look 14817-14818 likewise save player 1's and put them back (coop_prompt_look with exceptions: busy, -23040, -23280 kept when player 2's scene starts, docs/coop-no-pause.md:80). A true per-player swap of all 2060 bytes (Step 0) must exclude -22920..-22396 or re-introduce the hung-voice bug, and it changes what player 2's scan/step writes survive (previously discarded).
- REFUTED (Step 0 regression: 'standing whatever the stick' returns). [read, arithmetic] r13-23604..-23548 (CoopPlayerState.pad_globals, focus -23556 at +48, hle.c:11803, 19318) lies entirely inside r13-24040..-21981: the scene block and CoopPlayerState carry the same focus words. coop_player_turn deliberately clears -23556/-23552 AFTER coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], load) (18357-18368) because player 2's copy kept a stale focus and their controller channel was passed over for good (Roberto's room 82, Michael's room 50, comment 18347-18353). Step 0 inserts the block load 'right after coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], load)', i.e. after that clear if placed per the design text -> the stale focus comes back every turn. Order-dependent double ownership of -23556/-23552/-23592 between two blocks.
- REFUTED (Steps 0-A: double ownership of the camera set and prompt slots between the lend and the block). [read] coop_cameras_lend 14247-14270 swaps game<->g_coop_cameras.p1/.p2 for the DURATION of player 2's close-up and sets g_coop_set_in=2; coop_camera_step 14448 and coop_cameras_in use g_coop_set_in to know what is in. Step 0's block (camera set 0xE34 + depth + index + PROMPT_SLOTS + PROMPT_LOOKS) swapped in/out of every turn while a lend is on loads g_coop_cameras.p2 (a copy taken before the close-up pushed record 10 and its stack entry) over the live lent set each frame: player 2's close-up camera snaps back to pre-open state every turn, and the block-out then saves the live set into .p2 so the lend's 'back' (continue_coop_use 16006, coop_camera_set_io) restores the wrong set. coop_prompt_look (14818, 14831-14839), called inside the turn at 18418, also saves/loads PROMPT_SLOTS and PROMPT_LOOKS itself; with the block also carrying them there are two copies of player 2's slots (g_coop_prompt_slots vs the block) and the one left in memory at block-out is the pre-scan copy (coop_prompt_look restores `slots` after saving to g_coop_prompt_slots), so a 0x80117628 guest call in the turn (Step B) would show no new prompt. The design says coop_prompt_look 'uses the block instead', but then coop_prompt_slot_clear/coop_talk_slot_clear and coop_cameras_lend's own slot swap (14289-14300) still act on g_coop_prompt_slots.
- REFUTED (Step A: player 2's words tick only in the turn, which is refused while player 1 is busy). Design (3) 'the ticks: guest-call 0x80024AF0() in the turn' and (5) busy per block. [read] coop_player_turn 18306 refuses the whole turn while the game's busy word (player 1's block) is nonzero; player 1's words/find natively held = busy 2|8 / 2|16. So while player 1 reads a plaque, player 2's window gets no 0x8013E160 step: no scroll, no B close (0x80024220 runs only from 0x8013E160's row dispatch, asm 32710), their words stay up and their body stays held (0x80199EA0(0) in their block) until player 1 closes. The design's Step A 'playable' claim ('each closes with their own B; neither stands still for the other') fails for the both-reading case it names. Also coop_player1_turn (17318-17400), which today lets player 1 walk under player 2's close-up by giving the game's input to player 2, is deleted only in Step F; between Steps 0 and B the game's input is player 2's (coop_window_to_game 19320) while the block also swaps the channel pointers -23060/-23056/-23052 inside the 2060 block.
- REFUTED (two scenes at once / scene rule inputs). [read] coop_play_hold's scene rule 12850 decides `theirs = coop_use_theirs() || g_coop_screen` for a let-go from the stepper; the refused branch 18315 steps player 2's scene camera only when !g_coop_screen; coop_p1_close_up 16880-16882 excludes bits 8/16 only when g_coop_words_theirs/g_coop_find_theirs (set by coop_words_hold/coop_find_hold's coop_their_close_up test, which Step 0 replaces). Replacing these with coop_owner=g_coop_turn before Step B moves the press means a player-2 RETURN let-go (still dispatched by the game's pad in the main frame) is read as player 1's while player 2's own scene is on -> other_scene = coop_p2_scene_on() true -> the let-go is swallowed ('hold kept') and player 2 stays held after their own RETURN inside their scene (room 183's confirmed scene+examine interplay, docs/coop-next-session.md 'room 183 played and confirmed').
- REFUTED (node hold by the busy word). [read] COOP_NODE_HELD_ASK 0x801F68D4 (hle.c:17236-17241): the game asks the busy word for each node's hold (+28's 8) from its update at 0x800074AC and the motion steps 0x80027200/0x800272A8/0x80027774/0x80027804, all in the main loop with the game's (= player 1's) block in. With player 1's words hold run natively (busy 2|8), player 2's node is asked against player 1's busy and held in its pose, independent of the turn: the 'player 1 stood in their pose through player 2's ladder' case of 17239-17241 mirrored. coop_node_held_ask answers 0 only for room changes (17244-17249).
- NOT REFUTED but noted (inferred): hle_coop_found's RUNNING_SCRIPT read (15866, r13-23228 inside the 2060 block) and SCRIPT_STATES carry (15867-15890) assume the game's table is player 1's and `other` is the saved copy; with the block in during the turn the real table IS player 2's and g_coop_states.p2 is a stale copy overwritten at block-out, so the found word carried to `other` is lost. Step C must change the carry's target to the block-out buffer, not g_coop_states.
- Design elements that DO match today's code [read]: coop_be_player2 12147 identity words; coop_states_swap 14342 / coop_zones_swap 14382 per call; element x+4096 shown only in view 1 (interp.cpp 682-690, COOP_PROMPT_AT, COOP_BAR_AWAY); coop_found_mark 3270 and marked_out_of; coop_game_prompt_made 22933 moving a game-made prompt; hle_words_layout as a relayout hook (24313). The thesis 'no new engine mechanism' holds; what fails is the claim that the owner rule and the busy word can be made per-block without replacing the three host answers (coop_busy_ask at 0x8000749C, coop_player_turn's refusal 18306, coop_node_held_ask) that today keep the OTHER player free, and the step ordering (camera.c in A, owner in 0, lend until B).
- Safe ordering, if the design is kept (inferred): Step 0 must (a) keep coop_their_close_up/g_coop_use.p2/coop_close_up_p2s as the owner test until the press and tick are in the turn, (b) exclude r13-22920..-22396 and the pad_globals/focus range from the block (or load the block BEFORE the focus clear at 18357), (c) not carry the camera set/slots while g_coop_cameras.lent. The camera.c:1619-1663 deletion and coop_camera_step's `lent` skip belong in Step B together with removing the lend. Native holds (Step A) need coop_busy_ask / the 18306 refusal / coop_node_held_ask to answer from the OTHER player's block (i.e. 'busy' = the asked player's block, not the game's), or player 2 freezes during player 1's words exactly as before coop_no_pause (docs/coop-no-pause.md, failed-attempts 460).

### Review 2: feasibility, missed call sites and wrong readings

- REFUTED (premise of (2)b, the 2060-byte block): no code loads a player-2 copy of r13-24040..-21981 today. [read] hle.c:18376-18384 coop_player_turn saves it into p1_prompt ONLY under ED_COOP_P2_SCAN (`static int scan`, default off) and puts it back with coop_words_back; hle.c:21899-21904 coop_character_step saves p1_prompt before player 2's step and coop_words_back after (protects player 1's words, never puts player 2's in); hle.c:14816-14817/14890 coop_prompt_look the same round COOP_SCAN. The comment at 18377-18378 records the attempt: 'The words round the record are only put back with the scan: they hold player 2's walking too, which putting them back undid' -- swapping the block round the turn stopped player 2 walking. hle.c:11840-11849: the block holds MusyX's voice allocation (COOP_SOUND_WORDS r13-22920..-22396), which coop_words_back skips because putting it back leaked voices until a two-part sound hung. So 'the scene block = the 2060 block, swapped at one place' is a curated sub-list from day one (the kTwSwitches hazard the design claims to avoid), and the 'already swapped at 18408/21899' claim is false.
- REFUTED (step C mechanism): 0x801FA400 does not place the thing in camera space. [read] asm 514057-514075: 0x8014F8A0(drawable)->node, 0x801F633C->object, 0x801E1128(), 0x80119B94(object, 0, 30000). [read] 0x80119B94 (asm 284142-284173): 0x80205FA4(object, 0x80603798, &v) then dist^2 = v.v; writes {object, r4, r5, dist^2} into the frame's draw list 0x805F0780 + 16*n and n++ where n = r13-23956 (0x80119BDC/0x80119C10). r13-23956 is INSIDE the 2060 block. The list is reset at 0x8011985C and consumed in the same main frame by 0x80119DB0..0x8011A200 (asm 284277-284553, the object-draw pass just before COOP_OBJECT_DRAW 0x8011A278). Consequence: guest-calling 0x801FA400 in the turn with player 2's block in increments player 2's COPY of the count; after swap-out the consumer reads player 1's count, so player 2's thing is never drawn (its entry is overwritten by player 1's next registration). The 'fourth matrix mark: camera-fixed for view N' is built on a wrong reading; where the found thing is actually placed before the camera (0x801F9DE8's state 30 / object +596 |= 0x10100 at 0x80024528?) is not read [inferred, unresolved].
- REFUTED (3)/(risk 10), the prompt press channel: the channel that reaches 0x80006EBC is r13-26224 (0x8061EDB0), not r13-22296. [read] asm 2410-2432: 0x80006988 makes it with 0x8013DB3C(11 rows, 0x8023B9B8, pad 0), handler 0x80006900, stores r13-26224. [read] asm 2480-2485: 0x80006A94 steps it (0x8013E160(r13-26224)) from the main frame at 0x800084F4, gated by 0x800EBEE8 which is `li r3,0; blr` (asm 237251) -- always stepped. The row's fn is called at 0x8013E330 (bctrl) with lr 0x8013E334 = COOP_PROMPT_DISPATCH (hle.c:17000). r13-26224 lies outside the 2060 block (-24040..-21981) AND outside CoopPlayerState (globals -25680..-25632, pad_globals -23604..-23548): one shared pointer into whichever input copy is in. 0x801DA574's r13-22296 (asm 481406) is another channel, made at 0x801DB850. Also [read] hle.c:16995-16997: the dispatch calls 0x80006EBC with r4=3 and no button every 60 frames while walking (kind-3 stick prompts); the design's turn guest call 0x80006EBC(button, 0) replicates only the button press. 0x80006EBC has no bl callers (asm grep) -- pointer-reached only.
- MISSED CALL SITE, fatal to step A: coop_player_turn overwrites player 2's channel masks and focus every turn. [read] hle.c:18384-18392: for every channel +16 (COOP_INPUT_MASKS 0xE0, stride 32) the GAME's (player 1's) mask is written into player 2's copy (0x0FFFFFFE -> 0x0FFFFFFF), comment: 'Player 2's own copy kept a lock for good... Theirs is the game's as it stands.' Under the design player 2's words hold (0x80199EA0(0): r13-23060/-23052 +16 := 0 in their copy) and their window channel's +16 wait are replaced by player 1's each turn: player 2 walks under their words, or player 1's hold holds player 2. [read] hle.c:18399-18417: the turn zeroes r13-23556/-23552 in player 2's copy every turn (unless ED_COOP_P2_FOCUS_KEPT): player 2's words channel can never keep the focus, so 0x8013E160 (asm 321377-321540) never passes over their controller channel -- the stick moves them while reading. Both loops must go, and the reason each was added (a lock kept for good, a focus kept for good: hle.c:18395-18399, Roberto's room 82, Michael's room 50) returns as a bug to solve.
- MISSED GATE, fatal to 'neither stands still for the other': the turn is refused while the game's busy word is nonzero. [read] hle.c:18311-18323: `if (coop_busy(cpu) && !coop_door_turns(cpu) && !room_other) { ... return; }`; coop_busy (hle.c:3882) reads r13-23344 of whatever is in = player 1's in the main loop. With busy per player (design (5)), player 1's words set bit 8 (0x80042A8C -> 0x801556CC(8,1), asm 63916+) and player 2's turn is skipped for the whole read: player 2 freezes exactly when the design says they walk. The design's (5)(ii) 'coop_busy reading player 1's is right' is wrong for this gate (today coop_words_hold passes the hold over so bit 8 is never in the game's word).
- DOUBLE COUNT, step C's 0x80163E00 guest call: [read] asm 360073-360105: 0x80163E00 walks SCRIPT_STATES 0x805CAA40 (lis -32675/addi -21952), 10 x 8 bytes: if +0 (s16 script) != 0 and +4 (u16 timer) != 0: timer--, at 0 -> 0x801642D4(script, 0, 0). It is the timer step, as the design guessed -- but the host already counts player 2's timers host-side in coop_script_timers at the same hook: [read] hle.c:17999-18012 decrements g_coop_states.p2[i] +4 and runs the script through coop_use_start(cpu, script, true) when it reaches 1; hle.c:17923-17927 comment. A guest call of 0x80163E00 in the turn with the p2 table in would tick every player-2 timer twice a frame unless that loop is removed in the same step; the design does not mention it (docs/coop-todo.md:83 lists the timers as 'Shared').
- MODEL CONFLICT, scripts nested inside the turn: the design runs the close's 0x801642D4 (0x80024220 at 0x800242F4 / 0x80024434 at 0x800244BC, both -> stepper -> 0x801642D4 at 0x80025194/0x8002558C) inside a guest call of 0x80024AF0 inside the COOP_CONTROLLER hook. The host runs player 2's scripts by TAIL CALL, not guest call: [read] hle.c:15975-15981 coop_use_start sets g_hle_tail_call, lr = HLE_USE_RETURN 0x7FFF0070 (hle.c:375), pc = COOP_RUN_SCRIPT; continue_coop_use at the return (hle.c:5282, 15993-16020) undoes the swaps and restores the saved context. [read] main.c:60-108 hle_guest_call_within polls no interrupts, stops on any exception change, and memcpy's the whole CPUState back (main.c:107): a script that waits on a DVD read or a thread switch (dossier A6: the Tome's way in switched threads inside a call; notify_OSLoadContext FPU handover) stalls or is abandoned inside the turn, and a tail-call continuation set by a hook inside it is lost. coop_script_timers runs player-1 arrival scripts by guest call (hle.c:17957) only when nothing is 'going'.
- 0x800255A4's return is load-bearing; the intercept must not return 0. [read] asm 33513-33520 (0x80024F80-F9C): if (count & 0xFFFF) != 0 the stepper clears the blend bit (+24 &= ~1 -> cut). [read] asm 20130-20134: the script native at 0x80017E64 calls 0x800255A4(hash, blend, r5 = the script's own argument from 0x80163510/fctiwz) and on a nonzero count zeroes r30 (blend) before 0x801EE8E0(hash, TB[2]=0x80603288, ...). So the design's 'return 0' turns cuts into blends in both players' close-ups and in scripted scene cameras. Answer to risk 4's question: yes, run_now != 0 reaches 0x8002580C from inside 0x800255A4 (0x80025748) via the script native 0x80017E64, bypassing the main loop's 0x8000821C; callers [read, asm grep]: 0x800255A4 <- 0x80017E64, 0x80024F80, 0x800252B4; 0x8002576C <- 0x80017ECC, 0x80024C10, 0x80025160, 0x80025558; 0x8002580C <- 0x8000821C, 0x80025748.
- coop_prompt_look runs INSIDE the turn and is itself a swap site that moves windows the other way. [read] hle.c:18444 (called from coop_player_turn between the pad update and COOP_CONTROLLER), 14796-14905: saves the 2060 block and PROMPT_SLOTS, coop_cameras_in(2), coop_zones_swap/states_swap, loads g_coop_prompt_slots and g_coop_p2_looks, guest-calls COOP_SCAN, then (14893) coop_window_to_game(cpu, &g_coop_state[0], ...) moves the channel/focus words of a window a zone script opened in player 2's copy INTO the game's input copy so the game's pad reaches it (hle.c:19320-19350), and guest-calls COOP_PLAY_HOLD(0) in their copy (14902-14904). With the design's block already in for the turn, these nested saves/loads make g_coop_prompt_slots/g_coop_p2_looks/g_coop_cameras.p2 and the block two sources of truth for the same bytes, and coop_window_to_game does the opposite of the design (it is why player 2's windows live in the game's input today). The design's 'one swap pair' is not achievable without rewriting coop_prompt_look, which it lists only as 'use the block function instead'.
- MISSED SWAP SITE: coop_character_step (hle.c:21891-21909) runs player 2's character step from the main loop's behaviour pass (0x8014FDB8 at 0x800084AC, before 0x80117628/0x80024AF0 at 0x800084F8/FC) with CoopPlayerState[1] in but player 1's busy/hold/slot words in (only protected by p1_prompt/coop_words_back). The step reads the hold's channels (in the copy, fine) and the busy word/prompt words (player 1's), and its scan asks prompts into PROMPT_SLOTS (player 1's). Not in the design's list of 'out-of-turn as-player-2 paths' (coop_use_start/continue, tome_copy_left, coop_prompt_look).
- The camera-set swap has its own bookkeeping the design's whole-turn block must obey. [read] hle.c:14210-14225 coop_cameras_in(who) keys on g_coop_set_in ('one word, kept by every swap, so a script can be run with its owner's set in wherever it is reached from'); coop_camera_step (14447-14456) returns early while g_coop_cameras.lent and otherwise calls coop_cameras_in(2) itself round CAMERA_STEP; coop_camera_p2_in (14196-14208) REBUILDS player 2's set from the game's (coop_camera_set_fresh: pops the stack 0x801ECCEC up to 5 times, 14172-14181) whenever the room's start chose a camera since theirs was made (p2_bare && p2_seen != room_choices) -- so player 2's set carrying record 10 and a stack depth mid-close-up can be thrown away by a room camera choice. Also the live-view carry in coop_cameras_lend (14262-14281) is what keeps player 1's half from easing off the close-up's shot; the design deletes the lend without naming where that carry goes.
- Renderer: the HUD block is shared across both players' text, so per-element x is not fully per element. [read] interp.cpp:584-600 'A uniform block several draws share (identical constants are stored once) is the HUD's only when every draw using it is'; offRight/farRight are per BLOCK; 679-690: player 0 with farRight shifts the whole block back by 2*4096, player 1 (away) with offRight shifts the whole block by 4096. Under the design's camera.c change (view 0 hideHud=false always), whenever coop_p1_icon_show puts player 1's icon at 8192 (COOP_PROMPT_P1_AWAY, hle.c:18105-18145) the block holding BOTH players' words text becomes farRight and view 0 shifts it back 8192: player 1's words leave their screen. [inferred from the rule; not run]. The words-at-+4096 part itself is consistent: view 1 shifts the block left 4096 (player 2's words on, player 1's at -4096 off), view 0 leaves it (player 2's at +4096 off right).
- The prompt chooser is already hooked and guest-called by the host; and r13-24040's first word is a code pointer. [read] hle.c:17416-17445 coop_prompt_choose (COOP_PROMPT_CHOOSE 0x80117628) guest-calls the chooser itself with the hold channel's mask opened and busy put back; [read] asm 0x80117688-0x801176B0: the chooser calls the function at r13-24040 (0x800460EC, 'may have a prompt') via bctrl, then 0x801176B4-BC: any nonzero bit in r13-24048 -> no prompt chosen. So (a) the design's second guest call of 0x80117628 in the turn nests inside/alongside an existing hook that must be rewritten, and (b) a per-player -24048 is REQUIRED for DETAIL to appear in player 2's half while player 1's words hold bit 32 -- the design has it, but in a new 8-byte set, while coop_play_hold (hle.c:12855-12867) and coop_use_start (15957-15963) already write -24048 by hand; those writes land in whichever block is in.
- Callers the design did not list [read, asm grep]: 0x80024AF0 <- 0x800084FC (world), 0x800207A0, 0x80050D38, 0x80111484 (Tome): two more frame routines tick *(r13-25960). 0x8013E160 <- 0x80006AB0 (prompt channel), 0x8001B978, 0x80024B08, 0x80045D44 (inside the controller), 0x8004FBAC, 0x80050B74, 0x8007CE98, 0x801114A4/B8 (Tome), 0x80199FA8/B0/B8 (0x80199F98 steps the three control channels r13-23052/-23060/-23056; called from the controller at 0x80045D30 -- in the turn, fine). 0x8011708C <- 0x8000825C (a reset routine 0x80008234, after 0x80117260(16,0)) and 0x80117850 (the chooser). 0x8013E518 (asm 321615-321805) contains no bl 0x8013E160: the pad update does not dispatch channels, so the turn's guest call of 0x80024AF0 would not double-step (confirms that part).
- The behaviour pass that reaches 0x801FA400: [read] 0x8014FDB8 (asm 339575-339601) walks the behaviour list calling 0x8014F3E8(beh, 1, 48|0x800) -- the step pass at main-loop 0x800084AC; 0x8014FE24 (339602-339622) calls 0x8014F3E8(beh, 2, 80) -- the draw pass at 0x800084D0. Inside 0x8014F3E8 (asm L+50..L+78): kind bit0 -> fn at +32(beh)(beh, +56, flag) via bctrl; bit1 -> fn at +28(beh)(beh, +56); children recursed via +20/+4/+8. A hook on 0x801FA400 by address is feasible (it is a function entry reached by bctrl), but it runs mid behaviour pass, before the frame's 0x80117628/0x80024AF0, and the design's turn runs inside the controller hook inside that same pass -- so player 2's window tick would precede player 1's within one frame (ordering only, not fatal).
- Scale of the deletion in step F: g_coop_screen is read on 69 lines of hle.c, g_coop_assist_at on 37, g_coop_turn on 55 (grep -c). camera.c:1615-1663 is one branch; the 'lent' model also feeds hle_coop_player1_camera (hle.c:14537-14546, g_coop_p1_view set only in coop_cameras_lend) -- deleting the lend leaves player 1's kept view never set, and hle_coop_player2_camera (14550-14560) reads g_coop_own_view which coop_camera_step fills only when it runs (skipped while lent, and skipped by the busy gate above).
- Confirmed as the design states [read]: hle_guest_call nests (CPUState saved on the C stack, main.c:70-72); the game's own prompt element is a pool element at 0x805F87D0 (hle.c:22944 comment) so 0x801DA574's pool walk (asm 481396-481402) draws it wherever its +8 is; coop_game_prompt_made (22933-22959) already moves it to COOP_PROMPT_AT by g_coop_screen; coop_found_mark (3270-3277) and marked_out_of (interp.cpp:565-572) are the only renderer lines for a new mark; 0x80117628's slot 1/3/5 gate reads 0x80199F50/0x80199F74 (asm 0x801176F0-0x80117704) = channel masks in the input copy, per player.

## What round 2 must take as given

1. The busy word gates the game's own loop (0x8000749C skips the player's controller, and so player 2's turn, while it is
   nonzero) and player 2's turn refuses on it (coop_player_turn). Any per-player busy must be answered from the *asked*
   player's block at those three asks (coop_busy_ask, the turn's refusal, coop_node_held_ask), or the other player freezes
   exactly as before coop_no_pause.
2. The 2060-byte block at r13-24040 is not swapped per player today (only saved and put back, and never its sound words
   r13-22920..-22396); it holds the busy word, the running script, the channel pointers, the focus, the prompt channel and
   the frame's draw-list count. It cannot be swapped whole.
3. Player 2's press on a prompt and the find's pickup run outside the turn today (coop_use_start at player 1's controller,
   the game's pad in a close-up of theirs); an owner rule keyed on the turn is wrong until those move into the turn, and
   moving them means tail calls become guest calls (a script that waits on a DVD read or a thread would stall the frame).
4. The turn overwrites player 2's channel masks and focus every frame on purpose (a lock and a focus kept for good); a
   window of theirs cannot keep its focus under that.
5. The lend and a whole-turn block cannot both own the camera set and the prompt slots; the lend's live-view carry must go
   somewhere; coop_camera_p2_in can rebuild player 2's set under a close-up.
6. 0x800255A4's return is load-bearing (cut vs blend); the find's thing is placed into a per-frame list in player 1's
   block, not in camera space.
7. The prompt chooser (0x80117628) is already hooked and guest-called by the host and refuses any prompt while any bit of
   r13-24048 is set; a per-player r13-24048 is required.

## The two approaches, named

- Approach 1 (today): answer the game's questions per player at each gate. General, cheap per gate, and every path finds
  its gates one at a time; the examine-and-pickup path touched eight of them in one afternoon.
- Approach 2 (the Tomes): copy the object per player and run the game against the copy. Complete once done; the Tomes
  took several sessions, a hook-off list and a send-back list.

Round 1 tried a third, the per-player block, and the reviews show it is approach 2 in disguise with approach 1's gates
still in the way. Round 2 should start from the seven points above and decide per object.

Requires g_on && !g_skipViews, interp::recording(), the frame still in its staging buffer; computes each half's aspect (layout 0 side by side: (w/2)/h; layout 1 above/below: w/(h/2)); for each player not excluded by g_onlyView calls interp::split_build(player, ...) -> build_view and pushes a FrameOutput Kind::Split with .view=player, .layout, .zoom, .aspect. If a view has no draws, note_whole("no draws kept") logs "[split] frame shown whole" (1490-1494) and the frame is shown whole. plan_outputs (1680-1744) orders: [SplitBetween moments...], Split view 0, Split view 1, then Kind::Real (the game's own frame, drawn last "so what it leaves in the EFB copies is the game's"). plan_between (1551-1612) interpolates the two cameras for in-between moments (120 Hz). - Two-mode swap (world vs Tome frames) g_tw_split / tw_words_io (12989-13061): the six r13 words {25960, 25944, 25948, 25940, 23556, 23552} plus record 0x8023E458 +12/+44/+76 are saved/loaded per mode (words[2][9]); each element's owner (which mode laid it out) is tracked (owner[10]) and the other mode's elements are hidden for a frame by x = TW_ELEMENT_AWAY (-16000), alpha +7 = 0, fade bits +16 held, restored by tw_elements_show (13040-13061). tome_copy_no_window (13365-13390) clears all of them inside a Tome copy. [read]
- PROMPT_MAKE 0x801DB374 / PROMPT_FREE 0x801DA4D8 / PROMPT_STYLE 0x80252D68 "ButtonText" / PROMPT_SLOTS 0x80335A38 (7x28) / PROMPT_ASK 0x80117404 / PROMPT_CLEAR 0x80117360 / PROMPT_LOOKS 0x80307108 (68 bytes) / PROMPT_SCREEN r13-24036 (13956-13995). coop_prompt_show (18068-18097) and coop_p1_icon_show (18105-18145) make/free elements via guest calls, styled by coop_prompt_style (18054-18066, FINISH HIM id 23 via PROMPT_WORDS 0x801DB864 / PROMPT_FORMAT 0x801DA8CCu). COOP_GAME_PROMPT_MAKE 0x8011708C (22933, intercept coop_game_prompt_made hooked 24290-24294) moves the game's own new prompt into player 2's half (22909, 22951 write COOP_PROMPT_AT). coop_prompt_show is called from the controller at 23993. [read]

F. WHAT IT WOULD TAKE: the words window twice (one per half, each its own text)
Facts that constrain it [read]: one r13-25960 channel, one focus r13-23556, one tick 0x80024AF0 per frame, one close 0x80024220 that runs r13-25944; but TEN elements (0x805F87D0) all drawn every frame by 0x801DA574 regardless of the window, and 20 channel slots in 0x8013DB3C's table.
Pieces that already exist [read]: (1) drawing a second 2D element in only one half = make it with 0x801DB374 and put its x at +4096 (player 2's half, "away"/offRight) or leave it at the game's x (player 1's half, since hideHud hides it in player 2's view); this is exactly coop_prompt_show/the bars. An element at x+4096 is drawn by the game once, shown by the renderer only in player 2's half; nothing per-draw is needed because build_view works on 2D box positions. (2) Two windows' STATE swapped per frame already exists for world/Tome (tw_words_io: the six r13 words + 0x8023E458's three r4s), and per-player input copies exist (coop_player_turn / COOP_INPUT / focus, hle.c:14839-14904, 18328-18335 g_coop_p2_words_held). (3) 0x8013DB3C can hold a second channel (20 slots) and 0x8013E160 ticks one channel given its pointer; 0x80024220 reads r13-25960/-25944 so a close must run with the right player's set swapped in.
What is missing [inferred]: a per-player "words set" {r13-25960, -25944, -25948, -25940, -23556, -23552, 0x8023E458+12/+44/+76, and the element's x} swapped in around each player's turn and before the tick/close, as tw_words_io does for modes; opening player 2's words via the game's own 0x80024310 inside their turn (already the path for coop_prompt_look windows) and then moving that element's +8 by +4096 (and keeping it there across 0x801DB088 relayouts: hle_words_layout is the hook point, it runs on every layout). The B-icon and box belong to the same element (hle.c:13001 comment), so moving +8 moves all of it. Risks: 0x80024AF0 ticks only the r13-25960 window of the moment, so both windows need the tick run twice (once per set, e.g. a guest call to 0x8013E160(channel) in the other player's turn), and the focus r13-23556 passes over every channel but one (0x8013E1A0), which is why the sets must be swapped with the input copies, not shared. The words' hold 0x80042A8C is already passed over for both (coop_words_hold), so no new pause logic.
G. The close-up's camera per half [read + inferred]
Already per player: each player has their own camera set (records 0x80602B18.., stack 0x806038E8, depth r13-21700, index r13-26864) swapped by coop_camera_set_io (hle.c:14152, COOP_CAMERA_SET), lent as a whole by coop_cameras_lend (14247-14270) for player 2's close-up; the live view 0x803012E0 (CAMERA_VIEW, 52 bytes, FOV at +48) is kept per player (g_coop_own_view / g_coop_p1_view + zooms) and handed to camera.c via hle_coop_player1_camera / hle_coop_player2_camera, which camera_split_update turns into a per-half rot/pos relative to the game camera. coop_camera_step (14447-14480) already steps player 2's set with the game's camera step CAMERA_STEP in their turn (0x801EB9A0 per docs/game/systems/camera.md) and restores the game's view after. Record 10 is the close-up's (0x80024E90 pushes it; docs/game/systems/camera.md). So the renderer side needs nothing new: a second close-up at the same time only needs the other player's set to carry record 10 and be stepped in their turn (coop_camera_step is skipped while lent, 14448-14449, so the "lent" model, which assumes one close-up at a time, is the thing to change), plus hide_hud ownership in camera.c:1650-1656 would have to become per-element (both halves showing their own words/prompt) instead of one owner view: the words/prompt elements of player 2 at +4096 and player 1's at the game's x, with both views' hideHud=true for the rest of the HUD except what each brings back (offRight for player 1, farRight for player 0 as coop_p1_icon_show already does).
Related docs: docs/game/systems/words.md, docs/game/systems/prompts.md, docs/game/systems/camera.md, docs/coop-failed-attempts.md:85 and :182, docs/coop-next-session.md:9 (the user's both-at-once ask).
