=== report ===
SCOPE: read-only. Sources: runtime/host/hle.c (25408 lines), runtime/host/main.c, patches/gxruntime/eternal-darkness.patch (renderer side), docs/coop-two-tomes.md, docs/coop-two-tomes-design.md, docs/coop-failed-attempts.md 52-90/147-152, docs/coop-todo.md 27-30, docs/coop-next-session.md 253-262, build/runs/enchant/asm.txt 20675-20704. "[read]" = seen in code/docs; "[inferred]" = my reading of it.

================================================================
PATTERN A: THE WHOLE-MEMORY COPY (player 2's Tome, and player 1's Tome over player 2's close-up)
================================================================

A1. Switch and state [read]
- tome_copy_mode() hle.c:3866-3870: env ED_COOP_TOME_COPY, default 2 (on). 0 = old one-Tome path, 1 = empty-turn self-test (tome_copy_test 13280-13338), 2 = the copy.
- g_tome_copy struct hle.c:13140-13152: u8* copy; u8* real; bool started, has_magick; bool stands_in (player 1 has no Tome: the copy is the "world behind a Tome" owner); u8* clean (snapshot of real memory taken as player 1's Tome is asked for); bool clean_set; unsigned turns; int kinds_depth, kinds_count; u8 kinds[]; double ms_most.
- Flags 3854-3864: g_tome_copy_ask, g_tome_copy_open, g_tome_copy_p1 (the copy is player 1's), g_tome_copy_p1_pad (player 1's pad as last read, set at 4237), g_tome_copy_p1_hold, g_tome_copy_held, g_tome_copy_taps, g_tome_copy_spell[24], g_tome_copy_spell_set/open. g_tome_copy_turn at 565 ("we are inside the copy's turn").
- Open requests (g_tome_copy_ask = true): 23398 player 2's Start release in coop_menu_input; 23109 player 2's Start edge while a Tome is already shared (hle_coop_tome_shares()); 12301 player 2's Enchant cast asking for a Tome (coop_quick_cast: the 24-byte spell record 0x80335748 is taken into g_tome_copy_spell and the real record + r13-26188/-26184 put back); 23471 player 1's Start in player 2's close-up (sets g_tome_copy_p1 too).

A2. What memory is copied [read]
- The ENTIRE guest RAM: g_tome_copy.copy = malloc(cpu->ram_size) once (13568-13569); each opening memcpy(copy, source, cpu->ram_size) (13573). Design doc: ~40 MB, 3-7 ms per copy. Source = cpu->ram when no Tome of player 1's is up, else g_tome_copy.clean: a full snapshot memcpy'd in tw_mode_change 13807-13819 at the moment r13-26240==2 && r13-26188==3 (the world has asked for mode 3, before the Tome's way in ran), after taking the FPU (13810-13814). Patched on top of the copy: the story-flag block at r13-26528 (4 + ceil(count/32)*4 bytes, <=1028) 13574-13581, because the snapshot may be seconds old.
- Not copied [read, design doc risk 2]: ARAM; host (C) variables. GX record copied at every swap (A3).
- A second whole-RAM buffer g_tome_copy.clean exists alongside, so up to 3 x ram_size resident [inferred from 13568/13815].

A3. Swap in/out [read]
- tome_copy_swap(cpu, in) 13346-13353: gx = r13-26688 (TOME_COPY_GX_POINTER, the GX library record __GXData, 0x4F8 bytes, 13135-13136); memcpy that record from the current memory into the target (one GPU, its mirrored registers must be shared); cpu->ram = to; dol_aurora_mem1_turn(to).
- Why one pointer suffices: recompiled code reads through mem_read32 -> resolve_addr -> cpu->ram each time (.deps/GXRuntime/src/core/cpu.c:102 per design doc 125); host hle.c/dvd.c/mmio.c also go through cpu->ram. The one cached pointer is the renderer's g_mem1 (patch 590), switched by Op::Mem1Turn queued in stream order from the game thread (mem1_turn_op patch 1973-1979; consumed at 1613-1615 install_frontend_mem1), so the renderer's reads switch at the exact frame boundary of the draws it belongs to.
- Swap frequency: per TURN (one Tome frame), not per hle call: swap in at 13593, swap out at 13674. Between, every nested hle dispatch and guest call runs against the copy.

A4. When a turn runs, and the frame order [read]
- Hook site: TW_MODE_CHANGE 0x80018728 (12920), the function the main loop 0x800186E8 calls at 0x800186F4 before dispatching the mode's frame routine r13-26192 via mtctr/bctrl at 0x800186F8-0x80018700, then 0x80113DF8, 0x8013E518, 0x801718C0, (0x801DC9F8 unless mode==4), 0x8012EDDC, b 0x800186E8 (asm.txt 20675-20690). 0x80018728 itself compares r13-26240 (mode) with r13-26188 (asked) and switches (20691-20704).
- tw_mode_change 13794 -> tome_copy_step 13527 runs once per loop iteration. Order per round (design doc "How it runs", code 13558-13559): World frame -> player 1's real Tome frame if open (g_tw.sharing, world_tick false) -> copy's turn. With player 1's Tome up the step returns early on the world's tick (13558: `g_tw.sharing && !stands_in && g_tw.world_tick`), so the copy gets one turn after each of player 1's Tome frames. With no Tome of player 1's (stands_in), the copy IS the "Tome behind which the world runs": after the turn g_tw.sharing=true, world_tick=true, worlds_left=2 (13683-13686), so the host's existing world-behind-Tome code (kTwSwitches/TW_BLOCK, 12932-12994) treats the real world as "the world ticking behind a Tome" even though the Tome lives in the copy.
- Guard world_stays 13531-13544: mode and asked both in {2,3}, no cutscene, player 2 listed; otherwise the copy closes ("the world asks for something else").

A5. One turn, step by step (tome_copy_step 13560-13700) [read]
 1. FPU: if MSR[FP] clear, interrupt_switch_fpu_context in REAL memory first (13560-13564); a copy taken with another thread owning the FPU gave exception 32 at 0x80018728 lr 0x801110B0 (design doc "What test 5 found").
 2. Opening only: memcpy as A2; turns=0.
 3. dol_aurora_split_skip_views(p1 ? 1 : 2) (13590): "a menu's frame, its player+1" to the renderer; dol_aurora_frame_unpaced(true) (13591) so the present isn't paced (patch 1111-1120: kUnpaced).
 4. Save GQR[0..7] (13598-13599).
 5. tome_copy_swap(in).
 6. Opening only, in the copy: tome_copy_no_window 13365-13403 (frees any words window up at the snapshot: guest-calls 0x801DA4D8 on the words' elements and 0x8013DB94 on the window; zeroes r13-25960 window, -25944/-25948 its script/button, -23552/-23556 focus, -23344 busy, -24048 &= ~(2|4|32), -25932 find byte, -25696 play hold, -23040 := 1, -26324 script left for the world; marks Magick help flags 500/990/992/622 done in the flag set at r13-26528); coop_tome_player(cpu, true) 22742-22764 (r13-26524 := player 2's object, r13-21496 := their id, coop_equip_record, coop_equip_names, coop_quick_swap, coop_kinds; player 1's two words kept in g_coop_tome_was); coop_spells_in; save g_coop_kinds_* into g_tome_copy.kinds* and zero them (13605-13609); r13-29060 (last page) := 1 Inventory, r13-24448 (Journal-new) := 0, r13-26240 := 2, r13-26188 := 3 (13613-13616); COOP_SPELL_ITEM 0x80335748 zeroed or filled from g_tome_copy_spell (13617-13621); has_magick := guest call 0x80109A68; pad snapshot -> g_tome_copy_held (13626-13631); split: stands_in -> aurora_split_menu(1, layout) + aurora_split_only_view(0); beside player 1's -> only_view(2) (13635-13642). For player 1's copy: g_tome_copy_p1_record := magick record 0x80334D50 (0xD0) for the diff at close (13610-13612).
 7. g_tw.sharing=true, world_tick=false, g_coop_tome_p2 = !p1, g_tome_copy_turn=true (13649-13653).
 8. First turn: ok = hle_guest_call(TW_MODE_CHANGE) = the game's own way into the Tome (0x80018728 with mode 2 asked 3), then tome_copy_callbacks (13655-13658). Later turns: leaving = r13-26188 != 3 || r13-24244 != 3 (TOME_COPY_PAGE_STATE 13137); the Tome's way out is NEVER run in the copy (it waits at 0x80110DC4 for the room to come back; failed-attempts 66-70).
 9. Frame: guest-call 0x801E58C8(0,0,0) fog; guest-call mem_read32(r13-26192) (= TW_TOME_FRAME 0x80111234); then kAfterFrame {0x80113DF8, 0x8013E518, 0x801718C0, 0x801DC9F8, 0x8012EDDC} as the loop does; tome_copy_callbacks (13662-13670).
10. g_tome_copy_turn=false, g_coop_tome_p2=false, tome_copy_swap(out), GQRs restored (13672-13679), skip_views(0), tome_copy_equips in REAL memory (13681), g_tw flags restored or set as A4, timing; close if !ok or leaving (13697-13698).

A6. How the game's own functions run against the copy [read]
- hle_guest_call / hle_guest_call_within main.c:57-110: takes FPU if needed; memcpy's CPUState up to srr0; sets r3..r10 from args, f1; r1 -= 0x800 (16-aligned); lr = GUEST_CALL_SENTINEL 0x0BADF010; loops dolrecomp_call(cpu, pc) until pc == sentinel, up to 50,000,000 blocks; rfi on system-call exceptions, stops on any other exception change; returns r3 only (r4 answers are lost: failed-attempts "Door arrival"); restores CPUState. It polls NO interrupts (design doc test 1 note), so no retrace/audio/OS-thread interrupt lands in the copy. Nested hle dispatches inside run normally (and against cpu->ram = the copy).
- The copy's thread switches: the Tome's way in (0x80018728 -> 0x80042910 -> 0x800BBC08) posts a stop message to the background thread's queue 0x805B2AFC and waits on 0x805B2B5C (OSReceiveMessage 0x80201A2C), so the game's scheduler switches threads INSIDE the guest call (comment 4600-4607). Fix: notify_OSLoadContext 4624-4640 hands the FPU to the loaded context when it is __OSCurrentContext 0x800000D4 (otherwise the lazy FP-unavailable exception ended the call mid-thread), and GQRs are put back after each turn (ch11 crash: psq_lu at 0x8011E398 with GQR6 0 -> alignment exception pc 0x600 lr 0x8011E734).
- Waits skipped inside the turn: tw_retrace_wait (lr 0x801E4768) and tw_frame_wait (lr 0x801DFA50, returns 1) when g_tome_copy_turn (13244-13262). frontend_frame (Esc menu / save state) is not run in the turn (5250-5254) because a state saved then would be the copy.
- Callbacks: queue_guest_callback_after 568-581: during the turn, DVD/card completion callbacks go to g_tome_copy_callbacks[] instead of the main-loop queue and are drained in the copy by tome_copy_callbacks 13514-13525 (chained loader reads; page turn spun at 0x80103C54 when they were left to the main loop, failed-attempts 72).
- Hook gate: hle_dispatch 5457-5459: in the turn, any entry whose intercept or notify is in tome_copy_hook_off's list (23746-23770, ~50 names: hle_coop_controller, coop_sighting, coop_events, coop_character_step, hle_coop_found, coop_script_timers, tw_mode_change, tw_tome_start, coop_play_hold, coop_cast, coop_send_event, notify_GXSetDrawDone, coop_prompt_choose/set/clear, coop_words_hold, coop_find_hold, coop_flags_ask, coop_busy_ask, hle_find_by_id, hle_moves_*, coop_game_camera ...) returns false = the game's own code runs unhooked. Everything else (SDK HLE, GX, pad, words layout) runs, against the copy.

A7. Host statics kept per copy [read]
- Not copied with memory, so handled one by one: g_coop_kinds_in/depth/count saved into g_tome_copy.kinds* at open (13605-13609) and restored at close (13484-13486); g_coop_tome_was by coop_tome_player; g_tw.sharing/world_tick saved as sharing_was/tick_was around the turn (13587, 13687-13689); GQRs; g_coop_tome_p2 only true during the turn. Per-copy OUTPUTS collected in host statics by hooks that stay on: g_tome_copy_equip[16][5] (22707; coop_equip_take 22712-22719 records 0x800C9FD4's r3..r7 when lr in TOME_ITEM_CODE 0x80102000..0x80104800 instead of letting the real-game equip logic run), g_tome_copy_use_script/item (16317-16320, the Tome's 0x801642D4(script,item) call), g_tome_copy_taps/held. Other copy-only rewrites: hle_words_layout 16706-16716 renames tab labels "Options"->"Magick"/"Map", "Journal"->"Inventory" in the copy's memory; tome_copy_tab 13706-13719 redirects the tab-ring calls (lr 0x80103C44 -> 0x8010AAF0 Magick or 0x80111B40 Map; others -> 0x80102810 Inventory) by setting pc, only in the turn.

A8. Drawing: how the copy's frame reaches one half [read code, mechanism partly inferred]
- Game thread: dol_aurora_split_skip_views(n) before the turn, (0) after. Renderer (patch 5518-5519, 5538, 5651-5656, 6654): g_skipViews != 0 means "this frame is a menu's frame of player (n-1)": split::plan() returns no split outputs for it (5538), and split_menu_turn() tells the interp whose menu picture it is; those draws are kept as that half's picture (aurora_split_menu(player, layout) sets g_splitMenu 5027-5030, "the Tome's last picture in its owner's half" comment 12913-12916). World frames: split_only_view(player) (5669-5677) makes plan() build only that player's view (5562, 5608); -1 both; 2 = both halves are menus (split_two_menus 5661), world not drawn; it also turns frame-start pacing off (g_noFrameStartPacing). Combinations used: stands_in -> split_menu(1)+only_view(0); copy beside player 1's Tome -> only_view(2); player 1's copy over player 2's close-up -> split_menu(0)+only_view(1) (13644-13647). hle_coop_ui_over 13160-13181 undoes all this while the host's in-game menu is over the game (the menu's frames were being kept as Tome frames and the game looked frozen).
- Textures are keyed by content hash (design doc 129, TextureKey gxcore_draw.cpp:299), so the same address with other contents is safe.
- Cost seen: with both Tomes up longest turns 52.79/45.71 ms vs ~19 (design doc, hand9), unexplained; single copy Tome frames up to 7.35 ms with player 1's half at ~60 FPS.

A9. Input to the copy [read]
- hle_PADRead 4058-4060: if g_tome_copy_turn (and not the disc/reset monitor thread, pad_read_by_monitor 4045-4053) -> tome_copy_pad 3986-4025: pad 0 = player 2's controller (controls_set_tome_read(1); controls_read_player2) or g_tome_copy_p1_pad when the copy is player 1's; |= g_tome_copy_taps (presses between the copy's reads); buttons held at opening masked until released (g_tome_copy_held, A/B analogs zeroed); pads 1-3 error -1 (no controller); hle_set_u32(0).
- Outside the turn while the copy is open: player 2's world pad is emptied and its buttons accumulate into g_tome_copy_taps (4084-4092, tome_pad); their Start is masked from the world (4101). Player 1's copy: g_tome_copy_p1_pad set at every player-1 read (4237); their world pad emptied (4248-4255); what they held at close kept out of play (g_tome_copy_p1_hold 13441, 4256+).
- The game's pad-held word COOP_INPUT+48 is kept per read-owner (world vs Tome) at 23112-23123 so a button is not a new press in both.

A10. Closing: what comes back to the real game [read]
- tome_copy_close 13459-13512 (player 2): with cpu->ram = copy, read r13-26324 (script an item left for the world), COOP_NEXT_CAST 0x80334DFC, COOP_SPELL_ITEM 0x80335748 (24 bytes), g_coop_spells[24 words] from COOP_MAGICK_RECORD 0x80334D50+8; trace-diff the magick record copy vs real; restore g_coop_kinds_*; coop_tome_player(false) in the copy; cpu->ram = real. The copy's contents are then simply abandoned (buffer reused). Split views reset or left as player 1's. Then tome_copy_left 23585-23711 re-does the decided effects in REAL memory: a cast via COOP_CAST 0x801C4250 with coop_be_player2 around it and the real COOP_NEXT_CAST/spell record saved and put back; or "backed out" via COOP_CAST_SET(0) and state 64 cleared (0x801F64A0/0x801F6488); an item's script via COOP_RUN_SCRIPT 0x801642D4 in two steps (the Tome's (script,item,0) call then (script,0,0), second only if r13-25960 == 0) with coop_tome_player + coop_be_player2 + coop_zones_swap + coop_states_swap + coop_cameras_lend around, spell-record word 0 set to the copy's chosen item, r13-26324 cleared as 0x800082B0 does, and the words/close-up it opened marked theirs (g_coop_screen, coop_hold_player1). Equips: tome_copy_equips 23510-23535 replays EQUIP_TAKE 0x800C9FD4 and EQUIP_MOVES 0x8007A788 as player 2 after the turn.
- Player 1's copy: tome_copy_p1_close 13421-13457 diff-merges the magick record word by word against g_tome_copy_p1_record (only changed words written to real) and defers script/cast into g_tome_copy_p1_left, run by tome_copy_p1_left 23537-23583 only when player 2's close-up/use/words are over (or beside player 2's bare scene).
- "Use has two stages" (design doc): the Tome's call leaves its word in the script table and r13-26324; the world then runs the script with item 0; both must run in real memory or the first use does nothing.

A11. Known limits and bugs of pattern A [read, docs]
- design doc "Still unsolved"/"Limits": sound from the copy's Tome is never heard (voices live in guest memory; 0x8019DB90 not forwarded); Mix not sent; a new spell made in player 2's copy is theirs only via g_coop_spells (runes/circles shared); labels English only; story scripts on Tome open (480, 1979, 694, 821 at 0x80102A90) run in the copy and are lost for player 2; ARAM writes not covered; the snapshot `clean` can be seconds stale (only story flags re-patched); quick save/menu can't run in a turn.
- failed-attempts 62-90: close by asked-mode alone hung at 0x80110DC4 (fixed with r13-24244); callbacks on the main loop spun 0x80103C54; item scripts read the REAL spell record's item -> Talisman "drained" (fixed by writing word 0 both steps); second script step re-opened words (guard r13-25960==0, never exercised since); matching labels by a '.' (log artefact). 147-152: player 1's copy's world reads marked as the Tome's bounced player 2's camera; reading player 1's pad twice a frame killed every press (controls.cpp held-over logic) and scripted presses can't test controls.cpp.
- coop-todo 30: an item run inside player 1's copy was taken as player 2's by coop_run_script's Tome-item rule (fixed 2026-11-10, unplayed); player 1's copy item waits for player 2's close-up/scene by design.
- Per-open cost 3-7 ms memcpy of 40 MB plus a second 40 MB `clean` snapshot per player-1 Tome ask; ~1 extra game frame of CPU per turn; the hook gate list must be maintained by hand for every new co-op hook (138 registrations sorted once per design doc).

================================================================
PATTERN B: SWAP A BLOCK AROUND A CALL (CoopPlayerState and friends)
================================================================

B1. CoopPlayerState hle.c:11798-11805 [read]: u8 input[816] @ COOP_INPUT 0x805880A0 (raw pads, processed pads, input channels from +176, masks at +0xE0); u8 control[0x300] @ COOP_CONTROL 0x805D7900 (control blocks, 0x805D7BA0 among them); u8 aim[208] (channel 3's context block, pointer at COOP_INPUT+176+3*32+12); u8 globals[48] r13-25680..-25632 (controller state); u8 pad_globals[56] r13-23604..-23548 (pad routine's; focus r13-23556 = +48, lock -23592); u32 interact[5] {r13-29016, -24032, -23196, -23192, -21996} (scan result: who/what the player is next to). Total 1916 bytes [computed]. coop_state(cpu, s, save) 12077-12087 copies them in/out with coop_bytes (mem_read/write, i.e. in whatever cpu->ram is); breath words r13-29528/-29532 kept beside it (coop_breath 12035-12075). Instances: g_coop_state[2] 11817, g_coop_p1_free 17159, g_coop_events_p1 19237, statics game/own in coop_player1_turn 17318, p1 in coop_character_step 21891.

B2. coop_player_turn 18268-18520 [read], at the player-controller hook COOP_CONTROLLER 0x80045BC4 each frame before player 1's: refuses while busy (coop_busy) unless door/room-change; coop_kinds(true); coop_state(&g_coop_state[0], save); coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], load) if kept; player identity words to player 2 (coop_be_player2 12147-12165: r13-26524 object, -21492 node, -21496 id, -21488 id, -21484 node, plus the casting words kCoopCasting swapped with g_coop_casting); COOP_CAMERA 0x803012E0 swapped; threat words r13-25008/-25004 swapped; guest-call COOP_PAD_UPDATE 0x8013E518 with g_coop_pad_p2 so hle_PADRead answers player 2's controller (4064-4121); coop_quick_cast; guest-call COOP_CONTROLLER(p2 node); coop_camera_step; then coop_state(&g_coop_state[1], save), words back, camera back, coop_state(&g_coop_state[0], load), rehold/give/take held, kinds(false), copies_follow. coop_player1_turn 17318-17400 mirrors it with `game`/`own` while player 2's close-up owns the game's input (clears focus r13-23552/-23556 and opens masks 0x0FFFFFFE->0x0FFFFFFF for the call).

B3. coop_states_swap(to_p2) 14342-14345 [read]: SCRIPT_STATES 0x805CAA40, 80 bytes (10 x {script s16, value, timer, flag}) <-> g_coop_states.p1/p2. coop_state_to_game 14320-14340 moves one script's word.

B4. coop_zones_swap(info, to_p2) 14382-14394 [read]: for each zone of the room (list at character-info+180, count +176, COOP_ZONE_SIZE 116, up to 256) the "inside" word at +104 is swapped with g_coop_zones.p1/p2[]; coop_zones_new 14352-14366 clears player 2's on a room change.

B5. coop_cameras_lend(on) 14247-14300 [read]: swaps the whole camera set COOP_ZONE_CAMERAS 0x80602B18..CAMERA_STACK_END 0x8060394C (0xE34 bytes: the records and the stack entries at 0x806038E8) plus depth r13-21700 and index r13-26864 (coop_camera_set_io 14152-14168) between g_coop_cameras.p1/p2; carries the live view COOP_CAMERA (9 floats) both ways; swaps PROMPT_SLOTS 0x80335A38 (7x28) <-> g_coop_cameras.p1_prompts / g_coop_prompt_slots and PROMPT_LOOKS 0x80307108 (68) <-> p1_looks / g_coop_p2_looks (unless ED_COOP_SHARED_LOOKS). Lent for the DURATION of player 2's close-up, not per call (set in coop_use_start 15960, tome_copy_left 23650; back in continue_coop_use 16009, play-goes-on rules 23283/23373/23460).

B6. Where blocks are swapped around a single call [read]: coop_use_start 15930-15975 / continue_coop_use 15977-16015 (be_player2 + kinds + states_swap + zones_swap + cameras_lend around COOP_RUN_SCRIPT via tail call with HLE_USE_RETURN); coop_prompt_look 14796-14870 (cameras_in(2), zones_swap, states_swap, PROMPT_SLOTS and PROMPT_LOOKS swapped, prompt block r13-24040 (2060 bytes) saved around COOP_SCAN 0x80134A44); coop_character_step 21891-21909 (CoopPlayerState p1 + 2060-byte prompt block around COOP_CHARACTER_STEP); coop_events_words 19250-19263 (CoopPlayerState + r13-24048 bit 8 around COOP_EVENTS); coop_game_camera 22770-22790 (identity words around CAMERA_STEP 0x801EB9A0); tome_copy_left (A10).

B7. The words window under pattern B [read]: the game has ONE window r13-25960 (opened by 0x80024310 from channel 0x8013DB3C, closed-with -25944/-25948/-25940), ONE focus r13-23556/-23552, elements 0x805F87D0 x10 drawn by 0x801DA574 in both modes' frames (comment 12996-13010). Two partial per-owner treatments exist: (a) tw_split 13012-13055: kTwWords {25960, 25944, 25948, 25940, 23556, 23552} + 3 words of the words record 0x8023E458 (+12/+44/+76) saved/loaded per MODE (world vs Tome) at each mode's frame (tw_words_io), and each element's alpha (+7), fade (+16 bits 0x40/0x80) and x (+8 := -16000 TW_ELEMENT_AWAY) hidden for the other mode's frame (tw_elements_hide/show), owner = whose frame laid it out (tw_element_laid via hle_words_layout). (b) coop_window_to_game 19320-19350: a window opened inside player 2's input COPY (focus +48 of pad_globals, channel words COOP_INPUT+176..) has its changed words moved into the game's copy so the game's pad reaches it; the window itself stays the game's one, the close-up is marked theirs (g_coop_screen, g_coop_assist_at, cameras lent), and their body is held by the game's own channel hold 0x80199EA0(0) in their copy (18333-18340, coop-todo 30).

B8. Costs seen with pattern B [read, docs]: every READER of the shared words has to be found (coop-todo 27: "approach 1 (answer the game's questions per player) keeps surfacing gates" — 20 user plays on one close-up-beside-a-scene; the one find flag r13-24048&2 gates the main loop's script timers at 0x80008490; one look record count; one scene flag fn22; one hide bit r13-24048&1; one busy word -23344). Per-call swaps are cheap (hundreds of bytes to ~3.6 KB) and run in the real memory, so nothing has to be "sent back". Design doc's table of two-tomes-wip bugs is the cautionary case for pattern B applied to a LARGE object: 186 ranges + heap words, each missed range a new bug, each wrongly kept one a freeze, 80% speed.

================================================================
WHICH FITS A PER-PLAYER CLOSE-UP + WORDS WINDOW [inferred]
================================================================
- Pattern A fits an object that is (1) the game's "paused solo" state by design, (2) self-contained (no world side-effects while open), (3) has FEW lasting outputs that can be read at close and replayed (Tome: equip, use-script+item, next cast, spell list; A10). The Tome's way out never has to run. Costs: a 40 MB memcpy per open (+ a second snapshot per player-1 Tome ask), one extra frame of CPU per world frame, a hand-kept hook-off list, callbacks/threads/FPU/GQR handling, no sound, and every lasting effect re-done in real memory with its own identity/zone/state/camera swaps anyway (tome_copy_left IS pattern B).
- A close-up / words window is the opposite kind of object: its whole point is a world effect while it is open (pickup -> carry list, flags, scripts' timers and callbacks such as the shelf's event 6, 0x801F7748; camera stack pushes; busy/hold bits that other systems read). In a disposable copy those would be lost or need a growing "send back" list — the loop the two-tomes doc warns against. Also both modes' frames already run the words (0x800084FC world, 0x80111484 Tome), and the per-player state is SMALL and already enumerated in code: window + script/button words (6 r13 words + 3 record words, tw_words_io), focus (2 words), prompt block r13-24040 2060 bytes, prompt slots 196 bytes, look record 68 bytes, camera set 0xE34+8, script states 80, CoopPlayerState 1916, busy/hold bits in -23344/-24048/-25932/-25696/-23040. So pattern B — a per-player "close-up state" block swapped in for that player's frame/turn, generalising tw_split's per-mode words swap and coop_cameras_lend's duration-based lend — matches what the code already does and what coop-todo 27 calls "level 3 (the close-up stepper, its prompt and the find copied per player)". Its cost is the gate hunt (B8): the words/close-up readers in the main loop and stepper have to be listed from the disassembly first (the user's stated condition), not found by play.
- Hybrid worth noting [inferred]: pattern A's renderer half (skip_views "this frame is player N's menu picture", only_view, Mem1Turn) is independent of the memory copy and could show a per-player close-up frame in one half without a memory copy, if the close-up stepper and words were run per player in real memory with pattern B swaps. The game-side precedent for running the SAME frame routine twice with different blocks in is tw (world and Tome frames alternating with kTwSwitches/TW_BLOCK swapped, 12932-12994), which also carries the known cost: the hidden list of what each mode owns (kTwSwitches grew to 13 words + the camera depth/index after the 2026-11-05 stuck-camera bug, two-tomes.md 59).

=== report ===
Inventory of the close-up stepper 0x80024D78..0x800255A0 (build/runs/enchant/asm.txt lines 33383-33905). Everything below is READ from the machine code unless marked [inferred] or [docs] (taken from docs/game/... without re-reading). Bases, read at asm lines 2250-2253 (0x80005424): r2 = 0x80628460, r13 = 0x80625420; absolute addresses for r13/r2 words are computed from those.

== 1. Shape of the function ==
- r31 = 0x80307108 (look record). r26 = r31+8 (press counters). r30 = slot: 2, or 4 when the index word +64 is nonzero (0x80024D94..DAC).
- Entry = (+64) << 5, i.e. look 0 at 0x80307108, look 1 at 0x80307128. Every call first reads the press counter +8 into r7, then stores +8+1 (0x80024DA4, 0x80024DBC-DC0), then dispatches on kind +0 (0x80024DCC-DF0): 1 -> words only; 2 -> ed219 close-up; 3 -> ed221 pick-up; 4 or other -> return.
- Kind 1 (0x80024DF4): +0 := 0; words = 0x801DB374(r13-26496, +12 group, +16 entry, 0, 1, 0x8023E5A0 style); 0x80024310(words, +4 script, 0). Done.
- Kind 2, by r7 (press before increment) (0x80024E44-E68): 0 open (0x80024E6C), 1 words (0x80025050), 2 ask RETURN (0x800250B8), 3 close (0x80025114), else return.
- Kind 3 (0x8002519C-51C4): 0 open (0x800251C8), 1 words (0x80025344), 2 ask PICK UP (0x800253AC), 3 the find (0x80025408), 4 close (0x800254F8), else return.
- Open, kind 2 (0x80024E6C-80025048), in order: (a) if r13-26524 (player object) != 0: flags = 0x80122514(obj); 0x801223B8(obj, flags|4). (b) 0x80199EA0(0) hold. (c) 0x801ECD68() camera push. (d) 0x801EEB58(10) record 10 on. (e) 0x801EB974(TB[10]=0x806036C8, f=*(r2-32136)=*0x806206D8) and 0x801EB974(TA[10]=0x80603068, same f): blend divisor into +48 of both. (f) t = 0x801F0638(obj, +20 hash, +24&1); if 0: t = 0x801F077C(obj, hash, n&1, f1=*(r2-32132)=*0x806206DC); if t != 0: +24 |= 4 then +24 &= ~1 (0x80024F30-F48). (g) if +24 & 4: 0x80043C88(0) (0x80024F64). (h) r = 0x800255A4(hash, n&1, 0); if (r & 0xFFFF): +24 &= ~1 (0x80024F9C). (i) 0x801EE8E0(hash, TB[10], 0, 0, n&1, 0, 0, 0, [sp+8]=0) sets record 10 from the room's camera entry (0x80024FD4). (j) if +16 entry >= 0: 0x80117404(2, 34 DETAIL, -2, +4 script, 50, slot r30, 1) (0x80025048); else 0x80117404(2, 7 RETURN, -2, script, 50, slot, 1) and +8 := 3 (0x80025014-5024).
- Open, kind 3 (0x800251C8-8002533C): same but tests only 0x801F0638 (0x8002524C), and always asks DETAIL 34 (0x8002533C).
- Words, kind 2 (0x80025050-50B4) and kind 3 (0x80025344-53A8): words = 0x801DB374(r13-26496, group, entry, 0, 1, 0x8023E5A0); 0x80024310(words, -2, 0); then 0x801ECD68() push AGAIN, 0x801EEB58(10), both 0x801EB974 blends again. (The words window's own hold 0x80042A8C(1), called inside 0x80024310, has cleared the camera stack and put record 2 on; this re-push is what RETURN pops. [docs camera.md; the push and index set are read].)

== 8. 0x800255A4(hash, blend, run_now) (line 33906) and r13-25952 ==
Loops every node in the room (0x801F6310 list, 0x801F6334 next; room test 0x801F662C(node) == r13-23276 (0x8061FA34)); only kind 0x801F62C0 == 1, type 0x801F62D0 not 37/64/69, and 0x80117C64() not 32..35; obj = 0x801F633C(node); if type == 5: temporarily adds *(r2-32128) to the object's 0x80118850 value (0x801188D8) around the test; t = 0x801F0638(obj, hash, blend) else 0x801F077C(obj, hash, blend, *(r2-32132)); if t and the node has a behaviour (0x8014ED1C) whose 0x8014F898 == 0x8002790C: object flags (0x80033874) |= 0x04000000 via 0x800338BC, count++. If count: r13-25952 (0x8061EEC0) := 1. If run_now: 0x8002580C(). Returns count. Stepper passes run_now = 0, so 0x8002580C runs from the main loop (0x8000821C).
0x8002576C() (line 34020): counts kind-1 nodes in the room with object flag 0x04000000; if any: r13-25952 := 2; returns count. Called at every close and the cancel (0x80025160, 0x80025558, 0x80024C10) and by the script native at 0x80017ECC.
0x8002580C() (line 34060): state 1: for each marked node (flag 0x04000000, behaviour 0x8002790C): if its handle 0x801F62C8 == 0x801F6258() (r13-21488, "the player") -> 0x80043D70(0) (hide now), else 0x8014F820(beh, 0x8002825C) (swap its step); r13-25952 := 0. State 2: same set: the player -> 0x80043D70(1), others -> step back to 0x8002790C; clear flag 0x04000000; r13-25952 := 0. State 3+: nothing. Init r13-25952 := 0 at 0x80021760. [inferred meaning: characters the close-up camera would see are hidden/frozen for the close-up and restored at its end.]

== 10. Player object flags ==
0x80122514(obj) (line 292942): sub = 0x80122274(obj) = *(obj+64) + u16(*(obj+64)+2176)*272; returns *(sub+244). 0x801223B8(obj, flags) (line 292855): *(sub+244) := flags; on a change of bit value 2 runs 0x801226C0 and more; on a change of bit value 4 with the new bit set: idx = 0x801226C0(obj) & 0xFFFF; if != 0xFFFF: zeroes 3 floats at *(obj+340) + idx*12. Stepper sets bit 4 at 0x80024E84, 0x800250D0, 0x800251E0, 0x800253C4, 0x80025510 (every step but the words step), the words hold sets it too (0x80042AC0). Who clears it: not searched. [meaning of bit 4 inferred: stop the object's motion; docs/coop-no-pause.md warns clearing it gave attack stances.]

== 11. Prompt ask 0x80117404(button, id, script, zone script, prio, slot, flag) (line 281610) ==
Slots 0x80335A38 + slot*28. Slot 2/4 path (0x8011745C-801175D0): zone script must be >= 0; prio must be > slot's +0 (else -1); scans the room's regions (0x80155130(2): +180 list, +176 count, 116 bytes each; +104 bit0; shape +108 0 sphere/1 box): if a region's enter/stay script (+58/+62 sphere, +60/+64 box) == zone script or == r13-23228 (0x8061FA64 running script), and its +110 >= 90, and slot == 2 -> recurse with slot 4. Then writes +12 script, +4 button, +8 id, +20 slot, +24 flag (sth); does NOT write +0 priority in this path; returns the slot. Slot 0/1/3/5 path (0x801175D4): prio must be >= +0; writes +0 prio too. Stepper asks (all button 2, script -2, prio 50, slot r30, flag 1): id 34 at 0x80025048, 0x8002533C; id 7 at 0x80025014, 0x8002510C; id 19 at 0x80025400. The press clears: 0x80117360(1) (line 281569): memset 28 bytes of the shown record 0x80335AE0 (0x80003100), +8 := -1, +4 := -1, +20 := -1; if the shown slot != -1 and that slot's +8 != -1: 0x80116E70(slot, 0). 0x801179BC() = shown +20 (slot); 0x801179CC() = shown +8 (id); 0x801179DC stores r13-24040 (0x8061F638).

0x80024CDC: only for script == -2; slot 2 -> +64 := 0, slot 4 -> +64 := 1; 0x801DC80C(0x8023E570, script, index) [debug print]; 0x80024B24(0). No bl caller found by grep (reached by pointer; the host calls it the close-up cancel at 0x80024CDC). 0x80024B24: kind 1: +0 := 0; if +8 == 2: 0x80024384 (close words). Kind 2/3: +0 := 0; if +8 > 0: (if +8 == 2: 0x80024384); 0x80199EA0(1); 0x801ECCEC(); snap if !(n&1); 0x80043C88(1) if n&4; 0x8002576C(). If rerun: 0x80163C6C(+4 script, 100, -1) and 0x801642D4(script, 0, 0). Called from the press (stick, 0x8000702C) and 0x80024D64.

== 13. Input channel / focus the close-up uses ==
The stepper registers no channel of its own. Its words window takes r13-25960 and the focus r13-23556 (0x8061F81C; copied to r13-23552 by the pad update [docs words.md]); its find window does the same plus handler 0x80024434, data 0x8030A6E8, mask 3. The hold masks channels r13-23060/-23052 (+16 := 0) and opens r13-23056, and sets busy bit 2 (r13-23344). Words hold adds busy 8, prompt flag 32 (r13-24048), r13-25696, r13-23040; find hold adds busy 16, prompt flag 2, r13-25932.

== 14. Per-player copy: the complete state list (what a second close-up needs its own of) ==
Game-side, per player: look record 0x80307108 (68 B); pending record 0x803090C4 (32 B) [docs]; prompt slots 2 and 4 and the shown record 0x80335A38..0x80335AFB; r13 words -23344 busy, -25696 hold word, -24048 prompt flags, -25960/-25948/-25944/-25940/-25932 window set, -25952 hide state, -25688 show flag (+ -26272 after 0x80043CCC), -23556/-23552 focus, -23564, -23060/-23052/-23056 channel masks (+16) and the control data's +0 and +8 bit 0x400, 0x8025494C reset block; camera: r13-26864 index, r13-21700 depth, stack 0x806038E8 (5 x 20 B), records TB[10] 0x806036C8 / TA[10] 0x80603068 (+48 blend, whole record from 0x801EE8E0), shown records 0x806037D8/0x80603860 (+48, +64, +112), room record 2 via the words hold; window table entry in 0x80588150 (32 B) for the words/find window; words block 0x8023E458 (+12/+44/+76), find blocks 0x8023E4B8 (+12, +44) and 0x8030A6E8 (28 B); the player object's flag word (sub+244) bit 4; nodes' object flag 0x04000000 and behaviour step pointers (0x8002790C <-> 0x8002825C) set by 0x800255A4/0x8002580C; the thing's node +12/+33 via 0x801F9DE8 and the events 71, 46, 47, 190 it sends. Host already copies: PROMPT_LOOKS (g_coop_p2_looks, hle.c 13973-13985), the camera set (coop_camera_set_io), and intercepts COOP_PLAY_HOLD (hle.c 12832-12900) and COOP_SHOW_MAIN (hle.c 16055-16090).

=== 4. WHAT A SECOND WINDOW / SECOND FIND WOULD NEED (from the above; the list, not a design) ===
Per window: its channel (any of the 20 slots: the game makes them freely; or the slot in the other player's input copy), its own copies of r13-25960/-25944/-25948/-25940 and the focus pair r13-23556/-23552 (already per player in pad_globals), its own 96-byte bindings table (0x8023E458 is written per open at +12/+44/+76 and per frame at each row's +28 by the dispatcher), its own element (the pool has 10; 0x801DB374 just takes a free one), a per-frame 0x8013E160(channel) call (0x80024AF0 steps only the global's), and the hold's per-player words (r13-25696, busy -23344 bits 8/16, -24048 bits 32/2, -25932, player object flag 4, 0x80199EA0/0x8019C088, the camera 0x801EC3A0/0x801EEB58(2)/0x80134D48) which coop_words_hold/coop_find_hold already split. Per find additionally: the 28-byte record 0x8030A6E8 (+0 +4 +8 +16 +20 +24), the 64-byte bindings 0x8023E4B8 (+12 record, +44 element), the countdown r13-25948, the thing's object/drawable marks (object+592/+596/+720, drawable+24/+28 = 0x801FA400/0x801FA44C) and the two found objects hle.c tracks, and the three event sends 46/47/190 to record+8 plus 0x801642D4(script, 0, id). Hazard [read]: all three close paths (0x80024220, 0x80024434, 0x80024920) close *(r13-25960) and read r13-25944/-25948 from the globals, so the host must swap the globals around each window's step, as the Tome copy does per mode (kTwWords), rather than give a channel its own copy.

# Round 2 (2026-11-10 night): the design step A was built from, and step B ready to build

Round 2 ran four readers on the seven given points, a design, two refuters and one revision; the revised design is
below with the two reviews that followed it. Its remaining findings (the deferred close script waiting on player 1's
words; the let-go's event broadcast; a wall-clock frame key; a ninth state slot) were fixed in the build of step A
(commit 5330b64: `close_script` consumed at the controller before any keep, the busy word zeroed round the let-go,
`g_coop_turn_ran`, `g_coop_events_p1` reused). Step A is script-verified both ways (logs/run-20261110-190829-own-words-A,
-191353-own-words-C).

## Round 2's design (step A's basis)

ROUND 3 DESIGN (read only; nothing changed). Sources this pass [read]: docs/coop-level3-design.md 1-180 (round 1, both reviews, the seven given points, the two approaches), hle.c at HEAD+uncommitted: coop_player_turn 18268-18520, coop_prompt_look 14796-14960, coop_words_hold 17017-17074, coop_busy_ask 17218-17233, coop_node_held_ask 17241-17260, CoopHoldKept/coop_hold_open/shut 17285-17314, hle_coop_controller 17759-17872, coop_run_script 16380-16480 + coop_p2_script_mark/is 16177-16195, coop_events 19640-19690 + coop_events_words 19247-19265, hle_words_layout 16679-16730, the dispatcher 5454-5488, coop_way_out_p1/p2_up 14685-14694, coop_player1_mid_use 16821-16835, coop_script_hold_go/coop_their_close_up/coop_no_pause 16913-16960, hle_message_open 16763-16780, coop_close_up_start 16497-16516, coop_use_start 15931-15981, coop_be_player2 12147-12165, kTwWords/tw_words_io 13004-13040, coop_camera_set_io/coop_camera_p2_in/coop_cameras_in 14152-14225, coop_script_cameras 16204-16262, coop_player_held 17179-17187, tome_copy_hook_off 23746-23770, hook registration 24505-24525; asm.txt 33221-33233 (0x80024AF0), 32717-32740 (0x80024310), 12117-12120 (ed12's open at 0x80010134, lr 0x80010138 = EXAMINE_OPENS), 311899-311931 (0x80134D48), 482391 (0x801DB374's bl 0x801DB088 at 0x801DB538); the kit: `ed examine 182`, `ed examine 183`, `ed script 1298`; saves/ (slot1, slot2 and named states exist; no slot7). [read] = seen; [inf] = inferred.

WHAT CHANGES FROM ROUND 2 (each refuted finding, and the fix). The one structural change: PLAYER 2's HOLD IS NEVER IN THE GAME'S MEMORY. Round 2 let 0x80042A8C run natively for player 2 inside their calls with a four-word hold set swapped in; every refutation but one came from that: (R0-1) the turn-level restore of p1's hold words dropped coop_prompt_look's scene_began/asked keeps (14914-14922) -> no turn-level swap at all now, and no hold words in the set; (R1-1) the native close's 0x80134D48(r13-26524 = player 2) swung player 1's camera -> the close never runs natively; (R1-3) 0x801556CC's event 102 from the tick hit coop_events' stale-buffer load -> no 0x801556CC call from the tick but COOP_PLAY_HOLD's 2-bit (filtered as today, 19657-19668), and g_coop_events_own set for the tick; (R0-3) the script mark leaked onto player 1's close of the same script -> no mark; the close's script is DEFERRED to the existing tail-call route (g_coop_use_script at player 1's controller), so nothing runs a script inside the tick (given 3) and coop_run_script's 16420-16435 rule never sees a marked 1298; (R0-2) the open with .up false went into player 1's set -> an EMPTY player-2 words set (-25960 = 0) is in memory for EVERY look and press of theirs (swapped round COOP_SCAN + the press inside coop_prompt_look), so 0x80024310's unguarded store (asm 0x80024338-0x80024350) always lands in theirs and window_began is keyed on THEIR -25960; (R1-2) g_hle_passthrough is set at the END of coop_words_tick and the new coop_words_hold branch goes AFTER 17018; (LOW stale pad) the tick runs COOP_PAD_UPDATE in their copy when the turn did not run this frame. The second review's CONFIRMED items (addresses, 0x8013E160's per-copy cleanness, the element x arithmetic 4096 - w/2, the dispatcher's order, the exemption's recognisability, no double step/fire, the kit's switches) are kept as the foundation.

THE RULE. Player 1's objects ARE the game's memory (approach 1, untouched). Player 2's words window is a SMALL HOST-KEPT SET (approach 2 shrunk: ~120 B, no RAM copy, no send-back) that enters memory only round player 2's own calls: (a) the look's COOP_SCAN + kind-1 press inside coop_prompt_look (already inside the turn, their input copy/identity/zones/states/cameras(2)/slots/looks all in, 14815-14872), and (b) a new per-frame tick at 0x80024AF0 keyed on lr 0x80008500 that steps THEIR window channel in THEIR input copy. Player 2's HOLD is ANSWERED, not copied: coop_words_hold handles their open/close itself (as its 'theirs' branch 17019-17034 does today) with exactly today's physical effect in their copy (COOP_PLAY_HOLD(0)/(1) guest-called with the busy word put back, as 14901-14904 and 18328-18337 do today) and the camera calls in their set, and keeps bits 8|2 host-side only (g_coop_p2_words.held). So the three asks of given 1 (coop_busy_ask 17218 at 0x80155738, the turn's refusal 18306, coop_node_held_ask 17241) see player 1's word only and need NO change; the event 102 filter needs no change; -24048 (given 7) is never touched for player 2 (their prompt is host-chosen, forced -1 while .up); -23040 (zones) is 0 in memory only round their COOP_SCAN while .up, inside the 2060 save/put-back coop_prompt_look already does (14812-14813/14888), mirroring the game's zones-off while words are up and keeping 0x80024310's unguarded store from a second zone's ed12.

PER OBJECT.
(1) Words window: HYBRID. Copied (host-kept, swapped round their calls): CoopWordsOwn = {-25960 window, -25944 script, -25948 find countdown, -25940 closed-with, -25952 hide state, byte -25932, the 96-byte bindings 0x8023E458 (+12/+44/+76 element, rows' +20/+24/+28 counters)} = kTwWords + 25952 + 25932 + the whole record, layered under the Tome's mode axis (tw_words_io 13031-13040 is the template). Already per player, left where it is: the channel slot (CoopPlayerState.input, 0x8013DB3C takes the slot in the copy in), focus pair -23556/-23552 and taken -23564 (pad_globals; 0x8013DB1C/0x8013DB94/0x8013E154 write only these). Shared on purpose: the element pool 0x805F87D0 (player 2's element +8 += 4096; view 1 hide_hud=true brings back x > 1 NDC, interp.cpp 682-690, as COOP_PROMPT_AT proves). Answered as today for player 1: coop_words_hold's free path (17044-17071), coop_busy_ask p1_free, hle_message_open. Player 1's tick = the game's 0x80024AF0 (steps -25960 = player 1's); player 2's tick = the new intercept's 0x8013E160(their channel) with their copy+set in, then passthrough.
(2) Busy word and hold: ANSWERED (host), for both players. Player 1's: coop_words_hold free/coop_p1_close_up/coop_play_hold's lr test/coop_hold_open-shut round the turn: UNCHANGED. Player 2's: coop_words_hold's new own branch (A4) does channels-in-their-copy + camera-in-their-set + host bits; 0x80042A8C never runs natively for them; 0x801556CC/0x80117260/0x8019C088/-25696/object flag 4/0x80134D48 are not run (today's theirs branch skips the same ones [read 17019-17034]). Player 2 stands by their control channels' +16 = 0x0FFFFFFF in their copy (0x80199F98 at 0x80045D30 in their controller steps nothing) and by their window's focus in their pad_globals; player 1 stands by the game's window lock. Neither player's bit is in the other's word, so neither gate holds the other.
(3) Close-up stepper (step B): COPIED round their calls, all of it already exists but one word: PROMPT_LOOKS -> g_coop_p2_looks, PROMPT_SLOTS -> g_coop_prompt_slots, camera set+depth+index -> g_coop_cameras.p2 via coop_cameras_in(2), plus NEW the pending look 0x803090C4 (32 B, 0 hle.c occurrences) joining CoopWordsOwn; the stepper's hold 0x80199EA0(0) at 0x80024E8C is answered by coop_play_hold's new own branch (channels in their copy, bit 2 host-side, busy put back), the DETAIL/RETURN/PICK UP presses become guest calls of 0x80006EBC(slot6+4, 0) inside the look (script -2 is synchronous until its own RETURN script, which is deferred like the words' close), 0x800255A4/0x8002576C/0x8002580C intercepted to RETURN THE GAME'S COUNT but put hit objects into g_coop_unseen[owner's view] (given 6), their close-up camera = record 10 in THEIR set stepped by coop_camera_step without the `lent` skip and read by hle_coop_player2_camera; the lend and g_coop_screen stay for the other paths until F.

MEMORY, EXACTLY (new host state, hle.c near kTwWords 13004): `typedef struct { bool up; u32 channel; s32 script; u32 held; /* host-only bits 8|2 while up, never written to memory */ u32 words[5]; /* r13-25960 -25944 -25948 -25940 -25952 */ u8 find_byte; /* r13-25932 */ u8 record[96]; /* 0x8023E458 */ bool made; } CoopWordsOwn; static CoopWordsOwn g_coop_p2_words; static bool g_coop_p2_words_tick; static unsigned long long g_coop_turn_frame;` and `static bool coop_p2_words_call(void) { return own_words && (g_coop_p2_words_tick || (g_coop_prompt_look && g_coop_turn)); }` where own_words = ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS set (static int, read once). io: `coop_words_own_io(cpu, w, save)`: five words at r13-k (k = 25960, 25944, 25948, 25940, 25952), the byte at r13-25932, coop_bytes(0x8023E458, record, 96). No hold words, no -24048, no -23040, no camera words: all those are answered.

KEPT INTACT (review 1's confirmed paths), how: 'player 1 words with player 2 free': coop_words_hold free path, coop_busy_ask p1_free, busy_out untouched; nothing of player 2's ever enters the busy word. ### Step A as designed

STEP A: player 2 reads kind-1 words of their own (a zone's room words, ed12, or an EXAMINE's plain words, ed220) while player 1 reads words, each half its own window, each B its own, neither standing for the other. Switch ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS=1 (testing) until played; unset = today's path byte for byte. Line numbers hle.c HEAD+uncommitted.

A1. What the guest call runs [read asm 321377-321540, 32657-32716]: 0x8013E160 steps the rows when slot +16 == 0 and the channel is the focus (their -23552, or -23552 == 0); stick rows scroll the element; the A/B row -> 0x80024220: element free (0x801DA4D8), 0x8013E154, 0x8013DB94 (slot +28 |= 1, their -23556 := 0), -25960 := 0, 0x80042A8C(0) lr WORDS_HOLD_CLOSES -> A4's own branch (COOP_PLAY_HOLD(1) in their copy, busy kept, held := 0), -25940 := button, then 0x801642D4(script, 0, 0) lr WINDOW_CLOSE_RAN -> coop_run_script -> A7 defers it. The freed slot (+28 bit 0) is reclaimed by their next pad update (0x8013E518 at 18373 or here, asm 321786-321793). tome_copy_hook_off 23752-23756: add OFF(coop_words_tick).

### Later steps as designed then

- STEP B (the close-up stepper per player, kinds 2/3 off the lend): CoopWordsOwn gains the pending look 0x803090C4 (32 B); every press of theirs on a shown prompt whose script is -2 (B/DETAIL/RETURN/PICK UP) becomes a guest call of 0x80006EBC(slot6+4, 0) inside coop_prompt_look's swapped window (slots, looks, zones, states, cameras(2), their copy, identity, words set all in), replacing coop_close_up_start's tail call for close-ups; coop_play_hold (12846) gains an own branch for coop_p2_words_call() && lr in CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM..TO: COOP_PLAY_HOLD runs in their copy with the busy word put back and bit 2 kept in g_coop_p2_words.held (so coop_p2_holds/coop_busy never see it); the close-up camera is record 10 pushed by the stepper into THEIR set (cameras_in(2) is in), stepped every turn by coop_camera_step with the `lent` skip removed and the live-view carry's player-2 half moved into it (write g_coop_own_view into CAMERA_VIEW before CAMERA_STEP, keep the post-step save 14476), read by hle_coop_player2_camera; intercepts at 0x800255A4/0x8002576C/0x8002580C that run the game's test by guest call of itself and RETURN ITS COUNT (given 6) but put the hit objects into g_coop_unseen[owner's view] instead of 0x800338BC-marking them (owner = coop_p2_words_call() ? 2 : 1), g_coop_unseen -> [2][12], coop_found_object_draw marks out of the other view; the RETURN script (0x801642D4 at 0x80025194/0x8002558C with lr CLOSE_UP_END_RAN) is deferred like A7; player 2's DETAIL/RETURN/PICK UP prompts land in g_coop_prompt_slots (the stepper's 0x80117404 asks run inside the look) and show through coop_prompt_look's choice + coop_prompt_show; coop_camera_p2_in's rebuild (14196-14208) is suppressed while their set is not at rest (their stack depth > 0, .up, or a scene of theirs), keyed on the set copy rather than g_coop_screen/lent, and coop_camera_chosen's `lent` return (15338) becomes g_coop_set_in == 2. Playable: both players in ed219 close-ups (room 183's holes, L01/79-92: DETAIL -> words -> RETURN) at once, interleaved and reversed, neither frozen (assert -25952 == 0 in the game's and no 0x8002825C swap). Check: slot2.state in 183, ED_COOP_P2_PUT at CameraRgn03 and ED_PUT at CameraRgn05, b/DETAIL/ldown/b/RETURN scripts for both, `ed timeline`, shots of each half's record-10 camera.
- STEP C (the find per player): the find record 0x8030A6E8 (28 B) and bindings 0x8023E4B8 (64 B) join CoopWordsOwn (-25948/-25932 already in it); coop_find_hold (17082) gains an own branch (COOP_PLAY_HOLD in their copy, bits 16 and -24048 bit 2 host-side, -25932 in their set); hle_coop_found's finder = coop_p2_words_call() ? 2 : (today's guesses); g_coop_found_objects -> [2 views][2] with out_of/until per owner; coop_found_object_draw picks g_coop_mark_view = the other view of the matched object's owner; NEW intercept at 0x800D8A88 (the kind-30 item's state-35 draw-pass step, r3 drawable, r4 node): for the OTHER owner's found drawable, save COOP_CAMERA 52 B, write that owner's eye (+0)/target (+12)/FOV (+48) from g_coop_own_view/g_coop_own_zoom (player 2) or the game's (player 1), guest-call 0x800D8A88 itself, restore, skip the game's run; the find's close 0x80024434 runs in the tick (events 47/190 via 0x801F59B0 are synchronous; the row's +36 script deferred like A7); coop_script_timers' host count (17999-18012) unchanged. Playable: player 1 opens the Tome while player 2 reads/examines and back; player 2's Start while player 1 reads; player 2's Tome item use (tome_copy_left) opening their words beside player 1's examine (the user plays this).
- STEP E (save states, room changes, cutscenes): coop_words_own_cancel (A9) made complete: 0x80024384 (the cancel's close) or 0x80024CDC guest-called with their copy + set + cameras(2) in, their stack popped to depth 0, the empty set remade; order at a room change: cancel player 2's (theirs in) before coop_zones_new drops their set, then the game's own path for player 1's; a cutscene start cancels both, each with its own in; the frontend's state save refused while g_coop_p2_words_tick or inside the look's press; tw_state_loaded clears player 2's set and frees nothing stale (the channel table entries and pool elements are the loaded state's). Playable: save/load mid-words for each player; a door by player 1 while player 2 reads (player 2 brought along, their words cancelled with a log); a scripted cutscene while both read.
- STEP F (cleanup, only after B-E play): delete coop_window_to_game, g_coop_p2_words_held and the let-go block 18328-18337, coop_prompt_look's old window_began branch, g_coop_words_theirs' words-of-theirs uses, coop_their_close_up's words case, coop_cameras_lend/lent for close-ups (kept only if the Tome's cast words still need it), camera.c 1621-1663 together with hle_coop_player1_camera/g_coop_p1_view/coop_player1_camera, coop_player1_turn's game/own swap, g_coop_screen's close-up uses (69 lines: by grep, one at a time, each with a run), coop_prompt_pressed's ownership rule; drop ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS (always on); update docs/game/systems/words.md, prompts.md, examine.md, camera.md, docs/coop-next-session.md, docs/coop-todo.md, docs/items-log.md with what the runs showed, and docs/coop-failed-attempts.md with any attempt that failed.

### Risks

- 1 (highest, step A): the turn-refused frames. Player 2's pad lives in g_coop_state[1].input and is read by COOP_PAD_UPDATE; the tick's own pad update when the turn did not run (A5, keyed on g_coop_turn_frame) is new behaviour [inf]: it decrements every slot's +16 in their copy and reads pad 2 through g_coop_pad_p2 once per frame as the turn would, but controls.cpp's held-over logic may see two reads in a frame if the turn runs after a refused tick in the same frame (the tick is at 0x800084FC, after the behaviour pass, so it cannot; verify with ED_COOP_LOG). If it misbehaves, drop it: the cost is only that player 2 cannot scroll or close while player 1's fn91 busy bit alone or both scenes refuse the turn (today's path has the same hole through coop_menu_input only partly).
- 2 (step A): the deferred close script (A7) runs by coop_use_start's tail call at the controller with the LEND on for its duration (15960): for 1298 that is a few frames of camera_set(Camera13)+ease in player 2's set and an EXAMINE ask into their slots; but a close script that starts a scene (fn22) or asks a room (fn66) runs as today's uses do, with their bookkeeping, which is the confirmed path; a close script of theirs that waits (fn17 walk, a cinematic) is also today's route. The new exposure is only ordering: the script runs one controller later than the game would run it (one frame).
- 3 (step A): 0x80024310 has no guard on -25960 (asm 0x80024338-0x80024350): a second window of theirs opened while .up (a zone's ed12 of a zone they stand in; the press route is gated) would overwrite their -25960 and leak the first channel and element. Mitigated by -23040 := 0 round their COOP_SCAN while .up (the game's zones-off while words are up) [inf that the zone natives honour -23040 through 0x8019C088's word: `ed fn 0x8019C088` before coding]; log any open while .up.
- 4 (step A): the element pool (10 shared elements, 0x801DA600 frees only at alpha 0): two words windows + both prompts + the find's second element + fading ones; 0x801DB374 with none free prints and the window has no text. Count elements in the first runs (`[trace] words` + the pool walk); free fading ones early if seen.
- 5 (step A): the Tome copy and the game-mode Tome. The copy's frames pass 0x800084FC (guarded by !g_tome_copy_turn and OFF(coop_words_tick)) and draw the pool, so player 2's element at x+4096 is drawn into the Tome frame (fitted into a half: off-screen, but shared pool slots); the game-mode Tome stops the world frame so player 2's words neither tick nor close until it shuts (step D). Until D: test with no Tome while words are up.
- 6 (step A): coop_words_back at 14888 puts the whole 2060 block back AFTER their window was made in the scan, which includes the pad_globals' focus pair and -23564; A3(c) re-writes those three from values read before coop_words_back. Any other word the open wrote inside the block is lost by design (today's behaviour for the scan): the hold's effects are outside it or in their copy; verify with ED_COOP_LOG that -23556 == channel in their copy at the next turn (the turn's focus log at 18360-18363 names it).
- 7 (step A): the mask-loop exemption keeps their four slots' +16 while .up; after the close (.up false) the next turn copies the game's masks over their control channels: free if player 1 is free or in words (free path) or a close-up (coop_hold_open zeroed the game's for the turn), as today; held only in today's held cases. And their window slot: freed by their own pad update (+28 bit 0) before the loop can mark it 0xFFFFFFFE, provided the pad update at 18373 runs before 0x8013DB3C's next take (it does: pad update 18373, look 18418).
- 8 (step B): the hide-others intercepts must return the game's count (cut vs blend) and must cover the three callers of 0x800255A4 (0x80017E64, 0x80024F80, 0x800252B4) and four of 0x8002576C; a missed caller freezes a character or turns a cut into a blend. Verify with `ed fn` and a run with player 1's close-up alone first.
- 9 (step B): coop_camera_p2_in's rebuild under a close-up of theirs and the many `lent` readers (coop_camera_chosen 15338, coop_script_cameras 16225, cutscene 22847, coop_scan_player1 17739/17745, coop_menu_input 23278/23459, camera.c 1621): each needs 'player 2's close-up is on' re-answered by g_coop_p2_words.up/held and g_coop_set_in; done one reader at a time with a run each.
- 10 (step C): player 2's node's motion step 0x800276C4 reads -25932 (player 1's in memory) and 0x80119B94's draw list count r13-23956 is player 1's: the find's thing registers in the main loop (fine) but the find byte's behaviour for their node is not read; and 0x800D8A88's intercept assumes the thing is world-space eased toward a view-derived goal (read in the find-thing dossier) and that g_coop_own_view is fresh (refreshed only when the turn runs).
- 11 (all steps): save states taken with player 2's words up hold a channel slot and a pool element only the host knows about; until step E, save only with both idle, and refuse the frontend's save inside the tick/press.
- 12 (step A, low): sound/ambience zones (0x80134D48 at the words' close) are not run for player 2's close, as today's theirs path; a two-part sound or an ambience switch that the game would make at their position does not happen. Acceptable; log it.

### The two reviews of it (fixed in the build)

- REFUTED (regression on the design's own target scenario, 'player 2 reads kind-1 words while player 1 examines a room 183 hole', and on today's route for the same words). A7 defers player 2's close script through g_coop_use_script (hle.c:14994) with g_coop_use_prompt = -1, consumed at hle_coop_controller 17895-17905. [read 17895-17897] `if (g_coop_use_script != 0 && coop_player1_mid_use(cpu)) { coop_press_keep(&g_coop_p2_kept, 2, g_coop_use_prompt, g_coop_use_script, ...); g_coop_use_script = 0; }` -- coop_player1_mid_use (16832-16835) is true while player 1 is in a close-up (coop_p1_in_close_up: g_coop_p1_look and not a bare scene), has a found word pending, g_coop_find_free, or r13-26188 != r13-26240 (a Tome mode change pending). The deferred script becomes a kept press (prompt -1, script 1298). [read 18446-18475] coop_player_turn's kept block compares `g_coop_prompt != kept->prompt || g_coop_prompt_script != kept->script`: after the close g_coop_prompt is 6/-2 (the statue's EXAMINE) or -1/0 (coop_prompt_look 14935-14945 sets the script only with a chosen slot), never (-1, 1298), so `++kept->gone >= COOP_PRESS_GONE` (6, 16846) drops it: 'they're off that prompt'. The close script never runs. Today's route [read coop_run_script 16420-16435]: 0x80024220 -> 0x801642D4 lr WINDOW_CLOSE_RAN, `coop_p2_script_is(script) && !g_coop_use.active && !g_coop_turn && !g_coop_prompt_look` -> coop_use_start(script, true) at once, with no coop_player1_mid_use gate; so today 1298's L15 (camera_set Camera13, ease, the ed220 EXAMINE ask) runs while player 1 is in their hole close-up, and under the design it is lost. For kind-1 words whose close script sets a flag or gives a thing, the loss is the whole effect. The design's A7 text ('runs by today's tail-call route ... 18419-18420's rule') names the B block's route but not its consumer's 17895 gate.
- CONFIRMED (same consumer, the design's run 1 'both reading'): [read 17900] `if (g_coop_use_script != 0 && !g_coop_words_free)` -- the deferred close script waits for player 1's words to close. In run 1 player 2's close at ~7186 precedes player 1's B at 7486, so A10's expectation '~7186 ... then `script 1298 runs for player 2` (the use)' cannot appear before 7486. While it waits: (a) any B of player 2's on a prompt (18428-18444, .up is false now) does `g_coop_use_script = g_coop_prompt_script` and overwrites the pending close script (lost); (b) a kept press of theirs never goes in (18456: `g_coop_use_script != 0 -> kept->free = 0`). Today's route ran the close script at the close, unconditionally.
- DIFFERENCE FROM TODAY [read], consequence [inf]: A5's close runs A4's COOP_PLAY_HOLD(1) in the TICK (0x800084FC, outside hle_coop_controller), where the game's busy word is real. 0x80199EA0(1) -> 0x801556CC(2, 0) (asm 415409-415452): new word = old & ~2; if nonzero, 0x801F5620(102, r13-21496 = player 1 in the tick) broadcasts to every node but player 1's. Today's let-go 18328-18337 runs INSIDE coop_player_turn, after hle_coop_controller's coop_hold_open (17810 for player 1's look, 17820 p2 hold, 17860 room) or busy_out (17838-17845) zeroed the word, so (2,0) left 0 and fired nothing. Under the design, player 1's words from a fn91 script (busy 1, L01/103's door), player 1's bare scene (32|2) or a close-up with fn91 (2|1) leave the word nonzero at player 2's close -> event 102 from player 1 to player 2's node. coop_events (kind 2, 0x80058DD8) has no 102 filter: grep '102u' hits only coop_grab_events 19657. What player 2's behaviour does with a hold event from the other player is not read here; the design's claim that this (2,0) 'is the one today's 18328-18337 fire' is false in exactly the cases where the word is nonzero.
- HAZARD [read, arithmetic]: coop_state (12077-12087) calls coop_breath (12035-12076), which keys 8 slots by the CoopPlayerState pointer. Distinct pointers today: g_coop_state[0], g_coop_state[1] (18323/18325/18503/18510), g_coop_events_p1 (19252/19258), g_coop_p1_free (17795), coop_player1_turn's `game` and `own` (17360-17396), coop_character_step's static p1 (21902/21909) = 7. A5's `static CoopPlayerState p1` in coop_words_tick is the 8th: the array is then full. A9's cancel helper ('load their copy + set') with a static of its own would be the 9th: coop_breath's save finds no slot and returns, and its load with slot < 0 writes COOP_BREATH_FULL into r13-COOP_BREATH (12070-12073): player 1's breath reset to full at every cancel. Reuse the tick's static or g_coop_events_p1.
- LOW [read + inf]: in a refused frame the refusal branch calls coop_p2_press_held (18249-18266: controls_read_player2), and A5's tick then guest-calls COOP_PAD_UPDATE with g_coop_pad_p2 -> the PADRead hook (4085-4110) reads pad 2 again and sets g_coop_p2_buttons: two pad-2 reads in one frame, which the design's risk 1 said 'cannot' happen (it considered only the turn after the tick). controls.cpp's held-over logic is not read; verify.
- CONDITIONAL [read]: A3(a) forces r13-23040 := 0 before COOP_SCAN while .up (put back by coop_words_back). coop_prompt_look's scene_began keep (14914-14916) writes `zones_on` read AFTER the scan (14917) into the game's -23040 after coop_words_back. If a scene of player 2's begins in such a scan (possible only if the zone natives do not honour -23040 through 0x8019C088, the design's own unverified [inf]), the game's zones word becomes 0 for player 1 until something sets it: player 1's zones off. Verify `ed fn 0x8019C088` before relying on the zones-off mitigation (design risk 3).
- DESIGN FACTS THAT HOLD [read]: the kind-1 stepper path 0x80024DF4-0x80024E3C does only look+0 := 0, 0x801DB374(words), 0x80024310(element, look+4, 0): no 0x80199EA0, no camera push (those are the kind 2/4 paths at 0x80024E44+), so A3(b)'s press has no hidden hold. 0x80042A8C has exactly two bl callers (asm 32697 = 0x800242C0 lr WORDS_HOLD_CLOSES, 32739 = 0x80024368 lr WORDS_HOLD_OPENS), so A4's early `return` with passthrough for other lrs never runs the hold natively. The tick's key is right: bl 0x80024AF0 at 0x800084FC, next instruction 0x80008500 (asm 4168-4169). coop_window_to_game (19320-19350) is dead on the new path and nothing else moves the channel into the game's input. The turn's B block (18428-18444) is already fenced by A3(d)'s g_coop_prompt = -1 while .up, so a kind-1 press does not double-fire through g_coop_use_script = -2. coop_p2_press_held's keep is fenced the same way (g_coop_prompt >= 0). The two g_coop_words_theirs readers the design converts (18259 coop_p2_press_held, 23544 tome_copy_p1_left's p2_scene_alone) are the only ones meaning 'player 2's words are up' whose source changes; coop_way_out_p1_up (14685-14688), coop_scene_use_over (14681-14683) and coop_p1_close_up's bit-8 exclusion (16880-16882) read the game's -25960/-23344, which the new path never touches.

- REFUTED [read] A5's frame key is not a game-frame key. `cpu->timebase` advances only at VI retrace pops (runtime/host/interrupt.c 511-514: `while (dol_vi_clock_pop_retrace(...)) cpu->timebase += ticks`), and the retraces are WALL-CLOCK paced by default (interrupt.c 196 `wall_clock = true`; 432-487: vi_units = elapsed wall ns * 3 / 50 -> 60 Hz). So `cpu->timebase / 675000ull` is a 60 Hz wall counter independent of the game's frame routine: whenever the host runs below 60 fps (hle.c 18427 comment 'at 8 fps'; failed-attempts.md 'timeout 2400 kills runs'), one or more retraces pop between the turn (0x800084AC, asm 4148) and the tick (0x800084FC, asm 4168) of ONE game frame, `g_coop_turn_frame != cpu->timebase / 675000ull` is true, and the tick runs a second COOP_PAD_UPDATE in the same game frame in their copy: every slot's +16 decremented twice (0x8013E518 asm 0x8013E7E4-0x8013E7F4), -23592 decremented twice, and a second controls_read_player2 (hle.c 4069-4075) that controls.cpp's held-over logic sees. This refutes the design's 'the tick is at 0x800084FC, after the behaviour pass, so it cannot [see two reads]'. Fix: a bool the turn sets at 18319 and the tick clears (or a hook on the game's own frame boundary), never the timebase.
- REFUTED [read] A7/A10/Risk 2: the deferred close script does not run 'one controller later'. The ONLY consumer of g_coop_use_script is hle_coop_controller 17900: `if (g_coop_use_script != 0 && !g_coop_words_free)` ('what player 2 pressed for waits for player 1's words to close'); g_coop_words_free is set by coop_words_hold's free path at 17045 for player 1's kind-1 words and cleared at 17060 at their close. In A10 run 1 player 2's B closes at ~7186 while player 1 reads until ~7486, so `script 1298 runs for player 2` cannot appear before player 1's B at 7486: the expected log order ('closed with script 1298 ... then script 1298 runs for player 2') is wrong for the both-reading case the step is named for. Also 17896-17898: if coop_player1_mid_use(cpu) (player 1 in a close-up, the design's third play test 'player 2 reading while player 1 examines a room 183 hole') the script goes to coop_press_keep(&g_coop_p2_kept, 2, g_coop_use_prompt = -1, script) and g_coop_use_script := 0; the kept block 18446-18460 then sees g_coop_prompt_script (0) != kept->script (1298) -> kept->gone++ -> coop_press_drop after COOP_PRESS_GONE: the close script is DROPPED [inf: combination of two read paths]. For 1298 the loss is Camera13+ease+ed220; for a close script with world effects (a flag, a scene) it is a lost script. A7 needs its own consumer (or the 17900 rule must exempt a close script of theirs).
- REFUTED [read] A10's kit check 'no `[guest-call] ... stopped`' is not runnable as described: main.c 104-108 prints the ED_GUEST_CALL_LOG line only for the first 40 guest calls of the run (`static unsigned shown; if (shown++ < 40u)`), and the turn makes 5+ guest calls per frame (COOP_CHARACTER_INFO, COOP_SCAN, COOP_PAD_UPDATE, COOP_THREAT_LOOK, COOP_CONTROLLER...), so the log is exhausted long before frame 6500; the unconditional 'stopped' line (main.c 102-103) needs ED_COOP_TOME_COPY=1, not ED_GUEST_CALL_LOG. Add ED_COOP_TOME_COPY=1 to the command line or a counter.
- MINOR [read] A1/A9 name tw_state_loaded (hle.c 13185) as a call site, but it is `static void tw_state_loaded(void)` with no CPUState* (called once, at 18042); coop_words_own_empty(cpu, ...) and coop_words_own_cancel(cpu) must go at its caller (18042). And g_coop_events_own is declared at 18707, after coop_prompt_look (14796) and coop_words_hold (17017); coop_words_tick (A5) uses it with coop_state/g_coop_state (11798-12087), so it must sit after 18707 or forward-declare. Compile order only.

No hook exists on 0x80024AF0 or 0x8013E160 (grep hle.c: 0 hits), so the registration pattern of 24511-24515 (`hle_dispatch_entry`, `->intercept == NULL && ->notify == NULL`) applies; tome_copy_hook_off takes an HleHandler (23746-23750) so OFF(coop_words_tick) fits. # Step B, the close-up per player: design (2026-11-10 night), READY TO BUILD with two corrections

Four readers (the stepper's press path, the record-10 camera without the lend, the hide-others calls, today's close-up
state), a design, two refuters, one revision, two more refuters. The revised design is below. Its final reviews confirm
it line by line except for TWO corrections, which the build must apply:

1. **Do not add player 2's own close-up to `coop_scene_set`'s "other's close-up" test** (the design's B11): with it, player
   1's own scene ending while player 2's own close-up is held takes the kept-end path, writes the game's (player 1's)
   control channel counts back held with the busy word 0 and no flag set, and nothing lets player 1 go. Nothing of
   player 2's own close-up is in the game's word or channels, so the scene's end needs no keeping for it.
2. **The hide-others host walk's model test reads the OBJECT's +580** (`mem_read32(obj + 580)`, 0x80117C64 is
   `lwz r3,580(r3)`), not the node's +20 state: the design's walk had the wrong operand.

Also: the design lists 0x80024BC8 (the cancel's let-go) among the sites the `coop_play_hold` own branch covers; it is
outside the stepper's lr range, runs natively in their copy with the busy word zeroed by the cancel, and is harmless;
the cancel clears `.held` itself.

The switch: `ED_COOP_OWN_CLOSEUP=1`, only together with `ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS=1`.

## The design

STEP B DESIGN, ROUND 2 (read-only pass; hle.c = working tree = HEAD 5330b64, hle.c unmodified in git; camera.c, settings.c, main.c, asm.txt read; `ed room 183`, `ed examine 183`, `ed test --list` run). [read] = seen this pass in code/asm/kit; [inf] = inferred. Every REVIEW 0 / REVIEW 1 finding is answered under FINDINGS ANSWERED at the end; the body is written with the fixes in.

THE RULE (round 3, kept): player 1's close-up IS the game's memory. Player 2's kind-2 close-up (ed219: EXAMINE -> DETAIL -> words -> RETURN) is their host-kept tables, in memory only round their own calls: the press inside coop_prompt_look (slots in 14968 / saved 15056, looks 14971-14976 / 15049-15053, cameras_in(2) 14963 / back 15130, zones+states 14965-14966 / 15054-15055, their input copy and identity from the turn 18585-18668, CoopWordsOwn 14991-15000) and coop_words_tick (14452-14492), which step B widens to carry slots, looks, cameras(2) and identity while their close-up is up. Their hold is ANSWERED: COOP_PLAY_HOLD runs in their copy with the game's busy word put back, bit 2 kept host-side (g_coop_p2_look.held). Nothing of theirs enters r13-23344, -24048, -25688, -26272/-26276, the room nodes' 0x04000000 flag or their step pointers. coop_close_up_start, the lend, g_coop_screen, g_coop_assist_at, coop_player1_turn, camera.c 1619-1680 stay byte for byte for kind 3 (PICK UP), Tome item words, cast words, and the unset switch. Switch: ED_COOP_OWN_CLOSEUP=1, true only together with ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS=1 (a kind-2 DETAIL opens a words window; without the own words it would land in the game's -25960).

(1) WHAT JOINS THEIR SET; WHAT STAYS ANSWERED.
- Nothing new in GUEST memory. The pending look 0x803090C4 does NOT join (withdrawn, as the previous design said; the dossier's asm 19600-19680: ed221's fill and its copy into 0x80307108 are one native run inside their COOP_SCAN or their deferred RETURN use; never live across a call) [read in the dossier, not re-read].
- Already theirs round their calls [read]: PROMPT_LOOKS 0x80307108 (68 B) = g_coop_p2_looks; PROMPT_SLOTS 0x80335A38 (7x28, slot 6 = +168) = g_coop_prompt_slots; the camera set 0x80602B18..0x8060394C + depth r13-21700 + index r13-26864 = g_coop_cameras.p2 (coop_camera_set_io 14152-14168, depth/index host-endian memcpy at set+0xE34/+0xE38); CoopWordsOwn (14363-14372: words r13-25960/-25944/-25948/-25940/-25952, byte -25932, 96 B at 0x8023E458); their input + control + aim + globals + pad_globals = g_coop_state[1] (coop_state 12077-12087); identity: the turn's who[] (18660-18668: -26524/-21492/-21496) in the look, coop_be_player2 (12147-12165: those plus -21488/-21484 and the four casting words) in the tick.
- New HOST state (no memory): `static struct { bool up; /* their kind-2 close-up is open: from the open press's return to the RETURN press's return */ bool held; /* the stepper's bit 2 for them */ bool b_kept; s32 b_kept_id; /* a B of theirs on a prompt the look itself presses, seen in a refused turn: pressed at their next look (FINDING 4) */ u32 index, kind; } g_coop_p2_look;` `static bool g_coop_p2_prompt_own;` (the prompt coop_prompt_look chose is one the look itself presses); `static bool g_coop_p2_stepper_in;` (the stepper or the hide-others walk runs for player 2 by a host call: round the look's 0x80006EBC guest call, the tick's channel step while .up, the cancel's 0x80024B24; read by coop_camera_chosen, REVIEW 1 by_hash finding); `g_coop_show_kept[2]`, `g_coop_unseen[2][COOP_UNSEEN_MAX]`, `g_coop_unseen_out_of[2]` by owner (today's single ones 3250-3251 / 16280); `g_coop_unseen_look[COOP_UNSEEN_MAX]` (the hide-others hits of player 2's close-up, out of view 1); hle.h export `bool hle_coop_player2_close_up(void)` = coop_own_closeup() && g_coop_p2_look.up (FINDING 1). The close script reuses g_coop_p2_words.close_script / close_use (14367-14368) and the consumer 18138-18148; g_coop_use gains `u32 busy_before`.
- Stays ANSWERED (never written for them): busy r13-23344 (bit 2 -> .held); r13-24048 (their prompt is host-chosen: coop_prompt_look's choice + coop_prompt_show 18331-18364; 0x80117404 never reads -24048 [read in the dossier]); r13-25696; r13-23040 (zeroed round their scan only while their WORDS are up, step A 14999, unchanged: the game's stepper hold turns no zones off); r13-25688 (coop_show_asked swallows the hide, lets the show through: 16288-16300); r13-25952 (their words[4] in the set; the intercepts never write it); r13-26272/-26276 and the node flags/step pointers (no 0x8002580C work for them); the player object's flag 4 (r13-26524 is their object in the look and in the identity-swapped tick: the right object anyway).

(2) THE PRESS: a guest call of 0x80006EBC(slot6+4, 0) inside coop_prompt_look, for every press of theirs the look owns.
- Today [read 15016-15043]: outer gate `own && !g_coop_p2_words.up && !g_coop_screen && !coop_p2_scene_on() && !coop_door_turns(cpu) && !coop_left_behind(cpu)`, edge `b && !b_was` (b = own && g_coop_p2_buttons & 0x0200), loop i = 0..5 taking the FIRST slot with id != -1 && +16 == 0 and `break`ing after it whatever it is; inner `id == 6 && script == -2` && look kind (PROMPT_LOOKS + index*32, index = slot+20 == 2 ? 0 : 1) == 1 -> copy the slot's 28 B into PROMPT_SLOTS+168, guest-call COOP_PROMPT_USE, log. b_was = b after the block, every look (15043).

- Step B, which slot (FINDINGS 3 and 5): `if (coop_own_closeup() && g_coop_p2_look.up)`: scan ALL six for the stepper's own ask, `script == -2 && (id == 34 || id == 7)` (DETAIL, RETURN; 19 PICK UP joins in step C) with +16 == 0; nothing else is pressable while their close-up is up (the game's chooser 0x80117628 skips slots 1/3/5 under the hold through 0x80199F50/F74 [read in the memo's review 2 line 144], and the stepper's asks are the look's only steps). Else (not up): today's loop, first live slot, with the inner test `id == 6 && script == -2 && (kind == 1 || (coop_own_closeup() && kind == 2))`; kind 3 and ids with a script > 0 fall through to `break` as today (the B block / coop_close_up_start / coop_use_start route). A b_kept press presses only the slot whose id == b_kept_id (else dropped with a log). No second EXAMINE while .up: the id-6 branch is only reached when !.up (FINDING 5; the stepper keys on PROMPT_LOOKS+64 only, 0x80024D98-0x80024DA4 [read asm 33389-33392], so a second press 0 would push a second record-10 entry).
- The copy into slot 6 and the guest call are the same code as kind 1 (15031-15034). What runs inside, and where it lands [read asm 33383-33440, 33500-33530, 33600-33665 + the dossier]: 0x80006EBC fires iff extsh(r3) == slot6+4 && slot6+12 != 0; 0x80117360(1) clears their slot 6 and empties the source slot (coop_prompt_clear 18004-18010 passes through: !g_coop_p1_turn); PROMPT_LOOKS+64 := index; 0x80024D78 entry: r31 = 0x80307108, r30 = index ? 4 : 2, r7 = +8 before ++, kind = +0 (0x80024D98-0x80024DD0); kind 2: r7 == 0 -> 0x80024E6C (open: object flag 4 on r13-26524 = theirs; 0x80199EA0(0) at 0x80024E8C -> (3); push 0x801ECD68 + 0x801EEB58(10) + 0x801EB974 x2 + 0x801F0638/0x801F077C tests + 0x801EE8E0 record 10 -> their set (4); 0x80043C88(0) at 0x80024F64 if +24&4 -> coop_show_asked, theirs by identity; 0x800255A4(hash, +24&1, 0) at 0x80024F80 -> (5), `rlwinm. r0,r3,0,16,31` at 0x80024F84 clears the blend bit when nonzero; DETAIL 0x80117404(2, 34, -2, +4, 50, r30, 1) at 0x80025014 or RETURN at 0x80025000 with +8 := 3 when +16 < 0 -> their slots; coop_prompt_set 17785-17797 -> coop_prompt_player1s 17749-17760 returns false (!g_coop_screen) -> passthrough); r7 == 1 -> 0x80025050 (DETAIL: 0x801DB374 element (hle_words_layout +4096 at 16925: coop_p2_words_call() && lr 0x801DB53C, unchanged), 0x80024310(el, -2, 0) -> their words set, 0x80042A8C(1) -> coop_words_hold's own branch 17250-17271 (0x801EC3A0 + 0x801EEB58(2) in their set, COOP_PLAY_HOLD(0) in their copy, busy kept), re-push + record 10 at 0x80025080-88 -> their set; after the look own_began 15074-15087 sets g_coop_p2_words.up as for kind 1); r7 == 3 -> 0x80025114 (RETURN, the close: (7)). Then 0x8013E154 -> their -23564 (lost to coop_words_back 15073; harmless: the controller steps nothing under the hold).

Press 0 leaves +0 = 2, +8 = 1 (or 3); the close zeroes +0 at 0x8002517C [read asm 33649]; ed219's re-copy at the RETURN script leaves +0 = 2, +8 = 0 -> not up.

The game's own dispatch of 0x80006EBC (lr COOP_PROMPT_DISPATCH 0x8013E334) reads the GAME's slot 6 = player 1's: no double fire; coop_prompt_pressed 17222-17241 is silent for the guest call's lr 0x0BADF010 [read].
