Not up: today's loop.

(3) THE HOLD: coop_play_hold's own branch, FIRST (before the scene rule 12850 whose `theirs = coop_use_theirs() || g_coop_screen` is false on this route, and before the p1_look rule 12881 which the TICK's call (g_coop_turn false, identity swapped to p2 by coop_be_player2: r13-21492 == g_coop_p2 so its last test fails, but r13-21496 == p2's id too; both tests fail -> no g_coop_p1_look; in the LOOK g_coop_turn is true -> skipped; either way the native run would put bit 2 into the game's word and fire 0x801556CC's event 102) [read 12846-12900]:
`if (coop_own_closeup() && coop_p2_words_call() && cpu->lr >= CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM && cpu->lr < CLOSE_UP_STEP_TO && cpu->gpr[3] <= 1u && sda_ready(cpu)) { save kCoopHoldWords[4] (23344, 25696, 23040, 24048) [read 17542]; if (r3 == 1) mem_write32(-23344, 0) (as coop_words_hold's close 17262: 0x801556CC(2, 0) on a nonzero word broadcasts 102 from the identity in); lr = cpu->lr; hle_guest_call(COOP_PLAY_HOLD, r3); cpu->lr = lr; put the 4 words back; g_coop_p2_look.held = r3 == 0; log "[coop] player 2's own close-up %s in their copy (lr 0x%08X, game's word kept %u)"; g_hle_passthrough = false; g_hle_tail_call = false; return; }`
- Physical effect [read asm 415409-415452 via the dossier's F10/F11/F12]: r3 == 0 writes +16 = 0x0FFFFFFF into r13-23060/-23052/-23056's channels of THE COPY IN (theirs: g_coop_state[1] in the look 18587 and the tick 14468) and zeroes the control block's word; r3 == 1 writes 0 into -23060/-23052's +16, -23056 stays 0x0FFFFFFF, as the game leaves player 1 after RETURN. 0x8013E160 returns at once (r31 = 4) while +16 != 0; the controller 0x80045D30 steps nothing. COOP_PAD_UPDATE decrements +16 > 0 once a frame: permanent in practice. The nested guest call re-enters coop_play_hold with lr 0x0BADF010: outside both ranges and != COOP_ENCHANT_HOLDS -> passthrough (12898-12900), the shape the scene rule 12866 and coop_words_hold 17262 rely on today.

## Risks ranked

- 1 (highest): the hide-others host walk (B9). Per node per open it makes up to five guest calls (0x801F662C, 0x80117C64, 0x801F0638, 0x801F077C, 0x8014ED1C); a mismatch with the game's loop (a missed skip, the type-5 radius, the double arg) changes the count (cut vs blend, given 6) or hides the wrong character from player 2's half. Mitigation: for owner 1 the intercept passes through (today's path); for owner 2 log each hit and the count, and in the first run compare against ED_SHOW_TRACE of player 1's close-up at the same hole (same camera hash -> the same nodes should be hit).
- 2: the slot rule while their close-up is up (B1) assumes the only valid presses are the stepper's asks (34/7 with script -2); a zone's ask with a script > 0 that the game would let through under a close-up (not seen in the code read: the chooser skips 1/3/5 under the hold) would be unpressable for player 2 until RETURN. Log every slot passed over while .up.
- 3: the camera in modes 0/1 (B8, the FINDING 1 fix): the own-view branch's wall clamp and dream shift now run for a close-up camera; after RETURN their half snaps to the third-person camera instead of easing back to record 2; the first frame after the open is drawn from the step inside the same turn [inf]. Check the shots of each half at the open and the RETURN in mode 0 (the default) and mode 2.
- 4: coop_camera_p2_in's rebuild guard and the by-hash exemption (B8): if g_coop_p2_stepper_in misses a path (a by-hash notify from inside the stepper that is not a test) p2_bare is cleared and their set stops following the room's start for the rest of the room; if the `rest` guard is wrong a room-start choice drops their record 10 mid-close-up (no hang: the pop sees depth 0). Log both.
- 5: the deferred RETURN script's use (B4/B5): it waits at the controller behind g_coop_find_free / g_coop_use.active, so their EXAMINE comes back late while player 1 is in a free find; its lend swaps the live view for the script's duration (one frame for ed219; a RETURN script that waits is today's route, with the lend on). busy_before and the assist skip are new and unrun [inf].
- 6: event 102 from 0x801556CC(2, 1) at the open and press 2 (the hold's broadcast from player 2's identity) is the same event step A's words open fires today; what player 1's node does with a hold event from player 2 is not read.
- 7: the tick's extra swaps while .up (B2): coop_cameras_in(2)/(1) and coop_be_player2 every frame their words are up inside a close-up; coop_breath's 8 CoopPlayerState slots (reuse g_coop_events_p1, add none).
- 8: two show flags at once (B10): a script's fn111(1) show clears both owners' kept hides (today's one flag did the same), so the other player's close-up hide ends early while the game's show also shows 'the player'. Rare; log it.
- 9: the cancel (B12) is wired only at a room change; a cutscene or a state load while their close-up is up leaves their hold in their copy and their set at depth 1 until the room changes (step E). The frontend's save inside the look's press is refused by nothing new.
- 10: the element pool (10 shared elements): two words windows, two prompts and fading ones; 0x801DB374 with none free prints and the window has no text. Count elements in the first runs.
- 11: kind 3 (PICK UP) beside their own kind-2 close-up: a thing lying in reach (room 183's block) is not pressable under their close-up (the slot rule) and its PICK UP after RETURN takes today's lend route; a PICK UP of theirs while player 1 is in an own close-up is today's behaviour. Step C.
- 12: mode 2's culling to player 1's camera (known): a close-up far from player 1 may show fewer objects in player 2's half.

## The scripted check

All runs from the repo root with every log: `sh tools/ed/run.sh SAVE NAME` (logs/run-*-NAME/, both.tsv, shots every 250 ms; `ed timeline`, EVENTS.log and page-*.log first; freezes in saves/reports/hang-*.txt). Add ED_COOP_TOME_COPY=1 for the guest-call 'stopped' line (the ED_GUEST_CALL_LOG line shows only the first 40 calls). docs/coop-next-session.md says only saves/slot1.state (2026-11-09, the room 180 door) is current: first run `ED_RUN_FRAMES=6300` on saves/slot2.state and confirm from ROOM_LOG that it loads in room 183 at a place outside CameraRgn03/05 and before SKPlane01 (y < 2300: the block-holes scene; failed-attempts 666-675); if not, make a state in room 183 from slot1 by the door and use it. Coordinates from `ed room 183` [read]: CameraRgn03 sphere at (-114, 2004, 175) r 312 enter 90 (ed219 Camera04, hash 564924824, at (104,1571,293) looking at (-75,2002,535)); CameraRgn05 sphere at (1433, 1661, 175) r 312 enter 92 (ed219 Camera06, hash 565047707, at (986,1632,285) -> (1411,1630,514)); CameraRgn07 box x -1375..2120, y -1235..1975 (enter 187: Camera01/02): CameraRgn05's centre is inside it, CameraRgn03's is not. BlockRegion01 (the PICK UP block, kind 3) box x 215..504, y 195..485: both puts are far from it. Pads: ED_COOP_P2_PUT=frame,x,y,z (once, direct), ED_PUT=when,who,x,y,z;... (who 2 = player 2, else player 1; placed on the floor by collision), ED_PAD2_SCRIPT=start,end,id;... (ids a/b/x/y/z/start/l/r/up/down/left/right/lup/ldown/lleft/lright/rup/rdown/rleft/rright), ED_INPUT_SCRIPT=start,end,buttons[,x[,y[,analog]]] for player 1 (B = 0x0200; a stick-only event is kept by its stick).

WATCH, NOT SCRIPTED (REVIEW 1): the refusal-branch ease (B6) cannot be scripted (a refused player 2 presses nothing; player 1's bare scene does not refuse the turn); log once when it runs and watch for it in the user's play. The user's play (cannot be scripted): the feel of the hold and the RETURN ease in each camera mode, a Tome open during P2UP (step D), real controllers' held-over logic. After each run add seen items to docs/items-log.md and any failed attempt with its code to docs/coop-failed-attempts.md; `ed test` after every change.

## The final reviews (what they refuted, what they confirmed)

### Review, regressions

- REFUTED (regression, switch on; the design's own B11 / section (3) line for coop_scene_set): adding `|| (coop_own_closeup() && g_coop_p2_look.held)` to p2_close_up at hle.c:15329 freezes PLAYER 1 for good when their own scene ends while player 2's own kind-2 close-up is held. Trace [read hle.c 15315-15380]: fn22(0) from player 1's scene script reaches coop_scene_set with on == 0; owner = 0 (p2_acts false: identity player 1 in the main loop; g_coop_turn false; g_coop_screen false on the own route) at 15322; other_on = p2_on || p2_close_up at 15330 -> TRUE by the new .held term (p2_on false, nothing of theirs in the game's word); the kept-end path 15335-15380 runs the game's end by guest call, then WRITES BACK the three control channels' +16 counts (15344-15347, 15361-15363: these are the GAME's input = player 1's, masked 0x0FFFFFFF by the scene's hold), and busy := words[0] & ~32 (15359) = 0 (only the scene's bit was there), g_coop_p1_look = false (15371). Result: player 1's -23060/-23052/-23056 channels stay at 0x0FFFFFFF with the busy word 0 and no flag set. Nobody lets go: coop_p2_holds (17415-17426) needs g_coop_script_hold or player 2's scene and the word == 2 -> false; coop_p1_close_up (17097) needs g_coop_p1_look -> false, so hle_coop_controller's coop_hold_open (18055-18059) is not called; coop_player1_turn runs only under g_coop_screen (18021) which the own route never sets; player 2's RETURN let-go (0x80199EA0(1) at 0x80025118) runs in THEIR copy by the design's own branch with the game's word kept, so it never reaches player 1's channels. The controller 0x80045D30 steps nothing while +16 != 0 (dossier F11/F12) and COOP_PAD_UPDATE's decrement from 0x0FFFFFFF is permanent in practice. Then, after player 2's RETURN (.held false), the turn's mask loop (18604-18616) copies the GAME's 0x0FFFFFFF over player 2's three channels too: both players frozen, busy 0, no hang report. Today (no .held term) the same scenario takes the native end: p2_close_up false (15329: nothing of player 2's is in the word on the own route), other_on false, the scene's end clears the hold natively and player 1 walks; player 2's own close-up is untouched because their hold lives in g_coop_state[1], not in the game's input. Concrete case: switch on, player 1 in room 183's block-holes scene (L01/419, waits 80+55+150 frames) or the walk-in scene, player 2 opens their own ed219 examine at a filled hole (the user's documented play: coop-todo.md 25 'after each, an examine of a filled hole by each'; 'the pickup/examine beside a scene' is a confirmed behaviour), player 1's scene ends before player 2's RETURN. Fix: do NOT add .held (or .up) to coop_scene_set's p2_close_up; on the own route the scene's end touches nothing of player 2's (their channels are in their copy, their bit is host-side), so the native end is right for both. The design's own CONFIRMED BEHAVIOURS paragraph ('coop_scene_set/coop_p2_holds/the refusal read the game's word, which never carries their bits') contradicts B11.
- Design inaccuracy (not a regression): section (3) lists 0x80024BC8 (the cancel's let-go, 0x80024B24) among the call sites the coop_play_hold own branch covers, but CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM = 0x80024D78 and CLOSE_UP_STEP_TO = 0x800255A4 [read hle.c 12838-12839], so 0x80024BC8 is outside the range: the own branch (lr test) does not fire there, nor does the scene rule (12850: same range) or the p1_look rule (12881: r3 == 0 only); 0x80199EA0(1) runs natively (passthrough 12898-12900) with their copy in and the busy word zeroed by the cancel, so the channel let-go lands in their copy and 0x801556CC(2, 0) on a zero word broadcasts nothing; .held must be cleared by the cancel itself (B12 does). Harmless as written, but the claim is wrong; also coop_zones_new (14945) runs BEFORE coop_prompt_look saves the 2060 block (14957), so anything the cancel's native calls write in r13-24040..-21981 persists past the look (a room change anyway).
- Checked and NOT refuted: (a) frame order for 'one B closes their words and presses RETURN in the same frame': the turn (hle_coop_controller -> coop_player_turn -> coop_prompt_look at ~18690, b_was = b at 15043 every look, including while words.up) runs BEFORE the game's words tick 0x80024AF0 (coop_words_tick consumes g_coop_turn_ran, 14455-14456, set at 18583), so the B that closes their words in the tick is already b_was = true at the next look and presses nothing until released and pressed again. (b) coop_show_asked's hide fill reads node = theirs ? g_coop_p2 : r13-21492 (16321), not r13-21488, so player 2's object is the one hidden in their own look even though the turn's who[] (18660-18668) leaves -21488 as player 1's. (c) The words-cancel close 0x80024384 is `li r3,1; bl 0x80024220` [read asm 32746-32759], so its 0x80042A8C(0) comes with lr WORDS_HOLD_CLOSES and coop_words_hold's own branch (17250-17271) catches it, as the design says. (d) Per-owner show attribution by g_coop_screen (B10) cannot misfire with the switch unset because two close-ups never overlap on today's routes: player 1's B is kept under player 2's screen (coop_p1_press_kept, up = coop_p2_scene_or_hold) and player 2's B is kept under player 1's look (coop_player1_mid_use at 18709); the only overlaps are own-route ones, where coop_p2_words_call() decides first. (e) B6's mask-loop exemption, B3's own branch inside the turn (coop_hold_open 17543-17557 has already zeroed the game's word and channels when player 1 is in their own look; the own branch's save/restore of kCoopHoldWords is consistent with it) and B4's deferral keyed on coop_p2_words_call() all trace through without touching player 1's state.
- Line anchors at HEAD 5330b64 (hle.c unmodified): coop_play_hold 12846-12900 with CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM/TO 12838-12839; coop_words_tick 14452-14492 (ran/g_coop_turn_ran 14455-14456); coop_zones_new 14492-14507 called at 14945; the 2060-block save 14957; the press 15016-15043; the choice loop 15138-15148; slot emptying 15163-15170; coop_scene_set 15308-15420 (owner 15322, p2_close_up 15329, other_on 15330, kept end 15335-15380, counts put back 15361-15363, busy 15359, g_coop_p1_look = false 15371); coop_show_asked 16281-16347 (theirs 16310-16311, node 16321); coop_p1_close_up 17097-17140; coop_words_hold 17243-17330; coop_p2_holds 17415-17426; coop_player_held 17432-17441; coop_hold_open/shut 17543-17568; hle_coop_controller 18012-18200 (screen -> coop_player1_turn 18021, p1_look 18055, p2_hold 18069, close_script consumer 18138-18148); coop_p2_press_held 18512-18529; coop_player_turn 18531-18800 (refusal 18565-18580, g_coop_turn_ran 18583, mask loop 18604-18616, who[] 18660-18668, B block 18697-18717, kept-B 18719-18745, controller 18759, camera step 18761).

### Review, feasibility

- REFUTED (B9, the hide-others host walk; concrete) [read asm 33935-33945]: the game's loop does NOT call 0x80117C64 on *(node+20). At 0x80025614-0x80025628: `or r3,r24; bl 0x801F62D8` (= lwz r3,20(r3): the node's state word, asm 509887) whose RESULT IS DISCARDED by the next `or r3,r24` at 0x8002561C; `bl 0x801F633C` (= lwz r3,52(r3): obj) -> `or r23,r3`; then `bl 0x80117C64` with r3 = r23 = obj. 0x80117C64 is `lwz r3,580(r3); blr` (asm 282146; hle.c 11002-11008 names it OBJECT_MODEL, 'object +580, the character's file number'). So the skip is `*(obj+580) not in 32..35`, a model/file-number test on the OBJECT. The design's walk (section (5), B9, and the dossier's hide-others (a)) does `guest 0x80117C64(*(node+20))`: *(node+20) is the node's state (COOP_NODE_NOW, a small integer such as COOP_STATE_LOOKS), so the guest call executes lwz from state+580 (an address below 0x80000000): hle_guest_call_within (main.c 86-96) breaks on the exception, returns false with *result unset -> the filter reads garbage/0 -> every node reaching that test is mis-filtered (nodes of models 32..35, which the game skips, get counted and hidden; or with a crash-free garbage value others get skipped). The count (cut vs blend, given 6) and the hidden set then differ from the game's for owner 2, and RUN 1/2's 'same camera hash -> same nodes hit' comparison fails. Fix: `model = mem_read32(cpu, obj + 580u)` (no guest call needed) and skip when 32..35; 0x801F62D8 need not be called at all.
- CONFIRMED [read asm 33906-34060, 282909-282947]: the rest of the walk's shape matches the design: skip node == r13-21492 (0x800255E0); room via 0x801F662C(node) == r13-23276 (0x801F662C = obj->0x80118CAC, asm 510100-510108); kind *(node+56) == 1 (0x801F62C0 = lwz 56); type *(node+8) (0x801F62D0 = lwz 8) not 37/64/69; obj = *(node+52); radius 0x80118850 = `lfs f1,624(r3); blr`, type 5 writes obj+624 := radius + *(r2-32128) via 0x801188D8 (`lfs f0,624; stfs f1,624; fmr f1,f0`) and restores after the first test (0x800256AC-0x800256B4); t = 0x801F0638(obj,hash,blend) || 0x801F077C(obj,hash,blend, f1 = *(r2-32132)); beh = 0x8014ED1C(node), hit iff beh && *(beh+24) (0x8014F898 = lwz 24) == 0x8002790C; mark via 0x80033874/0x800338BC, r25++; (r25 & 0xFFFF) -> r13-25952 := 1; run_now -> 0x8002580C; returns r25. 0x8002576C (0x9421FFE0 first word, asm 34020): all nodes, skip r13-21492, kind 1 && flag bit (rlwinm. 5,5), count -> -25952 := 2, returns count. First words 0x9421FFC0 / 0x9421FFE0 as the design states. Both addresses lie inside the dispatch range (HLE_CODE_BASE 0x80003000 .. HLE_CODE_LIMIT 0x80240000, hle.c 5135-5136; hle_dispatch_entry 5148 covers every aligned address), and the registration pattern at 24786-24793 (first-word check + intercept==NULL && notify==NULL) applies.
- CONFIRMED [read hle.c 5454-5486, main.c 61-115]: an intercept may make guest calls and then return a value: hle_guest_call_within memcpy-restores the whole CPUState span (offsetof srr0) after each call, so gpr/lr are back to the intercept's entry state and only *result = gpr[3] survives; the design's `hle_set_u32(count); g_hle_passthrough = false; g_hle_tail_call = false` then reaches the dispatcher's `if (!g_hle_passthrough) { if (!g_hle_tail_call) hle_return(cpu); return true; }` (5477-5481). The gpr[0..12]/lr save in coop_show_asked (16315-16340) is therefore redundant, not required. The host must set gpr[3] AFTER its last guest call (the design's order is right).
- CONFIRMED [read]: every hle.c line the design names matches HEAD 5330b64 (hle.c unmodified in git): coop_play_hold 12846-12900 (scene rule 12850 `theirs = coop_use_theirs() || g_coop_screen`, p1_look rule 12881 with !g_coop_turn, passthrough 12898-12900); CoopWordsOwn 14363-14372; coop_p2_words_call 14391 = own_words && (g_coop_p2_words_tick || (g_coop_prompt_look && g_coop_turn)); coop_words_own_cancel 14424-14450 with ONE caller coop_zones_new 14496 (runs at 14945, before g_coop_prompt_look = true at 14978, so coop_p2_words_call() is false inside it, as the design says); coop_words_tick 14452-14492 (swaps only g_coop_state[1] + CoopWordsOwn; gate g_coop_p2_words.up); coop_prompt_look: cameras_in(2) 14963 / back 15130, slots in 14968 / saved 15056, looks in 14971-14976 / saved 15049-15053, own set 14991-15000, zones-off 14999 only while words.up, press 15016-15043 (outer gate `own && !words.up && !g_coop_screen && !coop_p2_scene_on() && !door_turns && !left_behind`, first live slot then `break`, inner `id == 6 && script == -2 && PROMPT_LOOKS[index] == 1`), b_was = b 15043, own_began 15074-15087, choice loop 15138-15148 skipping only while own && words.up, slots 0/1/3/5 emptied 15163-15170; # Step B build record (2026-11-10 night)

Built from the text above with its two corrections, behind `ED_COOP_OWN_CLOSEUP=1` (only with `ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS=1`; unset,
every new branch is off and the two intercepts aren't registered). What the build is, site by site, is in the comments at
`g_coop_p2_look` (above `coop_play_hold`), `coop_own_closeup`, `coop_p2_own_cancel`, `coop_words_tick`, `coop_prompt_look`'s
press and choice, `coop_hide_others`, `coop_show_asked`, `coop_p2_cameras_rest`, `coop_player_turn`'s refusal and mask loop,
`coop_p2_press_held`, and camera.c's own-view branch. Two small departures from the text: `coop_camera_p2_in`'s and the
scene-over `rest` are true whenever the switch is unset (today's path kept byte for byte), and the hide intercepts are
registered only with the switch on; the per-owner hide bookkeeping (B10) keeps one flag's behaviour when unset. Two
changes the text keys on step A's switch alone (ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS=1, the close-up switch unset) and so change step A's
path: B5 (the words' close script's use takes no assist and tests only what the script itself set busy) and
tome_copy_p1_left's wait on their own words.

What the scripted check found and the build changed: step A's zones-off round their scan while their words are up cleared
their inside words under the close-up, and the look after the words re-entered the zone and ran ed219 again over RETURN
(docs/coop-failed-attempts.md, "Player 2's own close-up"): no zones-off while their own close-up is up.

The runs (saves/slot2.state loads in room 183 at frame 14872; player 2 put at CameraRgn03's centre, player 1 at
Puzzle_Mkr04 inside CameraRgn05; presses 100 frames apart, three pages scrolled, ED_FIN_LOG=1 ED_SHOW_TRACE=1):
- run1-p1alone (logs/run-20261110-213017-...): player 1's own close-up as today ("player 1's close-up holds them alone",
  hidden from their own view, words with no hold, RETURN, script 92), player 2 walking through it, the intercepts passing
  through.
- run2-p2first (-213050-) and run3-p1first (-213123-): both close-ups at once in both orders. Player 2's: "own close-up
  held in their copy (the game's word kept 0 / 2)", "player 2 is hidden from their own view alone", "hides 0 of the room's
  others (0 seen)", DETAIL in their half, "own words open in their copy", their stick under the hold moves them nothing,
  their B closes the words in their tick and press 2 runs there ("after their words: look 1 kind 2 count 3; slots 4:7/-2/4"),
  RETURN shown and pressed ("let go in their copy", the show for owner 1, "shows the room's others again"), "closed with
  script 90: it runs as theirs at the controller", "script 90 runs for player 2", EXAMINE back. Player 1's half untouched
  by any of it. Sheets: sheet-15010-15960.jpg in each run (both halves on their own hole's camera at 15110, each player's
  words in their own half, both back on Camera01 at 15960).
- run2m0-p2first (-213156-, ED_SETTINGS with mod_p2_camera = 0): the same, their close-up camera in their half in mode 0,
  the third-person camera back the frame RETURN ends it (the known cost).
- run4-roomchange (-213228-, ED_RUN_SCRIPT=400:119,460:119 under their open close-up): "own close-up cancelled: a new
  room's zones (look 1, words closed)" with the show and the hide-end intercept first, player 2 brought to room 180 and
  walking, no hang.
Not checked by script (the text's watch list): the refusal-branch ease (needs player 1's busy word refusing the turn:
log line "own close-up camera stepped in a refused turn"), player 1 standing inside player 2's close-up frame (counted,
not hidden: "player 1 in view" in the hide line), a Tome during their close-up (step D), real controllers, the feel of
the hold. `ed test` was not run (its saves are outdated, docs/coop-next-session.md).

The build's review (four readers on the diff against this text, each finding verified by a refuter): 11 findings, 10
refuted (behaviour the text orders), one real and fixed: the cancel at a room change runs inside player 2's turn, where
their input copy is in memory already, and swapped the turn-start snapshot in round the game's cancel, so the hold's
let-go landed in a copy the turn's end discarded; now no copy swap in the turn (`coop_p2_own_cancel`, `copy_in`), the
swap kept for a use or cast of theirs. Re-checked by the room-change run after the fix.

## Risks ranked

1 (highest): the placement (C8b). The unproject rebuild in player 2's frame is host math against one native call; a sign error in the basis (cross order, up vector) or the k scale puts the thing off-screen in their half or behind their eye; the goal is recomputed every frame so a wrong goal is a wrong place, not a hang. Mitigation: build C8(b) last among the visible parts, after RUN 2 has shown everything else; log the first ten frames' out1/out2/goal and compare the thing's object +0/+4/+8 against eye2 + f2*dist; the fallback (0x801E4BDC rebuild) is written down. 2: two scripts from one close (C5): the queue is new; press 4's look+4 script (1181) runs with main kept player 1's (ed221's carried test on the list that holds the block; a re-asked EXAMINE id 6 on look L after ~15560 in [fin] means the rule failed) and the +36 script with r5 restored, one controller apart, each as a use of theirs with the lend on for its duration (B's risk 5). 3: the find hold's own branch (C3) is keyed on two lrs; a find of theirs made by a script in their look (ed52 in a zone script run by their scan) takes the same branch and the same tick, unrun: its close runs FOUND_CLOSE_RAN (already deferred, r5 = record+8 now kept by the queue) but with `look` false in the tick (no stepper), so 0x80042BB8(0)'s let-go lands in their copy with player 1's identity in: right for the hold (channels of the copy in), untested. 4: the cancel (C7) sends 46/47/190 and closes the channel by hand, and in the 2-tick window after press 3 drops the queued 71 by marking it delivered (+40 |= 1) without reading how the pump's second loop frees marked entries [inf: 0x801F5508 on]; a mismatch leaves a drawable with +28 set in the new room or a pool element leaked: count elements and log the thing's node, state and +28 before and after. 5: coop_found_until per owner: today's one `until` cleared the marks of ANY find 4 frames after ANY find's close; with two finds at once the other owner's thing must keep its marks (C3's owner rule): a wrong owner shows a thing in both halves. 6: the thing's +24 registration and 0x8011A278's culling to player 1's frustum [inf]: a thing in front of player 2's camera and outside player 1's view could vanish from both halves (B's risk 12): watch the first run; nothing in the design fixes it. 7: g_coop_own_view/own_fov are refreshed only when their turn runs (coop_camera_step) or the refusal branch steps it; a refused turn with neither leaves the goal a frame stale against a 6%/frame ease: invisible. 8: r13-24984 is one spin angle: two things in state 35 each add 1.5 deg/frame, both turn 3 deg/frame [inf]; cosmetic. 9: the element pool (10): two words windows, two "has found" elements (the find's +0 and the tied +4), two prompts: count in the first both-at-once run. 10: the 190 handler's 0x800D97E0 (the tied second thing) and 0x800D95E4's fades run with player 2's identity inside the tick: they read the object, not the player [read by reader 2]. 11: the find byte's live reader (correction (f)): player 2's held things' holder records run their frame events through their own find, where solo's finder's would not; unknown effect [inf], traced, left. 12: save states (step E), a Tome during their find (step D), a cutscene start while it is up (logged only). 13: the hle_coop_found `other`/`.p2` fix is behind the switch, so a script-made find in their look on ED_COOP_OWN_WORDS alone keeps today's wrong table (unchanged behaviour by rule). 14: the 0x801EB15C intercept's dispatch cost on its 24 HUD/menu callers with the switch on: measure once.

CONFIRMED items kept as designed: the stepper/find/handler/hold/cancel addresses (with press 4's and the cancel's show conditional on look+24 & 4, now in RUN 2's expectations), the own-branch-first argument in C3 (both failure modes written in), the tick's write set (the -25688 leak noted; C11's optional hygiene), hle_coop_found's early return (its reason corrected: -23228 is 0, correction (e)), C8(b)'s mechanics (hle_set_u32 in both intercepts, the trace comparing r3), the channel in the input copy, the unset path byte for byte (the until rule 'every owner with out_of >= 0' when unset), the cancel's words set (theirs in memory at the C7 reads; the deferred scripts re-pushed past coop_words_own_empty), the .up state blocking every other press path (the kind test named as line 15417).

## The second reviews of this text

### Review 1

Refuter pass over the step C design against hle.c (working tree = c630f4c) and build/runs/enchant/asm.txt. One REFUTED item: the tick's per-frame relayout line (14617-14619) now runs for the whole find because C1 makes g_coop_p2_words.up true from press 3 to the close, and it reads pointers that the game frees but never zeroes: the words' close 0x80024220 (and 0x80024384) frees the element and stores nothing at 0x8023E458, and the find's B 0x800243BC frees record+0/+4 and stores nothing (asm 32767-32774), while 0x801DA4D8 only clears a 15-byte mark, so guest_ram_range_valid passes; a words element player 1 opens in that window (same pool slot) is pushed to +4096 by player 2's tick. C2's 'record+0/+4 are 0 after the B' is false; fix by gating the relayout on the words' own channel (rd(ch+4) == COOP_WORDS_RECORD) and dropping or time-gating the find-record extension. Everything else tested held: the kind-3 stepper and dispatch, 0x80024528's writes and the r5 rewrite timing (notify at entry, r5 copied at 0x8002454C), the channel table at COOP_INPUT+176 inside the 816-byte copy, C3's own-branch-first argument (coop_their_close_up true by identity; coop_no_pause false -> native body), the guest-call re-entry via GUEST_CALL_SENTINEL, the 102 rule, 0x801EEE90 into the swapped set, the snap 0x801EEA9C writing only the shown records (in the set, not 0x803012E0), the tick's close path, C5's lrs and argument restore (kind-0 r5 natively 0 at 0x800242DC/0x80025180), the kind-30 handler states, the later-events queue, the cancel 0x80024B24, correction (f), and the unset/A+B paths. NOTED: C0's extra swap must be gated on coop_own_find(); the two C8(b) intercepts belong in tome_copy_hook_off's kOff; C3's 0x801EEE90 at lr 0x80024494 hits the game's set when `look` is false (gate on g_coop_set_in == 2); replace the two asserts with logs; guard the consumer's gpr[5]/main writes on g_coop_use.active.

- **The two new intercepts (0x800D8A88, 0x801EB15C) are not in tome_copy_hook_off's list** (NOTED; hle.c 24440-24458 (tome_copy_hook_off, kOff), design C8(b); docs/coop-level3-design.md 85 and 658). C8(b) registers coop_found_place and coop_found_unproject 'beside 25216-25227's pattern' and says nothing of the Tome copy; the repo's rule (design 85, 658) is that every new co-op intercept goes into kOff or it runs against the copy.
  Evidence: [read] kOff lists coop_find_hold, hle_coop_found, coop_found_object_draw, coop_words_tick, coop_hide_others(_end) and the rest; 0x801EB15C has 24 callers in HUD/menu code (the design's own read), which the copy's Tome frames run. Both new intercepts pass through unless g_coop_place_p2/lr match, so against the copy they are harmless today; the omission is a rule gap for the urn-from-the-Tome path, not a regression.
  Fix: Add OFF(coop_found_place), OFF(coop_found_unproject) to kOff; assert !g_coop_place_p2 in tw_mode_change's clean snapshot as step D plans for g_coop_p2_words_tick.
- **C3's unconditional 0x801EEE90(2, ...) at lr 0x80024494 lands in the GAME's set when the tick's `look` is false** (NOTED; hle.c 14624-14635 (cameras_in(2) only under `look` in coop_words_tick), design C3 ('UNCONDITIONALLY ... their camera set is in at both lrs') and risk 3). C3: their camera set is in at both lrs, so 0x801EEE90(2, 0x80238D58) resets THEIR room record.
  Evidence: [read] In coop_words_tick coop_cameras_in(cpu, 2) runs only inside `if (look)` (14624-14635), and `look = coop_p2_look_up()` is false for a find not made by the stepper (the design's risk 3: ed52 in a zone script of their look, whose close also reaches 0x80042BB8(0) at lr 0x80024494 under coop_p2_words_call). Then the game's set is in and the write at 0x80603178 + 2*136 + 72..80 is player 1's room record. [inf] Not a confirmed path, but a one-line gate avoids it.
  Fix: Run the 0x801EEE90 call only when g_coop_set_in == 2, or wrap it in `set_was = g_coop_set_in; coop_cameras_in(2); ...; coop_cameras_in(set_was)`.
- **asserts in C1's tail and C2's post are crash paths in the host** (NOTED; design C1 ('assert(g_coop_p2_find.up) (C3 set it)'), C2 ('assert(!g_coop_p2_find.up) (C3 dropped it)')). The two states are always consistent when the find's channel is seen.
  Evidence: [read] The look's tail (15322-15330) runs for every own look, and the find's channel in words[0] can come from a script-made find in their look (ed52) whose 0x80042BB8(1) also takes C3's own branch, so the invariant holds there too; but a find opened in their look and closed by the game's own path before the tail (none read) or any later route change would abort the process. The codebase logs such mismatches (14655-14660, 14675-14679) rather than asserting.
  Fix: Replace both asserts with an ED_COOP_LOG line and set the flag to the observed state.
- **The kind-30 handler's states, the later-events queue and the game's close-up cancel are as the design reads them** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x800D87D0-0x800D8890 (217341-217388), 0x800D8A70, 0x800D95B8-0x800D95C4 (218231-218234), 0x801F57C0 (509177-509207), 0x801F5350 (508893-508903), 0x801F53E8 (508938-508943), 0x80024B24-0x80024C18 (33243-33294); hle.c 14544-14561). Corrections (c), (d) and C7: state 1 answers 3/71/72/73/223/1 only and returns 0 for 47/190; +28 := 0x800D8A88 as the thing enters 35; the queue entry layout and the pump's +40 & 1 skip; the cancel for kind 3 count 4 lets go, pops, snaps, shows, counts and never touches the find window.

The pump walks *(r13-21524)+36 and skips entries with +40 & 1 (0x801F5410-0x801F5418). 0x80024B24 for kind 2/3 (`cmpwi r0,1; beq; bc 4,0 (>1); cmpwi r0,4; bc 4,0 skip`): +0 := 0 (0x80024B9C); count <= 0 -> 0x80024C14; count == 2 -> 0x80024384; 0x80199EA0(1) at 0x80024BC8 (lr 0x80024BCC, outside [0x80024D78, 0x800255A4): native, in the copy coop_p2_own_cancel has in, busy zeroed round it 14548-14559); pop; snap unless +24 & 1; 0x80043C88(1) if +24 & 4 at 0x80024C0C; 0x8002576C at 0x80024C10; r30 == 0 -> exit. Nothing reads -25960/0x8030A6E8 in the cancel.

- **The find byte's reader is live (correction f) and the holder step's path is as read** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x8014FDC4-0x8014FDFC (339578-339592), 0x800083F8-0x800083FC and 0x800084A4-0x800084AC (4103-4104, 4146-4148), 0x8014F4B4-0x8014F514 (338998-339022), 0x80027730-0x80027760 (36053-36065)). 0x8014FDB8(r3) passes r5 = 48 | (r3 ? 0x800 : 0); the main loop calls it with 1 and 0; the +32 bctrl gets r5 = flags & 0x800 and the +28 bctrl's lr is 0x8014F510 with r3 fed to 0x8014F3B4; 0x80027748 reads -25932 on the flag-0 path.

C4: finder_p2 is true by gpr[5] == player 2's id, the notice goes to player 1, the r5 rewrite names player 1's list, the word carry is never reached (script 0), and 240/46/47/190/71 to the thing pass the event intercept.

### Review 2

The step C design holds up on feasibility: every address, lr, offset and hle.c line it names was re-read and matches (the find's open/handler/hold/stepper/cancel/prompt-use paths, 0x801EEE90's target inside the swapped camera set, the kind-30 state table, the event queue's layout and the pump's free loop, the +28/+32 hook passes and lr 0x8014F510, the dispatch/chain-stop mechanics with GUEST_CALL_SENTINEL 0x0BADF010 as the nested lr, the 2060-block membership of each word the own branches touch, the per-frame clock behind the 71's 2.0 delay, and 0x801DC76C as a stack-only vsprintf). Two test expectations are refuted: RUN 2/3 have FOUR 102s from player 2's id (the words' open at press 1 runs 0x80199EA0(0) natively with the game's word in, coop_words_hold 17644-17647), and the ED_COOP_EVENTS_LOG trace can never show player 2's own 102s (coop_events logs only events to player 2's node; 0x801F5620 sends 102 to everyone but the sender), so the count needs a log at 0x801556CC. Noted: C8(b) decomposes out1 (from last frame's draw matrices) against this frame's player-1 view, so the goal drifts by player 1's camera motion while they walk; RUN 2's press-0 hide line is as conditional on look+24 & 4 as the show; C5's post-coop_use_start writes need an `active` guard; and the find channel's +16 = 3 wait, 0x8011A278's culling and the matrix build's frame position stay unread.

- **RUN 2/3 102 count: four from player 2's id, not three (the words' open at press 1 broadcasts one too)** (REFUTED; hle.c coop_words_hold 17636-17647 (the own branch: `if (!opens) wr(-23344, 0)` only for the close; the open runs COOP_PLAY_HOLD(0) with the game's word in); asm 0x80199F14-0x80199F34 (0x80199EA0(0) -> 0x801556CC(2, 1)); 0x801556F0-0x80155720 (102 sent from 0x801F62B8() when the result is nonzero); 0x80024368 bl 0x80042A8C lr 0x8002436C = WORDS_HOLD_OPENS, reached from press 1 0x80025370 bl 0x80024310). Finding 3 / RUN 2 and RUN 3: 'exactly three 102s from player 2's id (15000, 15300, 15400)' — press 0, press 2, the find's open.
  Evidence: [read] Press 1 (0x80025344) calls 0x80024310, whose 0x80042A8C at 0x80024368 is coop_words_hold's intercept; its own branch guest-calls COOP_PLAY_HOLD(hold = 0) without zeroing the busy word (17644-17647 zero it only when !opens), so 0x80199EA0's hold-on path 0x80199F2C-0x80199F34 runs 0x801556CC(2, 1) natively: result = word | 2 is nonzero -> 0x801F5620(102, 0x801F62B8() = r13-21496 = player 2's id in the look). The step B log logs/run-20261110-213050-stepB-run2-p2first shows the words' open hold at 15200 with 'the game's word kept 0' (the word was 0 before, 2 during the native call). So RUN 2 has four 102s of theirs: ~15000 (press 0), ~15100 (press 1, the words' open), ~15300 (press 2 in the tick; the words' close zeroes the word, none from it), ~15400 (the find's open); none at ~15551.
  Fix: RUN 2/3 expectations: four 102s from player 2's id at the four hold-ons (press 0, the words' open, press 2, the find's open), none at the words' close or the find's close. Decision (a) stands; only the count was wrong.
- **The events trace (ED_COOP_EVENTS_LOG) cannot show player 2's own 102s at all** (REFUTED; hle.c coop_events 20062-20076 (`node == g_coop_p2` gate before the `[fin] player 2's event` line, log_left 400); 20352-20365 (the 102 rule logs only under coop_grab_log()/coop_room_change_log() and only when held != from); asm 0x801556FC-0x80155720, 0x801F5620 (the broadcast, 'everyone but the player who set it', hle.c 20344-20345)). RUN 2: 'the events trace has exactly three 102s from player 2's id'; RUN 3: 'the same three 102s of theirs'.
  Evidence: [read] The only ED_COOP_EVENTS_LOG line in coop_events (20072-20075) is written for events reaching player 2's node (`node == g_coop_p2` at 20068). A 102 from player 2's id is broadcast by 0x801F5620 to the room's others, never to its sender, so none of player 2's own 102s reaches that trace; player 1 hearing one is logged only with ED_GRAB_LOG or the room-change log and only when `held >= 0 && from >= 0 && held != from` (20355-20362). The assertion as written would pass vacuously with zero lines.
  Fix: Count the 102s at the busy set itself: a one-line ED_COOP_LOG/ED_COOP_EVENTS_LOG print in a notify at 0x801556CC (the kNotify entry {0x801556CCu, notify_trace_busy} at 25405 already exists; extend trace_look or add a coop notify) printing r3, r4, the word's result, 0x801F62B8()'s id and the frame; or enable ED_GRAB_LOG in RUN 2/3 and assert the '[coop] player 2's busy set doesn't let go of player 1' lines when player 1 is held (RUN 3 only).
- **C8(b): out1 is decomposed against this frame's player-1 view, but 0x801EB15C used last frame's matrices** (NOTED; design C8(b) ('view 1 FROM THE SAVED COPY g_coop_place_view1', saved at the +28 step); asm main loop 0x80008424 (camera step 0x801EB9A0(3)) -> 0x800084D0 (0x8014FE24, the +28 step) -> the draw later in the frame; 0x801E4BDC's callers 0x801E9104/0x801E9640/0x801E97D0/0x801EA0F0/0x801F192C (the camera/draw module, asm 496458 etc.); 0x801EB15C reads 0x8060234C/0x8060244C/0x806024BC (asm 498528+)). out2 = eye2 + r2*x*k + u2*y*k + f2*z with (x, y, z) = (d.r1, d.u1, d.f1), d = out1 - eye1 where eye1/axes1 come from CAMERA_VIEW as saved at the intercept (this frame's camera step).
  Evidence: [read] The design itself states the matrices are 'built by the previous DRAW (player 1's, one frame old)'; the +28 step runs at 0x800084D0 after this frame's camera step at 0x80008424 and before this frame's draw, so out1 lies on last frame's camera ray while eye1/r1/u1/f1 are this frame's. [inf] With player 1 walking (RUN 2's 15350-15450 burst) the decomposition error equals player 1's camera motion per frame (position and rotation), fed to a 6%/frame ease: a visible drift/jitter of the goal in player 2's half, not a sign error. The game's own path has only the eye-position part of this mismatch (0x800D8ACC reads the live eye).
  Fix: Decompose against the view the matrices were built from: keep the CAMERA_VIEW copy from the previous frame's +28 intercept (or save it in a notify at the matrix build 0x801E4BDC, lr 0x801E9108 etc.) and use that as view 1; or drop the native unproject for player 2 and build the ray from the screen point directly (x = 0 at 320, y from 165 against the centre, z from the depth word 16248 through the projection) in player 2's frame. Log out1 against eye1 + axes·(x, y, z) for the first ten frames to see the mismatch size before deciding.
- **The 71 delay unit is frames: r13-22216 is bumped once per game frame** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x800186E8-0x80018724 (the frame loop: -26148++ at 0x800186EC, 0x8013E518 pad update at 0x80018708, `lwz r0,-26240(r13); cmpwi 4; beq; bl 0x801DC9F8` at 0x80018710-0x8001871C); 0x801DC9F8-0x801DCA10 (addic. +1, reset to 0 on overflow); 0x801F57C0 0x801F57F8-0x801F5810 (due = 0x801F5350() + f1); 0x80024664 lfs f1,-32152(r2) = 2.0). Correction (d) [inf]: 'r13-22216 is the game's tick counter ... the delay is 2 ticks: 71 lands ~2 frames after press 3'.
  Evidence: [read] The three writers of -22216 are 0x801DC9F0 (set), 0x801DCA00 (increment) and 0x801DCA0C (reset); the incrementer 0x801DC9F8 is called from the frame loop at 0x8001871C once per pass unless -26240 == 4 (a menu/pause mode), and from 0x801A3174/0x801A5034 (not the loop). The pump 0x801F53E8 runs each frame at 0x800084D4. So due = now + 2.0 delivers on the second pump after the press.
  Fix: None; RUN 2 may assert 71's delivery at press 3 + 2 frames (+1 if the turn runs after the pump in the frame).
- **0x801DC76C(0x8023E4F8, script) formats into a stack buffer: nothing written to static memory** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x801DC76C-0x801DC808: r30 = r3 (0x8023E4F8); varargs r3..r10 spilled to sp+8..36; 0x801DC7BC `addi r3,r1,116` (dst on the stack), r4 = r30 (the format), r5 = sp+104 (the va_list); bl 0x800F22F0 (vsprintf); returns). CODES / finding 9: '0x801DC76C named (a formatter, no output)'.
  Evidence: [read] 0x8023E4F8 (bindings + 64) is the FORMAT string, not a destination; the destination is sp+116 and the result is discarded. Nothing of the find's leaks past the own set through it.
  Fix: None.
- **The pump frees a 'delivered' entry cleanly: C7's drop of the pending 71 by +40 |= 1 is sound** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x801F53E8: first loop 0x801F5410-0x801F5418 skips +40 bit 0; second loop 0x801F5508-0x801F5560: `lwz r3,32(r4); stw r5,36(r3)` (prev->next = next; the world record *(r13-21524) is the head, so prev is never 0), `stw r0,32(r3)` (next->prev), +36/+32 zeroed, -21528 -= 1, 0x80171828(-21508 pool, entry)). Risk 4 [inf]: 'without reading how the pump's second loop frees marked entries [inf: 0x801F5508 on]'.
  Evidence: [read] The chain is doubly linked (+32 prev, +36 next) under the head record; the second loop unlinks every entry with +40 bit 0 and returns it to the pool at r13-21508, decrementing the count at r13-21528. Marking a queued 71 delivered (C7) leaks nothing and is skipped by the first loop. Also [read] 0x801F5000 0x801F501C-0x801F5060: an entry whose target node no longer resolves (0x801F5F88 -> 0) is returned 8 (dropped) and still marked delivered by 0x801F54F0: a pending 71 to a vanished node is harmless on its own.
  Fix: None; keep the guard on 0x801F5F88(thing) and the state check.
- **The +28 placement hook runs only from 0x8014FE24 (r4 = 2) and the +32 holder hook only from 0x8014FDB8 (r4 = 1): C8(b)'s lr 0x8014F510 and correction (f) are both right** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x8014FE48-0x8014FE54 (`li r4,2; li r5,80; bl 0x8014F3E8` per drawable, main loop 0x800084D0); 0x8014FDE4-0x8014FDFC (`li r4,1; r5 = 48 | (r3 ? 0x800 : 0)`, main loop 0x800083FC with r3 = 1 and 0x800084AC with r3 = 0); 0x8014F3E8: +32 bctrl needs r27 & 1 (0x8014F4B4), +28 bctrl needs r27 & 2 (0x8014F4EC), lr 0x8014F510, r3 -> 0x8014F3B4 at 0x8014F514). C8(b): 'THE LR THE INTERCEPT SEES IS 0x8014F510, r3 = drawable, r4 = *(drawable+56)'; correction (f): the holder step's byte read is reached on the second pass.
  Evidence: [read] as cited; 0x8014F3E8 also recurses into children (0x8014F490-0x8014F4AC) with the same r4/r5, so the thing's drawable is reached whether it is a root or a child. 0x800276C4's `cmpwi r29,0; beq 0x80027730` (r29 = r5 = flags & 0x800) reaches `lbz r0,-25932(r13)` at 0x80027748 on the 0x800084AC pass.
  Fix: None.
- **Dispatch and chaining: the two new intercepts are chain stops if registered in find_coop; the nested guest call's lr is 0x0BADF010** (CONFIRMED; hle.c find_coop 24805-25554 (the 25216-25227 pattern inside it), 26000 find_coop(cpu) then 26004 publish_codegen_options(); 5594-5615 (`g_chain_stop[i] = intercept != NULL || notify != NULL`, chain_ok from it); 5158-5163 hle_dispatch_entry (any aligned address in range has an entry); main.c 50-80 (GUEST_CALL_SENTINEL 0x0BADF010u set as lr, sp lowered 0x800 per nesting); grep: no existing hook at 0x800D8A88 or 0x801EB15C in hle.c). C8(b): 'both entries free of intercept/notify; find_coop runs before publish_codegen_options so the bctrl-reached entry is a chain stop'; 'its nested lr is the sentinel: passes this gate'.
  Evidence: [read] A direct `bl` to a non-stop target chains past hle_dispatch (5560-5566), so 0x801EB15C (reached by bl at 0x800D8B80) MUST be registered before 26004: inside find_coop it is. The sentinel 0x0BADF010 is outside [CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM, CLOSE_UP_STEP_TO), != 0x8002471C/0x80024494/0x8014F510/0x800D8B84, so every nested guest call of an intercepted function passes through its own intercept natively, as coop_play_hold's own branch already relies on (12896-12898). First words: 0x800D8A88 = stwu r1,-592 (0x9421FDB0) and 0x801EB15C = stwu r1,-96 (0x9421FFA0) [read asm 217515, 498528].
  Fix: Register both in find_coop under coop_own_find() (the 25216-25227 pattern), never later; the comment at 5580-5582 says a hook installed after publish needs publish_codegen_options called again.
- **The kind-30 handler's state 1 answers 3/71/72/73/223/1 only; 71 -> 35; 47 and 190 return 0 there** (CONFIRMED; asm 0x800D87D0-0x800D8890 (state 1), 0x800D8A70 (the 0 return), 0x800D8960-0x800D8A64 (state 35: 61, 74, 47 -> 0x800D99EC, 190 -> 0x800D9B64 + 0x800D97E0 + state 1, 46 -> 0x800D95E4, 1 -> 0x800D9530(node, 0) installs +28 := 0x800D8A88 at 0x800D95C4), 0x800D9B64 0x800D9BA4 bl 0x801642D4 lr 0x800D9BA8 with r5 = 0x801F62C8(node)). Correction (c)/(d), C7, C8(b) as named.
  Evidence: [read] as cited; 0x800D8A88 is installed only on entering 35 (event 1 in state 35), so in 5b (cancel within the 2-frame delay) the thing is in state 1 with +28 unset and 47/190 would be ignored: the design's drop of the queued 71 is the right move.

bindings+44 at 0x80024558 and +12 at 0x800246D4 into 0x8023E4B8; 0x801F95A0 at 0x800245C0/0x800245E4; 0x801F9DE8(node, r28, 30) at 0x80024660; 71 with f1 = 2.0 at 0x80024664-0x80024678; +24 setter 0x8014F820(drawable, 0x801FA400) at 0x80024688; Also [read] coop_prompt_look's per-look clear of the world thing's prompt (15199-15201) is by the pair (19, 2246) (coop_prompt_slot_clear 14079-14091, COOP_THING_PROMPT/SCRIPT 14104-14105), so the stepper's PICK UP (19, -2) asked at press 2 survives the next look; 0x801F6250 reads -21484 (main's node), so ed221's actor test and carried test (0x801F9228 on main's list) both read MAIN, as C5's kind-1 restore assumes [read asm 0x801F6250-0x801F625C, `ed fn 0x80017604`].
  Fix: None.
- **RUN 2's hide line at press 0 is as conditional as the show: both test look+24 & 4** (NOTED; asm 0x80025258-0x80025268 (+24 |= 4 when 0x801F0638 found the camera), 0x80025280-0x80025298 (`rlwinm. r0,r0,0,29,29; beq 0x8002529C; li r3,0; bl 0x80043C88`), 0x80025540-0x80025554 (the same test before 0x80043C88(1))). RUN 2 expects '[show] main(0) lr 0x8002529C player 2' unconditionally at ~15000 and the show '[show] main(1) lr 0x80025558 player 2' ONLY IF look+24 & 4.
  Evidence: [read] The hide at 0x80025298 is skipped when look+24 bit 2 is clear, exactly like the show at 0x80025554 and the cancel's at 0x80024C0C. Either both lines appear (the camera found: MantBlockCam01 should be) or neither; 'player 2 is hidden from their own view alone' is conditional too.
  Fix: State both as conditional on the same bit, or assert their pairing (a hide without a show, or the reverse, is the regression).
- **C5's consumer: coop_use_start can return without starting a use; the gpr[5]/main writes after it must be guarded** (NOTED; hle.c coop_use_start 16411-16413 (`if (p2 && !guest_ram_range_valid(cpu, mem_read32(cpu, g_coop_p2 + 52u), 640u)) return;`), 16452-16461 (active = true, tail call, gpr[4] = gpr[5] = 0); hle_coop_controller 18523-18531). C5: 'coop_use_start(script, true) ... THEN cpu->gpr[5] = entry.arg5' and for kind 1 'mem_write32(r13-21488, was[3]); mem_write32(r13-21484, was[4])'.
  Evidence: [read] On the early return nothing is tail-called and g_coop_use.was is unset: writing gpr[5] then alters hle_coop_controller's own live r5 (the intercepted controller function's argument) and the main writes would store stale was[] values into the identity words. Today's one close_script consumer has the same shape but writes nothing after the call, so the hazard is new to C5. Also [read] coop_be_player2 (12157-12170) writes was[3]/was[4] back at the use's end, so the kind-1 restore is undone correctly only when the use did start.
  Fix: After coop_use_start, `if (!g_coop_use.active) { log; return; }` before the gpr[5] and -21488/-21484 writes (and drop the queue entry or re-push it).
- **What the design still leaves unread (gaps, not errors)** (NOTED; asm 0x8013E190-0x8013E1E0 and 0x8013E35C-0x8013E368 (a channel whose +16 is nonzero exits the step: who counts the find channel's +16 = 3 down from 0x80024710's 0x8013DBC0(ch, 3) is unread); 0x8011A278 (culling, risk 6, unread here too); the function at 0x801E9014 whose 0x801E9104 builds the matrices (its place in the frame relative to 0x800084D0, relevant to the C8(b) note above); grep: 23 `bl 0x801EB15C` sites in all (22 others), the design says 24; 0x801F5620's receivers of player 2's 102 (every room behaviour but the sender) and what the room's others do with a 102 whose busy word is restored to 0 the same call: step B's existing behaviour at press 0/1/2, extended by the find's open). C9/risks 6, 9, 14; finding 3's acceptance of the hold-on's 102.
  Evidence: [read] as cited; none contradicts the design, each is a place a run would have to settle. The +16 = 3 'wait' is the same mechanism step A's words channel already lives with, so it is a documentation gap rather than a risk.
  Fix: Read 0x8013E35C's caller side for +16 (or log ch+16 per tick for the find's first frames in RUN 2); count 0x801EB15C's dispatches once (risk 14); keep the 102 broadcast under watch in RUN 3 where player 1 is held (the 'doesn't let go' line, 20358).



## The readers' reports behind the step C design (2026-11-10 night)

Four readers on the disassembly (build/runs/enchant/asm.txt) and hle.c at c630f4c: the kind-3 stepper, the find window and its hold, the host's find code, the thing's placement. Kept as written; the design above corrects them where it says so (its "Corrections to round 1").

Runs INSIDE the find handler, inside 0x8013E160, inside the tick (lr chain 0x80008500 -> 0x80024AF0 -> 0x8013E160 -> 0x80024434 at 0x800244D4 -> 0x80024D78).
1. obj flag 4 SET again (0x80122514 lr 0x80025508, 0x801223B8 lr 0x80025514). Press 4 sets it; nothing in the stepper clears it.

7. 0x801642D4(looks[idx]+4 script, 0, 0) lr 0x80025590. This lr is NOT CLOSE_UP_END_RAN (0x80025198, kind 2's) and NOT FOUND_CLOSE_RAN (0x800244C0, the script > 0 branch of 0x80024434, never taken for -2).
CONFIRMED: NO 0x80199EA0 in press 4 (the kind-3 stepper calls 0x80199EA0(0) at 0x800251E8 and 0x800253CC only; (1) is at 0x80025118, kind 2's RETURN, and the cancel's 0x80024BC8). What lets the player go for kind 3 is 0x80042BB8(0) at 0x80024490 -> 0x80199EA0(1) at 0x80042BF0 (lr 0x80042BF4), run BEFORE the stepper's press 4, in 0x80024434's close. [read]

## 6. The object's flag 4 [read + inf]
- Setters: presses 0/2/4 (0x80024E7C kind 2 too), 0x80042A8C(1) at 0x80042ABC, 0x800429B4 / 0x80042B60 (the other hold functions), 0x8007B928, 0x80168290 (a script's hold).
- The setter 0x801223B8(obj, flags) stores to model(obj)+244 (model = 0x80122274(obj)); on a bit-0x2 change it re-enables the model's parts (0x80122400-0x801224A4); on a bit-0x4 change (0x801224A8-0x801224F4) with bit 4 now set it writes three zero floats at obj+340 + 12*0x801226C0(obj) (a bone index from the model) [inf: a bone's extra turn zeroed: the head stops looking about].
- Clearers (rlwinm mask 30,28 before bl 0x801223B8): 0x8003C39C, 0x8005F268, 0x80072BB0, 0x800770C0/154/21C, 0x80077AA0, 0x800890EC, 0x8008F40C, 0x800918C0, 0x80094EB0, 0x800A6E4C, 0x800DD8A4, 0x800E0EF8. The player's is 0x8003C398 inside 0x8003B470 (stwu -352; r3 = event in r23, reached by table; its case r23 == 2 at 0x8003C348): r16 = 0x801222F0(obj) motion; f = 0x80122514(obj); if (f & 4) and motion is 3 or 2: if r13-23276 room == 83 -> 0x80124788(obj) else 0x801223B8(obj, f & ~4); then obj... stfs 196(r27) := *(r2-31460) (r27 = the state block at +140 of r21). So the clear is the player's own per-step event handler once their motion is idle (2/3) [inf on the meaning of 2/3]; it runs on whichever object the behaviour steps, i.e. player 2's node clears its own. ######## READER readers
**READER 3 report: who reads the find's words (game and host), and what an own-route find of player 2's would get wrong.** Sources [read] this pass: asm.txt lines cited; hle.c at HEAD 122c2df (line numbers below); docs/coop-level3-design.md 657, 767-780, 815, 877-915.

## 1. The game's readers, word by word

**r13-25932 (the find byte).** Written only by 0x80042BB8 (stb at 0x80042C18, asm 64015). Read by exactly ONE instruction in the whole DOL: 0x80027748 (asm 36059), inside 0x800276C4 [read, grep `-25932(r13)`].
- 0x800276C4(h, node, flag) (asm 36026-36099) is a held-thing holder record's step call: installed at slot +32 by 0x8014F864(h, 0x800276C4) and at +52 by 0x8014F830(h, draw, 0x8002790C, 0x800276C4) in the ed3 spawn 0x8000E4A4 (asm 10512-10528, effect 3 "given to the controlled character's hold record") and in event 57's queue reader 0x80025FF0 (asm 34615-34640, r23 = the character node written to +56 by 0x8014F880); the host installs the same on player 2's copies (hle.c 22908-22921 coop_copy_holder). Its node (+56, via 0x8014F8A0) is the HOLDING CHARACTER, not the thing. Body: r5 (flag) != 0 → 0x80027504(h, object, room) and return; r5 == 0 → if 0x8014F890(h) (+28, the draw slot set) and node: `if (find byte != 0 && 0x801F62C0(node) == 1 [kind 1, a character]) skip; else if (busy 0x80155738 && 0x801F68D4(node)&0xFF [held, +28's 8]) skip; else 0x801F5D48(node)` (the node's frame events 3/4, COOP_BAR_FRAME), then 0x80118C0C(object). So when set it would stop the frame events of every character node that holds a thing — both players'.
- BUT the only bctrl of slot +32 is 0x8014F4E0 in 0x8014F3E8 with `r5 = flag & 0x800` (rlwinm r5,r28,0,20,20 at 0x8014F4D4, asm 339001-339004); the four callers of 0x8014F3E8 pass (beh,1,48|0x800) at 0x8014FDE8-0x8014FDFC (the step pass 0x8014FDB8, main loop 0x800084AC), (beh,2,80) at 0x8014FE4C-54 (the draw pass: bit1 → slot +28 only), (beh,0,384) at 0x8014FEA8-B0 (r4=0: neither slot called), and itself (339000). Slot +52 is never read (grep `lwz r12, 52(`: only stack loads and 0x8009F254's r5-based record). The one other reader of +32 is 0x800B7C60 (0x8014F888, a non-null test). So r5 is always 0x800 when 0x800276C4 runs → the 0x80027748 branch is DEAD at runtime [read + inf: no caller passes bit0 of r4 without 0x800 in r5]. Risk 10 of the round-2 design (line 673) is moot: the motion step never reads -25932. What the holder's draw calls DO read is the busy word: 0x800271B4 (asm 35717: busy && held(+28's 8) → 0x80123B70(object) else 0x80123698(object), gated by *(0x802F62C0+456) == -2) and 0x8002723C (asm 35759: if 0x80027504 != 0: busy && !held → skip else 0x80126514/0x8011DFD0(object)). For whom: the HOLDER's node (the player or copy), reading the game's one busy word.

**r13-24048 (prompt flags), bit 2.** Writers: 0x80117260(bit, on) (asm 281505-281510; the find hold's at 0x80042C14 with r3=2), 0x80117338 (:= 0 in the prompts' reset 0x80117320), the hold's bit 32 etc. Readers [read, grep `bl 0x8011727C` + direct lwz]:
- 0x80008490: `li r3,2; bl 0x8011727C; cmplwi r3,0; bne 0x800084A0` → 0x80163E00 (the script timers) runs only with bit 2 CLEAR (asm 4140-4144). 0x80163E00 (asm 360073-360105): walks SCRIPT_STATES 0x805CAA40 rows of 8 (10 rows; +0 lha script, +4 lhz timer); timer-- ; at 0 → 0x801642D4(script,0,0). On the table IN MEMORY = player 1's (the tick/main loop never swaps it). When set: every script's wait stops, for whoever's table is in.
- 0x800084DC: `li r3,-1; bl 0x8011727C; beq skip; li r3,0; bl 0x80117360` (asm 4158-4163): with ANY flag bit set, every frame 0x80117360(0) (asm 281563-281600) zeroes the shown prompt slot 6 (0x80335AE0, 28 B), sets its +8/+4 := -1, +20 := -1; r3==0 → no 0x80116E70 clear of the source slot. So while a find is up no prompt is shown to the player whose slots are in (player 1's).
- 0x801176B4 inside the chooser 0x80117628 (asm 281747-281800): after `r12 = *(r13-24040)` callback (0 → no choosing) it tests `-24048 != 0 → skip all choosing` (bne 0x8011778C); else slots 0..5: slots 1/3/5 count only while 0x80199F50() (= 0x8013DBD8(r13-23060) channel live) or 0x80199F74() (= 0x8013DBD8(r13-23056)) says live (asm 281782-281800, 415453-415476); skip +24&1 with r30==0, +16 != 0, +8 == -1; the first live one is copied to slot 6. For whom: the slots/channels in memory (player 1's between turns). 0x80199F50/74's other callers 0x80006D18/24, 0x80006EF4/F00 are the prompt press 0x80006EBC's own gates (asm 2639-2642, 2758-2761), run in whoever's input copy is in.
- 0x8009E7BC in 0x8009E778 (asm 157925-158000): a behaviour state handler (table entry via 0x8009C61C in 0x8009C5C0's init, 44-byte rows) counts record+8 up to 10, then `0x8011727C(2) != 0 → return 0` (waits while any find is up); clear → stb 0 at r29+104, three 0x8006AF5C(r29,*(r31),0,0,0), 0x801F6828(player node, 0) [clears the held bit], 0x80199EA0(0) [hold ON], 0x801F59B0(81, 0, *(*(r31+140)+68), 0), return 1. Which behaviour isn't traced [inf: one that waits for the finder's close-up before taking the hold itself]. It reads the game's one bit.
No other reader: 0x80024434/0x80024528/0x800243BC/0x80024D78 never read -24048, -25932 or the busy word [read: callers lists].

**Busy word r13-23344, bit 16.** Only 0x801556CC writes (asm 345276-345302: andc/or, stw; if the NEW word != 0 → 0x801F5620(102, player 0x801F62B8(), 0, 0x801F662C(...), f1) = event 102 broadcast from the identity in). 0x80155738 reads (asm 345303). Readers that matter here [read]:
- 0x8000749C in the player update 0x80007460 (asm 3105-3135): with r30 bit 0x8000, `busy != 0 && 0x801F68D4(node)&0xFF [held]` → skip 0x80045BC4 (the controller, hook hle_coop_controller) and 0x801F5D48(node); otherwise both run. (Correction to the round-1 note: busy alone doesn't skip the controller; busy AND the node's +28's 8 does.)
- 0x80144B98 in 0x80144B34 (main loop 0x80008488, asm 328160-328200): the record at *(r13-23524) steps unless `busy && (+4904 & 8)`.
- 0x801F541C in 0x801F53E8 (main loop 0x800084D4, asm 508930-508960): the later-events pump (what 0x801F57C0 queues): for a pending event whose target 0x801F5F88(+8) is held (+28's 8) or whose event is 75, while busy, its due time +20 += *(r2-18680) [inf: postponed through the busy].
- 0x80027764/0x800277F4 (dead branch, above), 0x800271F0/0x80027298 (the holder's draw calls, above), 0x800074AC-style held asks everywhere (hle.c 17873-17877 list).

## 3. Verdict, per reader, for a find of player 2's on the step-B route (switch on)

Sees the wrong thing today → must answer:
1. coop_find_hold (17725 branch (a) by identity): writes -24048 bit 2 into the game's word (not in the put-back block) → 0x80008490 stops PLAYER 1's script timers; 0x800084DC/0x80117628 blank player 1's prompt; 0x8009E778 waits → OWN BRANCH (section 2), bit 2 host-side, -25932 in the set, 0x80042BB8 run natively in their copy with the busy word kept (zeroed round the let-go), `.held = on`.
2. coop_script_timers 18597: player 2's own timers don't stop through their find → add the own flag to the return.
3. coop_run_script 16858-16859: the +36 script's run at lr 0x800D9BA8 inside the tick on player 1's table → defer like FOUND_CLOSE_RAN; the stepper's -2 close and the record's +24 script are already deferred.
4. hle_coop_found: finder and notice right by identity; r5 rewrite keeps one list (as designed); `other`/`found_word.p2` wrong only for a script-made find inside the look (key on coop_p2_words_call()); g_coop_found_objects/out_of/until single → per owner.
5. CoopWordsOwn: lacks 0x8030A6E8 and 0x8023E4B8 → add; coop_p2_own_cancel needs the find case.
6. coop_prompt_look's two kind/id tests: admit kind 3 and id 19.

Right as they stand (nothing of theirs enters the game's words once 1 is built): coop_flags_ask (never triggered), coop_busy_ask, coop_node_held_ask, coop_p2_holds, coop_player_held (coop_p2_look_up), coop_way_out_p1/p2_up, coop_scene_set's p2_close_up (player 1's scene end touches player 1's words only), hle_coop_controller's g_coop_find_free return (player 1's), coop_player_turn's refusal (player 1's word), the mask loop (given .held), coop_events/coop_send_event (the find's events go to the thing, not a player), the 0x8000749C gate, 0x80144B34, 0x801F53E8 (player 1's busy only). The motion step 0x800276C4's -25932 read is unreachable (r5 = 0x800 always), so neither player's node is affected by either player's find byte; the holder draw calls 0x800271B4/0x8002723C read the game's busy + the holder node's +28's 8, which an own find never sets.

== 4. The design's intercept, checked ==
(a) **Swapping only the block (eye/target/FOV) is not enough** [read]: the goal's direction is out - eye where `out` is the unproject through 0x8060234C/0x8060244C/0x806024BC (player 1's). With the block alone swapped the thing goes to eye2 + normalize(out1 - eye2) * dist: wrong direction, and dist uses len = |out1 - eye2|. Two fixes:
  - Preferred [inf, built on the read]: register a second intercept on **0x801EB15C** (first word 0x9421FFA0: stwu r1,-96) gated on `g_coop_place_p2 && cpu->lr == 0x800D8B84` (the bl at 0x800D8B80), set only round the host's guest call of 0x800D8A88 for player 2's thing. In it: guest-call 0x801EB15C itself with the same r3/r4/r5 (the nested call's lr is GUEST_CALL_SENTINEL 0x0BADF010, main.c 54/81: passes the gate) to get out1 (12 bytes at r4); read player 1's camera from CAMERA_VIEW (eye +0, target +12, up +24, fov +48, as in memory now); compute x1/y1/z1 = out1 - eye1 on player 1's right/up/forward; out2 = eye2 + right2*x1*k + up2*y1*k + forward2*z1 with k = tan(fov2/2)/tan(fov1/2) (the screen offset of (320,165) kept at the same fraction of the frame; 0x800D8A88 then scales dist by its own tan(fov2/2) from the swapped block); write out2 to r4, passthrough false. Then only eye/target/FOV of the block need swapping (0x800D8A88 reads nothing else of it [read]). No GX calls, no matrix writes.
  - Fallback: before the guest call, guest-call 0x801E4BDC(&p2block, 0) with a 52-byte block built from g_coop_own_view (eye 0..2, target 3..5, +24 = 6..8) + player 2's FOV, after saving 0x8060234C..0x8060251C (464 B) and the block; restore both after. Costs: GXSetViewport/Scissor/Projection and GXLoadPosMtxImm(27) issued mid-update (the draw's 0x801E9728 redoes them before any draw [inf]) and notify_look_at firing with lr 0x801E4D5C (camera_split_update etc.): guard it.
(b) Called once per thing per frame: yes, from 0x8014FE24 -> 0x8014F3E8 at 0x800084D0, lr 0x8014F510 [read]; the intercept's gate must test `cpu->lr == 0x8014F510` so its own nested guest call of 0x800D8A88 (lr = sentinel) passes through — the same shape as coop_play_hold's lr range (hle.c 12890). (c) FOV: block +48 in degrees [read]. == 6. Risks left for the builder ==
- The 0x800D8A88 intercept runs the step for player 2's thing with player 1's identity in (as the game does for player 1's): the trailing 0x80118E10/0x80123698/0x8011DFD0 read r13-26524/-21484 [read, ed needs]; today's path for player 1's thing is the same, so leave the identity.
- The +24 registration's dist2 is to player 1's eye: draw ORDER only [read]; if 0x8011A278 culls to player 1's frustum [inf, not read], a thing in front of player 2's camera far from player 1's view could vanish from both halves — watch the first run (step B's risk 12 is the same culling).
- 0x800276C4's player step reads -25932 at 0x80027748 [read 36054-36075] when drawable+28 != 0 and skips a branch for kind-1 nodes while the byte is set: player 2's node steps with the byte 0 in memory (theirs is in their copy only in the tick) [inf]: watch their body during their own find.

## The build's review (2026-11-11, 21 agents: five lenses, two refuters per finding)

Eight findings, five confirmed, one split, two refuted; all fixed in the tree before the commit, the refuted pair hardened:
- (unset, confirmed) `coop_found_for_finder` wrote only the finder's pair, so with the switch unset the other owner's pair kept
  its marks through HEAD's four-frame tail until its own expiry where HEAD dropped it at any find: unset, the other pair is
  dropped at every find as HEAD's one pair was.
- (unset, split) the expiry cleared the draw flag only for the owner whose pair set it, where HEAD cleared it whoever set it
  (a hidden-list match in the same call): unset, cleared as HEAD's.
- (place, confirmed; the tick lens found it too) `coop_found_place` engaged on today's lend route too (a use's or a scene's
  find of player 2's sets owner 1's pair; while the cameras are lent their own view is frozen at the lend): it passes
  through while `g_coop_cameras.lent` (the block is their camera then, as their half is drawn), logged once.
- (place, confirmed) the unproject's matrices are THIS frame's: the main loop runs 0x801EB9A0 (the camera step), then
  0x801E1164 at 0x8000843C, which copies the block and calls 0x801E4BDC to build 0x8060234C/0x8060244C/0x806024BC, then the
  placement pass at 0x800084D0. The design's "built by the previous draw" (its second review listed 0x801E4BDC's callers
  and missed 0x801E1204) was wrong, and the build's previous-frame rule was the drift it meant to cure (RUN 2's x -7..-5
  and z 163..154 against x 0, z 139 on the first call): the decomposition uses the block as the placement found it.

  a cutscene, or busy bits beyond 2, as the casket's sword in logs/run-20261108-131047-fix-casket-p2) leaves

# Step D: the Tome beside player 2's own words, close-up and find (2026-11-11)

## The build record

The user's decision (option A): player 1 opening, using and closing their Tome must not touch player 2's own words,
close-up or find at all, player 2's Tome must not touch player 1's examine, and nothing of either is closed because the
other opened a Tome ("no co-op game would disrupt a function of another player because they checked their inventory").

## What six readers found on HEAD 16b844e (the whole text in the session's scratch, stepd/reports.md)

Most of option A was already true by construction. Player 1's Tome is the game's mode 3, and the host alternates the
frames strictly, world, Tome, world (`tw_mode_change`, TW_WORLD_FRAMES 1). The world's frame 0x800083C8 runs everything
of play: the controller (so `coop_player_turn`, whose gate has no Tome test), the script timers, the event pump, the
stepper 0x80117628, the words tick 0x80024AF0 at lr 0x80008500 (`coop_words_tick` runs there: `game_menu_open()` is
`!tw_world_turn()` under sharing), the placement pass, the draw. The Tome's frame 0x80111234 runs 0x80024AF0 at lr
0x80111488 (the game's own window only; the tick refuses that lr), the Tome's own channels, `0x8014FDB8` on the Tome's
own behaviour list (r13-23456 objects off: the world's drawables are never placed in a Tome frame) and the text pool's
draw. Player 2's pad under player 1's Tome keeps their action bindings, Start stripped, A/B/PICK UP into their copy on
world turns. Player 1's Start is gated by the game alone on player 1's own state (0x80006CDC: r13-25696, the control
channels' +16, event 135, the casting word; the in-play channel is passed over while a words window holds the focus
r13-23552); nothing of player 2's own set is in those words, and the host never refuses player 1's Start. The Tome's open
costs 13-27 frames of Tome-only frames (the book's load, before `tw_tome_start` sets `have_world`): the whole world
stands then, player 2's presses in that window are dropped (the two-Tome work's behaviour, not step D's).

The gaps: player 2's Start opened their copy over their own window (their pad then went to the copy while their window
ticked with no input); a Tome item used by player 2 (`tome_copy_left`) ran its script in the game's words with the old
screen route (g_coop_screen, the cameras lent, player 1's pad taken) when words or busy were up after, and its busy test
read player 1's own close-up as the script's; the deferred close script's consumer tested neither the game's words window
(the mode-2 enter runs ed9's left script with no hold test) nor player 1's Tome (its frames step the one -25960 with
player 1's pad: logs/run-20261106-044758); a state load didn't reset `g_coop_p2_words`; a save with their window up
wasn't held.

## Refuted before the build (five refuters on the design, stepd/refute.md)

- D1, clearing the Tome's prompt bit 0x10 of r13-24048 in the world's frames for player 2's own prompts: unnecessary and
  harmful. Player 2's own prompts are chosen and drawn by the host (`coop_prompt_look`'s tail, `coop_prompt_show`), never
  by the game's chooser 0x80117628, whose one caller is the world frame with the GAME's slots in; with the bit cleared the
  chooser would make player 1's zone prompt ("B EXAMINE") and the Tome's frame would draw it over the page. The bit stays
  (the existing clear is the lend route's); logs/run-20261111-061558-stepD-pre-R1 and its sheet-3900-4340.jpg (today's
  code) already show player 2's PICK UP prompt under player 1's Tome.
- D7, the placement intercepts passing through on a Tome frame: dead code. 0x8014EE5C picks the behaviour list by
  r13-23456 (1: the Tome's r13-23432), so the found thing's placement never runs in a Tome frame.
- D3 as first written would have frozen player 1: `coop_states_swap` swaps only the script table; the words' open
  registers its channel (0x8013DB3C, the table at 0x80588150 inside COOP_INPUT) and focus (0x8013DB1C, r13-23556) in
  whatever INPUT copy is in, and `tome_copy_left` never put player 2's in. Built with `coop_state(&g_coop_state[1])` in
  round the run, as `coop_words_tick` does.
- D4 as first written waited on the game's words only; under player 1's Tome the one -25960 is stepped by the Tome's
  frame with player 1's pad, so a script of theirs that opens words behind the Tome would have taken player 1's B (the
  2026-11-06 fault). Built to wait for the Tome too.
- The cast's ask (hle.c 12324) needs no refusal: 0x80006CDC refuses the quick cast's Tome ask on their held channels in
  their own copy. The copy's open needed its own guard for the frame where their Start is read before the look that
  opens their window.

## What was built (behind the A-C switches; unset, nothing changes)

- `coop_p2_own_up()`: their own words, close-up or find is up. Their Start is refused with it (both ask sites in
  `coop_menu_input`, one log per press) and `tome_copy_step` refuses the copy's open with it (the same-frame case), as
  the game refuses the player's Start while words or a close-up hold play.
- `tome_copy_left`: their input copy (`coop_state(&g_coop_state[1])`) and their words set (`coop_words_own_io`) in round
  the item script's two runs, with `g_coop_p2_left_run` taken as a call of theirs by `coop_words_hold`'s own branch and
  `hle_words_layout`'s +4096 only (`coop_p2_words_open_call()`; a scene's hold, a find's hold and `hle_coop_found` keep
  today's route). Words the script opened are recorded as their own (`.up/.channel/.script`, closed by their B in their
  tick) with no screen route and the cameras given back; the old route stays for a close-up or scene the script set, told
  by the busy word CHANGING over the run (`busy_was`), not by player 1's own busy.
- The deferred script's consumer waits while the game's words window is up or player 1's Tome is open (one log per
  wait; g_coop_close.script and g_coop_use_script wait with it).
- A state load zeroes `g_coop_p2_words`; `hle_save_held` holds a save while their own window is up (quickstate.c says
  so once); `coop_player_held` counts their find's hold; two WARNING logs at the mode switch (their set in memory there,
  or the old words split on beside their set), never expected.

  turn in player 2's screen: busy 2 ... screen 1 ... lent 1' and '[trace] busy r3 2 r4 0 lr 0x80199F28 who 1' (the use's end
  clears hold bit 2 of the game's one busy word, which is player 1's close-up's hold too), 'player 1's prompt 7 in player 2's
  close-up', and after player 1's RETURN at 4950 ('look stage 2 script 90 count 1') nothing more of player 1: on HEAD the
  game ran on to frame 575908 with 'lent 1' in every gate line (the run's frame limit didn't close a variant exe:
  tools/ed/.run_exe_tmp.sh now kills the RUN_EXE's own name). So the fault is step C's and older, not step D's: the use
  route (`coop_use_start`'s end, `busy_new = coop_busy(cpu)` for a use that isn't a close one) takes player 1's close-up's
  busy for the use's own and goes the screen route, and the game's one busy word has one bit 2 for both players' holds. The
  whole-game fix is the busy word per player (the 'Half' row of docs/coop-todo.md's table), not a step D guard. Step D's two
  changes on the way (`busy_was`, the plain branch's `g_coop_assist_at = 0`) stay: they take `tome_copy_left`'s own screen
  route out of this case (build 3's 'cameras lent 0' at 4702), and docs/coop-failed-attempts.md has them as attempts at r5b.

## The build's review (2026-11-11 morning, three refuters: the swaps, the gates, the Tome frames)

The user agreed to a lighter pass than step C's 21 agents. Three findings held, all fixed in build 4:

- **(gates) The unset route was no longer HEAD's.** `tome_copy_left`'s screen-route test read the busy word CHANGING over the
  run (`busy_was`) whether or not the own run was on, so with the three switches unset a Tome item of player 2's beside player
  1's close-up took the plain branch where HEAD took the screen route. A fix of the logged fault, but ungated, and r0 never
  reached the Tome item. Now `own_run ? busy != busy_was : coop_busy(cpu)`: the unset route is HEAD's again, fault and all.
- **(gates) Player 2 unlisted with their own window up.** An area change (`coop_pad_read`'s 'player 2 is gone') zeroes
  `g_coop_p2` and nothing then clears their own set: the tick and the cancel return at the same gate, the spawn and the adopt
  set `g_coop_p2` again without touching it. With step D that meant `hle_save_held` true for good (every save waiting) and
  their Start refused for good once they were back; from step A on, `tome_copy_p1_left` was blocked by the same `.up`. Now
  one `coop_p2_own_clear()` (the three sets and `g_coop_p2_left_run`), called at the 'player 2 is gone' site (logged when a
  window was up) and by `hle_coop_state_load` in place of its four lines. Not scripted (an area exit with their window up).
- **(gates) Their cast's Tome ask dropped without a back-out.** `coop_quick_cast` runs in their turn, which their own hold
  never refuses, so a D-pad spell needing an item asked for their Tome (state 64, `g_tome_copy_spell_set`), and step D's
  refusal in `tome_copy_step` cleared the ask and returned: neither the cast nor `tome_copy_left`'s back-out ran, and player
  2 stayed in state 64, where every later cast is refused. Now no cast begins under their own words, close-up or find
  (`coop_quick_cast`, as the game's controller begins none under a hold; logged with ED_COOP_LOG), and the refusal backs an
  asked cast out (`tome_copy_cast_back_out`, the branch `tome_copy_left` had, shared). Not scripted (chapter 1's room 183
  has no spell for player 2 to cast; the user's play of a D-pad cast during their own text is the check).

Pre-existing and left: `tome_copy_step`'s not-world_stays branch (a cutscene or a mode change as their cast asks) clears the
ask with `tome_copy_left(cpu, 0, ...)` and `g_tome_copy_spell_open` false, so that cast isn't backed out either; in
docs/coop-todo.md's open issues.

Refuted by the same pass (not real): the two COOP_RUN_SCRIPT calls leave no swap in on any path (the room-change case skips
only `.up`, and no Tome item both opens words and asks for a room); the deferred script's wait ends with the game's words or
the Tome, which player 1 closes, and the waited script never is the one that closes them; the Tome's frame can't see their
set in memory (the three flags are set and cleared inside world-frame calls, so the WARNING logs stay silent); the memset at
a state load leaves no registered channel (the load replaces the input table too).

The recheck of build 4: `saves/jtg-ch1-room183.state` remade on it from Jump to Game (logs/run-20261111-094915-jtg-stepD4-state, the door chain 101, 105, 111, 29 twice each; loads at 3650), then the whole chain (logs/run-20261111-0950*..0954*-stepD-r0..r7): r0 today's lend route line for line with no step D line; r1, r2, r3, r4b, r6 as in the build record; r4 the Start at 3930 refused, then 'player 2's Tome opens in the copy' after their RETURN; r5 'ran as theirs: words 0, busy 0, cameras lent 0', player 1 walking; r7 the save held once and written (35.6 MB) after their PICK UP; r5b failing as at HEAD (the busy word). New in every run with a D-pad page turn under their words: 'player 2's quick cast refused: their own words, close-up or find is up' (the page still turns in their tick; the cast is the game's refusal under a hold). The D-pad cast with a spell and the area change under their window stay for the user's play.
